Abstract: We present a perturbative closed-time-path (in-in) formulation of an optomechanical system in which a quantum field interacts with a moving mirror via radiation pressure. We derive the effective action governing the dynamics of the moving mirror, incorporating the full back-reaction of the cavity field. These effects are encoded in fluctuation and dissipation kernels, that we show satisfy fluctuation-dissipation relations, and whose spectral structure reveals a direct connection with the underlying physical mechanism responsible for the back-reaction, that is particle creation by the dynamical Casimir effect. By deriving the semiclassical equations of motion for the moving mirror, and computing the energy radiated into the field within the in-out formalism of quantum field theory, we verify the energy balance between the mechanical energy dissipated by the optical back-reaction forces acting on the mirror and the energy carried by the particles created in the field.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出子空间蒙特卡洛方法：在每个逻辑量子操作后测量每个交换量子比特的总自旋z分量，从而解相干不同自旋投影量子数状态的相干混合。每个量子比特具有确定的自旋投影量子数，n个量子比特的密度矩阵可用维度3^{2n}的向量表示，外加维度n的向量标记每个量子比特的量子数，而不是传统的8^{2n}维度。

Result: 当模拟电路通过随机化编译使噪声旋转，将相干误差转换为随机误差时，该动力学近似方法仍能忠实于真实动力学。使用该方法研究了带有重置泄漏门电路的测量结果相关性如何受CNOT实现选择的影响，特别是使用6个交换量子比特的多轮贝尔态稳定电路。

Conclusion: 子空间蒙特卡洛方法通过解相干不同自旋投影量子数状态的相干混合，显著降低了多交换量子比特开放系统动力学的模拟复杂度，在噪声旋转条件下仍能保持准确性，为研究量子电路中的相关性效应提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a Monte Carlo based method for simulating the open system dynamics of multiple exchange-only (EO) qubits. In the EO encoding, the total spin projection quantum number along the $z$-axis of the three constituent spins remains unchanged under exchange operations, in contrast to the open system (or multi-qubit miscalibration) setting where coherent and incoherent mixing of states with different quantum numbers occurs. In our approach, we choose to measure the total spin component along the $z$-axis of each EO qubit after every logical quantum operation, which decoheres coherent mixtures of states with different spin projection quantum numbers. Independent simulations thus give different trajectories of the system in the associated subspaces, so we refer to this method as the Subspace Monte Carlo method. With each EO qubit having a definite spin projection quantum number, the density matrix of $n$ qubits can be represented by a vector of dimension $3^{2n}$, instead of $8^{2n}$, with an additional vector of dimension $n$ to label the quantum number of each qubit. We show that this approximation of the dynamics remains faithful to the true dynamics when the simulated circuits twirl the noise, converting coherent errors to stochastic errors, which can be achieved using randomized compiling. We use this simulation approach to study how correlations in measurement outcomes of circuits with reset-if-leaked gadgets, such as a multi-round Bell state stabilization circuit that uses 6 EO qubits, are affected by the choice of CNOT implementations.

</details>


### [77] [Generalized Inverses of Quantum Channels: a categorical perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13946)
*Robin Cockett,Jean-Simon Pacaud Lemay,Priyaa Varshinee Srinivasan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从范畴论角度研究量子通道的广义逆，证明了Drazin逆总是保迹的，对于幺正量子通道，Drazin逆也是幺正的，并推广到dagger Drazin逆，从而证明对于幺正量子通道，Moore-Penrose逆既是保迹的也是幺正的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通道的广义逆（如Moore-Penrose逆和Drazin逆）在量子误差缓解中具有重要作用，但广义逆本身通常不是量子通道（往往不满足完全正性）。特别地，由于广义逆保持保迹性质在应用中很重要，而Drazin逆是保迹的，Moore-Penrose逆通常不保迹，因此需要从理论层面深入理解这些广义逆的性质。

Method: 采用范畴论视角研究量子通道的广义逆。利用范畴论框架给出了Drazin逆总是保迹的简单证明，并证明对于幺正量子通道，Drazin逆也是幺正的。进一步推广到dagger Drazin逆，从而证明对于幺正量子通道，Moore-Penrose逆既是保迹的也是幺正的。

Result: 1. Drazin逆总是保迹的；2. 对于幺正量子通道，Drazin逆也是幺正的；3. 对于幺正量子通道，Moore-Penrose逆既是保迹的也是幺正的。这些结果为量子信息理论，特别是量子误差缓解中应用Drazin逆和Moore-Penrose逆开辟了新途径。

Conclusion: 范畴论视角为理解量子通道广义逆的性质提供了有力框架。证明了Drazin逆的保迹性和幺正性，以及幺正量子通道下Moore-Penrose逆的保迹性和幺正性，这些理论结果将促进广义逆在量子误差缓解等量子信息应用中的新应用。

Abstract: A quantum channel is defined as being completely positive (CP) and trace preserving (TP). While not every quantum channel is invertible or reversible, every quantum channel admits various kinds of generalized inverses such as the Moore-Penrose inverse and the Drazin inverse. A generalized inverse of a quantum channel may not itself be a quantum channel: it often fails to be CP. However, generalized inverses still play an important role in quantum error mitigation. Here, because it is often desirable for the generalized inverse of a quantum channel to be at least TP, the Drazin inverse, which is TP, is favoured over the Moore-Penrose inverse, which is not in general TP.
  In this paper, we take a categorical perspective on generalized inverses of quantum channels. This allows us to give a simple proof of the fact that the Drazin inverse of a quantum channel is always TP. It also allows us to show that for unital quantum channels, the Drazin inverse is also unital. We then generalize this result to dagger Drazin inverses, which allows us to show that for unital quantum channels, the Moore-Penrose inverse is both TP and unital as well. This opens the door to new applications of both the Drazin inverse and Moore-Penrose inverse in quantum information theory and, in particular, in quantum error mitigation.

</details>

Abstract: We propose collective nuclear polaritons formed by hybridizing a 229Th nuclear ensemble with a vacuum-ultraviolet cavity mode generated via four-wave mixing, achieving a collective light-matter coupling that scales as $\sqrt{N}$. In the strong-coupling regime the system displays vacuum Rabi oscillations, indicating the hybridization between cavity photons and nuclear excitations. In the superradiant regime, the stored excitation is released in a cooperative burst with peak intensity scaling as $N^2$. The emission lifetime shrinks from thousands of seconds to the millisecond scale and remains tunable. Detuning sweeps across the polariton avoided crossing allow adiabatic conversion of the photonic excitation into a collective nuclear excitation, enabling reversible quantum storage. Our results demonstrate that cavity-mediated nuclear polaritons enable deterministic lifetime engineering and coherent quantum storage in nuclear systems.

</details>


### [79] [Folding-Free Zero-Noise Extrapolation by Layout-induced Noise Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13949)
*Debarthi Pal,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FF-ZNE是一种无需电路折叠的零噪声外推方法，通过利用异构硬件布局的不同原生噪声特性实现可控噪声变化，消除了噪声因子选择和外推器选择的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子处理器在噪声主导环境下运行，需要误差缓解技术。传统ZNE方法存在偏差且缺乏严格误差界限，其应用需要复杂的噪声因子选择和外推模型选择，对用户专业知识要求高且常需昂贵的试错过程。

Method: FF-ZNE通过利用具有不同原生噪声特性的异构硬件布局，在不进行电路折叠的情况下实现有效噪声放大。在去极化噪声模型下，该方法产生固定的线性外推形式。论文还提出了两种算法来识别能使给定电路产生足够不同期望值的异构硬件布局集合。

Result: 在133量子比特IBM Quantum设备上的实验表明，FF-ZNE对于最多50量子比特的EfficientSU2（稀疏）和哈密顿量模拟（密集）电路，平均偏差分别为~6%和4.5%。

Conclusion: FF-ZNE通过消除噪声因子和外推器选择，将ZNE从需要专家调优的技术转变为实用、可扩展且广泛可用的误差缓解方法，适用于当前量子硬件。

Abstract: Near term quantum processors operate in a noise dominated regime, motivating error mitigation techniques that recover accurate expectation values without full fault tolerance. Zero Noise Extrapolation (ZNE) is a widely used but biased error mitigation method that lacks rigorous error bounds. Its effective application requires nontrivial technical choices, most notably the selection of noise scaling factors and extrapolation models, making ZNE sensitive to user expertise and often necessitating costly trial and error procedures. Here, we introduce Folding Free Zero Noise Extrapolation (FF-ZNE), a method that removes the need for noise factor selection by achieving effective noise amplification without circuit folding. FF-ZNE exploits isomorphic hardware layouts with distinct native noise profiles, such that executing a fixed circuit across these layouts induces controllable variations in the effective noise strength. Under a depolarizing noise model, we analytically show that the resulting extrapolation admits a fixed linear form, eliminating extrapolator choice and enabling a seamless, user independent mitigation procedure. We further propose two algorithms that identify sets of isomorphic hardware layouts on which a given circuit yields sufficiently distinct expectation values to enable reliable zero noise extrapolation. Experiments on a 133 qubit IBM Quantum device demonstrate that FF-ZNE yields mitigated expectation values with average deviations of ~6% and 4.5% for up to 50 qubit EfficientSU2 (sparse) and Hamiltonian simulation (dense) circuits, respectively. The method is thus scalable and applicable to a broad range of circuits. By eliminating noise factor and extrapolator selection, FF-ZNE transforms zero noise extrapolation from a technique requiring expert tuning into a practical, scalable, and broadly accessible error mitigation method for current quantum hardware.

</details>


### [80] [Evidence of Uncollapsed Quantum Amplitudes After Consecutive Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13974)
*Christoph Adami,Lambert Giner,Jeff S. Lundeen,Raphael A. Abrahao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过单光子测量实验证明，在三个或更多连续量子测量中，幺正理论与坍缩理论会做出不同预测，实验结果支持幺正理论


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量理论中存在两种主要解释：标准坍缩理论（哥本哈根解释）认为测量后未实现的振幅会消失；而幺正理论认为测量设备成为纠缠态的一部分，未实现的振幅继续存在。这两种理论之前没有实验可区分的预测，本研究旨在通过实验验证这两种理论在连续测量中的不同预测

Method: 使用单光子测量可调量子态，进行三个或更多连续测量，分析三个测量的联合密度矩阵，观察其中的相干性。通过显式引入退相干，比较两种理论预测的差异

Result: 实验结果显示三个测量的联合密度矩阵中存在相干性，支持幺正理论。当显式引入退相干时，量子系统和测量设备的联合密度矩阵变得与坍缩理论的预测一致

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了连续量子测量的动力学，为量子测量解释提供了新见解，实验证据支持幺正理论对量子测量的描述

Abstract: Two of the most common interpretations of quantum measurement disagree about the fate of quantum amplitudes after measurement, yet this disagreement has not previously led to experimentally distinguishable predictions. In the standard collapse picture, commonly linked to the Copenhagen interpretation of quantum mechanics, measurements eliminate unrealized amplitudes without leaving a memory. In contrast, in the unitary theory, the measurement device registers one of the possible outcomes while remaining part of an entangled state that continues to harbor the unrealized amplitudes. This persistence arises naturally under unitary evolution, since a measurement device that is part of an entangled system cannot serve as a faithful probe of the joint quantum state. Using single-photon measurements of a tunable quantum state, we experimentally show that these two theories make different predictions when three or more consecutive measurements are performed on the same quantum system. Analysis of the joint density matrix of the three measurements reveals coherence among them and supports the unitary theory of quantum measurement. When decoherence is explicitly introduced, the joint density matrix of the quantum system of interest and the apparatus becomes consistent with what a collapse theory would predict. This work clarifies the dynamics of consecutive quantum measurements and offers new insights into the interpretation of quantum measurements.

</details>


### [81] [Block-QAOA-Aware Detection with Parameter Transfer for Large-Scale MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14042)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出BQA-MD检测器，通过块状QAOA感知预处理、动态正则化块状目标函数和K-best候选传播，将大规模MIMO检测分解为适合有限量子比特资源的局部子问题，实现量子-经典混合检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO检测面临挑战：精确或近最大似然搜索难以扩展，而现有量子资源不足以通过QAOA直接解决完整检测问题。需要设计兼容有限量子比特资源的检测方案。

Result: 在16×16 Rayleigh MIMO系统（16QAM）的经典模拟实验中：1) 正则化块状检测器优于非正则化版本，验证了块状目标函数和块状QAOA感知设计的合理性；2) 参数迁移QAOA检测器接近正则化块状穷举参考，明显优于直接训练QAOA的BER性能；3) 在低SNR区域MMSE略优，从中等SNR开始参数迁移QAOA检测器具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: BQA-MD框架成功将大规模MIMO检测分解为适合有限量子资源的局部子问题。参数迁移QAOA是推荐的实现策略，在中等至高SNR区域表现出色。该工作为量子-经典混合MIMO检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale MIMO detection remains challenging because exact or near-maximum-likelihood search is difficult to scale, while available quantum resources are insufficient for directly solving full-size detection instances by QAOA. This paper therefore proposes a Block-QAOA-Aware MIMO Detector (BQA-MD), whose primary purpose is to reorganize the detection chain so that it becomes compatible with limited-qubit local quantum subproblems. Specifically, BQA-MD combines block-QAOA-aware preprocessing in the QR domain, a standards-consistent blockwise 5G NR Gray-HUBO interface, an MMSE-induced dynamic regularized blockwise objective, and K-best candidate propagation. Within this framework, fixed-size block construction gives every local subproblem a uniform circuit width and parameter dimension, which in turn enables parameter-transfer QAOA as a practical realization strategy for structurally matched local subproblems. Experiments are conducted on a 16x16 Rayleigh MIMO system with 16QAM using classical simulation of the quantum subroutine. The results show that the regularized blockwise detector improves upon its unregularized counterpart, validating the adopted blockwise objective and the block-QAOA-aware design rationale. They also show that the parameter-transfer QAOA detector nearly matches the regularized blockwise exhaustive reference and clearly outperforms direct-training QAOA in BER, thereby supporting parameter reuse as the preferred QAOA realization strategy within the proposed framework. In the tested setting, MMSE remains slightly better in the low-SNR region, whereas the parameter-transfer QAOA detector becomes highly competitive from the medium-SNR regime onward.

</details>


### [82] [Adaptive quantum metrology with large dynamic range using short one-axis twists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14095)
*Tyler G. Thurtell,Akimasa Miyake*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于单轴扭曲操作和旋转交替应用的相位估计方案，适用于具有大动态范围的相位估计，通过数值计算展示了该方案能达到接近海森堡极限的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有大动态范围的相位估计（如原子钟应用），需要开发能够接近海森堡极限的实用方案。先前提出的方案使用高斯压缩态作为探针，但需要探索非高斯特征态的应用潜力。

Method: 提出基于单轴扭曲操作和旋转交替应用的实验可行相位估计方案。协议使用多个OAT角度，其持续时间随系统尺寸多项式减小，自旋压缩参数以N^{-μ}衰减（μ>2/3）。通过数值计算贝叶斯均方误差的系统尺寸依赖性N^{-ν}来验证方案有效性。

Result: 数值计算表明这些态适用于相位估计方案，在最后适应阶段使用两个和三个OAT操作分别能达到ν=17/9和53/27的精度。分析了所得探针态的非高斯性有限性，并讨论了非高斯性在协议中的作用。

Conclusion: 提出的基于OAT操作的相位估计方案在实验上可行，能够达到接近海森堡极限的精度。分析了协议对粒子数波动和相干控制波动等缺陷的鲁棒性，证明了该方案在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Phase estimation with potentially large phase values, i.e., with large dynamic range, has many applications in quantum metrology, for example to atomic clocks. A recently proposed phase estimation scheme approaches the Heisenberg scaling in this global setting using sequences of increasingly squeezed Gaussian states as probes and adaptively chosen, potentially mid-circuit, measurements. In this work, we first observe that the pattern of increase in the squeezing of the probes is applicable even to states with some non-Gaussian features. We then propose an experimentally feasible version of this phase estimation scheme, based on the alternating application of one-axis twist (OAT) operations and rotations. Our protocols are explicitly described in terms of multiple OAT angles whose durations decrease polynomially with system size and spin-squeezing parameters that decay as $N^{-μ}$, with $μ>2/3$ in most cases. Using numerical computation of the system-size dependence $N^{-ν}$ of the Bayesian mean-squared error of an estimator, we show that these states are suitable for use in the phase estimation scheme, and highlight the protocols to achieve $ν=17/9$ and $53/27$ using two and three OAT operations respectively in the last adaptation stage. We also analyze the limited non-Gaussianity of the resulting probe states and discuss the role of non-Gaussianity in this protocol more generally. Finally, we analyze how robust these protocols are with respect to imperfections such as particle number fluctuations and coherent control fluctuations.

</details>


### [83] [Analysis of Hydrogen Contamination in Al/AlOx/Al Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14113)
*Yu Zhu,Aldilene Saraiva-Souza,Félix Beaudoin,Hong Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子动力学模拟和量子输运计算，揭示了氢污染对约瑟夫森结器件性能的影响机制，预测了氢含量导致的器件参数变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢污染是超导量子比特中约瑟夫森结器件间变异性和双能级系统损耗的潜在来源，需要从原子尺度理解其影响机制。

Method: 结合CHGNet分子动力学模拟氧化铝生长过程，以及NEGF-DFT量子输运计算（使用NanoDCAL软件和GGA+U方案），分析400个统计独立样本中氢的分布及其对电子输运的影响。

Result: 氢在氧化物中的数量服从β-二项分布，主要形成Al-OH和Al-OH-Al结构；氢原子增加费米能级附近的透射系数，表现为有效的p型掺杂；对于平均氢含量2.56%的约瑟夫森结，预测约瑟夫森能量为EJ/h=10.92±0.26 GHz。

Conclusion: 研究提供了氢污染的原子尺度图像，并量化了约瑟夫森结器件参数的变异性，为超导量子器件的优化设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Hydrogen contamination in Josephson junctions is a potential source of device-to-device variability and two-level-system loss in superconducting qubits. In this work, we investigate hydrogen incorporation in oxidized aluminum barriers by combining molecular dynamics simulations with atomistic quantum transport calculations. The oxide growth simulations are performed using CHGNet for Al surfaces exposed to dense O$_{\text{2}}$ and H$_{\text{2}% }$O environments, yielding amorphous AlO$_{\text{x}}$ layers with hydrogen content comparable to experimentally relevant levels. From $400$ statistically independent samples, we find that the number of H atoms in the oxide is well described by a beta-binomial distribution, reflecting correlations induced by the self-limiting oxidation process. Structural analysis shows that most hydrogen atoms reside near the AlO$_{\text{x}}$ surface and predominantly form Al-OH and Al-OH-Al motifs. To assess the impact of hydrogen on transport, we construct Al/Al$_{\text{2}}$O$_{\text{3}} $/Al junction models and perform NEGF-DFT calculations with NanoDCAL, using a GGA+U scheme to calibrate the band gap and band alignment. H atoms are found to increase the transmission coefficient near the Fermi level and shift the electronic structure in a manner consistent with effective p-type doping. By combining the H atom number statistics from molecular dynamics with the transmission coefficients from quantum transport calculations, we obtain a probability distribution for the Josephson energy. For a Josephson junction with an average hydrogen content of $2.56$ at.\%, the resulting Josephson energy is predicted to be $% E_{J}/h=10.92\pm 0.26$ GHz. These results provide an atomistic picture of hydrogen contamination and an estimate of device variability in Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [84] [Practical Limits to Single-Mode Vacuum Squeezing in a SNAIL Parametric Amplifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14123)
*Theodore Shaw,Debsuvra Mukhopadhyay,Zhuoqun Hao,Josiah Cochran,Haley Cole,Archana Kamal,Shyam Shankar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SNAIL参量放大器在实际应用中产生的单模真空压缩特性分析，发现克尔非线性对压缩性能影响不大，主要限制因素是内部谐振器损耗和微波链插入损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前预期克尔非线性会限制简并参量放大器中的压缩性能，因此研究在实际传感和量子比特读取实验中，SNAIL参量放大器产生的单模真空压缩特性。

Method: 通过改变外部磁通和泵浦功率，探索克尔非线性理论上最小化的操作点，测量SNAIL参量放大器在实际应用条件下的压缩性能。

Result: 在实际应用中，克尔非线性变化约2倍，但可实现的压缩性能与克尔非线性无显著依赖关系。理论模型支持这一观察，表明最先进SPA中的基线克尔值已经太小，不足以构成实际限制。

Conclusion: 在实际SPA中，改进压缩性能的主要途径是减少损耗（内部谐振器损耗和微波链插入损耗），而不是抑制克尔非线性。

Abstract: We characterize single-mode vacuum squeezing generated by a SNAIL Parametric Amplifier (SPA) operated under conditions representative of practical sensing and qubit-readout experiments. Motivated by prior expectations that Kerr-induced distortion limits squeezing in degenerate parametric amplifiers, we varied external flux and pump power to explore operating points where Kerr nonlinearity is theoretically minimized. We find that for practical applications where the squeezing frequency is fixed, the Kerr was variable by about a factor of two and the achievable squeezing showed no significant dependence on Kerr. Theoretical modeling supports this observation and indicates that baseline Kerr values in state-of-the-art SPAs are already too small to impose a practical limitation. Instead, squeezing was dominated by internal resonator loss and insertion loss in the microwave chain. These results indicate that, in practical SPAs, reducing loss, rather than suppressing Kerr, is the primary route to improved squeezing performance.

</details>


### [85] [Frequency-resolved N-photon correlations in the ultra-strong coupling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14201)
*Wen Huang,Qian Bin,Ying Wu,Xin-You Lü*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探索了超强耦合腔QED系统中频率分辨的N光子关联，揭示了奇偶对称性在塑造光子统计特性中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率分辨的光子发射对于量子信息编码和高分辨率光谱学至关重要，研究其关联可以揭示基本的发射路径和多光子统计特性。然而，在超强耦合腔QED系统中，频率分辨的多光子关联尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 研究超强耦合腔QED系统，其中量子比特与单模腔相互作用。利用反旋转相互作用强烈修改本征态和能谱，通过频率选择性探测分析发射的频率分辨光子的光谱和统计特性。

Result: 揭示了显著的多光子反聚束和源自于修饰本征态级联跃迁的多光子聚束。特别发现奇偶对称性在塑造这些关联中起决定性作用：对称性破缺打开了额外的跃迁通道，显著增强了不同频率的相关光子对甚至光子三重态的生成。

Conclusion: 该工作将频率分辨关联扩展到超强耦合区域，并展示了其作为光-物质相互作用系统中对称性敏感探针的潜力。

Abstract: Frequency-resolved photon emission is central to applications from quantum information encoding to high-resolution spectroscopy, and then studying their correlations is therefore essential for revealing the underlying emission pathways and multiphoton statistics. Here, we investigate frequency-resolved N-photon correlations in an ultrastrongly coupled cavity QED system where a qubit interacts with a single-mode cavity. Owing to counter-rotating interactions, the eigenstates and energy spectrum are strongly modified, giving rise to rich spectral and statistical properties in the emitted frequency-resolved photons. Through frequency-selective detection, we reveal pronounced multiphoton antibunching, as well as multiphoton bunching originating from cascade transitions among dressed eigenstates. In particular, we show that parity symmetry plays a decisive role in shaping these correlations. The symmetry-breaking opens additional transition channels and dramatically enhances the generation of correlated photon pairs and even photon triplets of different frequencies. Our work extends frequency-resolved correlations to the ultra-strong coupling regime and demonstrates their potential as a sensitive probe of symmetry in light-matter interaction systems.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-qubit controlled gate with optimal T-count](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14202)
*Soichiro Yamazaki,Seiseki Akibue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文改进了多量子比特受控SU(2)门的T门计数上界，达到3log₂(1/ε)+o(log(1/ε))，并证明在禁止使用近似受控门时该下界是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控门是量子算法的关键组件，改进其合成效率对量子计算资源优化至关重要。先前Gosset等人的工作存在改进空间，需要更优的T门计数上界。

Method: 改进了Gosset等人的方法，通过更高效的合成方案减少T门使用量，并分析了在禁止近似受控门条件下的理论下界。

Result: 证明了对于允许误差ε，3log₂(1/ε)+o(log(1/ε))个T门足以近似大多数多量子比特受控SU(2)门，且该T计数在禁止近似受控门时达到下界。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了多量子比特受控门的T门计数上界，为受控门合成和SU(4)门合成提供了高效方案，对量子算法资源优化有重要价值。

Abstract: Controlled gates are key components in various quantum algorithms. Improving on the prior work of Gosset et al., we show that, for an allowed error $\varepsilon$, $3\log_2(1/\varepsilon) + o(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ $T$ gates are sufficient to approximate most multi-qubit controlled SU(2)s. We also show that this T-count matches the lower bound when the use of an almost controlled gate is prohibited. As an application, general controlled gate synthesis and efficient SU(4) gate synthesis are given.

</details>


### [87] [Phase-preserving control of Floquet-engineered cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14279)
*J. W. Yu,X. Q. Zhou,Z. B. Ni,X. T. Cheng,Y. Zhao,H. H. Zhu,C. H. Li,F. Liu,C. Y. Jin*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究驱动量子系统中光-物质相互作用的相干控制，探索Floquet工程作为量子信息处理工具的潜力，解决时间调制腔中相位保持和退相干问题。

Method: 采用Floquet工程框架，在时间调制腔中控制两能级系统的光-物质相互作用，引入声子库分析非马尔可夫过程的影响，严格演示相位保持的TLS-腔相互作用操作。

Result: 实现了严格相位保持的TLS-腔相互作用操作，能够中断和恢复相干Rabi振荡而不损失量子信息，证明非马尔可夫过程引起的频率不稳定性在Floquet调制期间不会产生显著相位退相干。

Conclusion: Floquet工程为相干量子信息处理提供了强大工具，为驱动量子系统的基础物理提供了新见解，展示了在时间调制环境中保持量子相干性的可行性。

Abstract: We propose a Floquet-engineered framework for the coherent control of the light-matter interaction in a two-level system (TLS) located in a time-modulated cavity. Strictly phase-preserving operation of the TLS-cavity interaction is demonstrated, allowing the interrupt and retrieval of coherent Rabi oscillations without the loss of quantum information. By introducing a phonon reservoir, it is proved that the frequency instability induced from non-Markovian processes does not produce significant phase decoherence during Floquet modulation. Our results provide new insights into the fundamental physics of a driven quantum system and establish Floquet engineering as a powerful tool for coherent quantum information processing.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum-limited traveling-wave parametric amplifier based on DUV lithography-defined planar structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14455)
*Hao Li,Marco Scigliuzzo,Evgenii Guzovskii,Seog-Tae Han,Kyungho Han,Tobias J. Kippenberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一种采用深紫外光刻技术制造的紧凑型四波混频行波参量放大器，实现了宽带增益和接近量子极限的低噪声性能，为大规模量子系统的可扩展制造提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微电子学的持续发展得益于深紫外光刻技术的精确性和可重复性。实现大规模超导量子处理器需要遵循类似可扩展制造路径的低温微波组件，特别是对于高电路密度器件如行波参量放大器。现有实现方案在增益、带宽、饱和功率和噪声方面表现良好，但在占地面积、插入损耗和可扩展集成之间存在权衡。

Method: 采用混合制造方案：结合深紫外光刻定义的平面电路元件和电子束图案化的约瑟夫森结。使用平面电容器来减少损耗，实现四波混频行波参量放大器的制造。

Result: 器件实现了紧凑的占地面积和3-11 GHz的宽带增益，平均1 dB压缩点为-102 dBm。噪声性能接近量子极限：在标准量子极限之上添加了0-1.5个光子噪声，在4-8 GHz频段平均为0.4个光子。相位匹配阻带保持窄带宽（43 MHz），谐振频率变化低于1%，表明深紫外光刻技术实现了良好的均匀性。

Conclusion: 深紫外光刻定义的平面元件能够实现紧凑、低损耗、接近量子极限的行波参量放大器，为大规模量子系统的高密度低温微波硬件提供了一条有前景的可扩展制造路径。

Abstract: The relentless scaling of classical microelectronics has been enabled by the precision and reproducibility of deep-ultraviolet (DUV) optical lithography. Implementing large-scale superconducting quantum processors will require cryogenic microwave components that follow a similarly scalable fabrication path. This need is particularly acute for high circuit-density devices such as traveling-wave parametric amplifiers (TWPAs), where recent implementations have demonstrated high gain, broad bandwidth, high saturation power, and near-quantum-limited noise, but trade-offs between footprint, insertion loss, and scalable integration remain. Here, we demonstrate a four-wave-mixing TWPA fabricated via a hybrid scheme that combines DUV-defined planar circuit elements with electron-beam-patterned Josephson junctions, constituting a first step toward fully scalable manufacturing. The device combines a compact footprint with broadband gain from 3 to 11 GHz and an average 1 dB compression point of -102 dBm. By using planar capacitors to reduce loss, it operates near the quantum limit, with added noise near 0 and 1.5 photons above the standard quantum limit and an average of 0.4 photons in the 4 to 8 GHz band. The phase-matching stopband remains narrow, with a bandwidth of 43 MHz, consistent with resonator-frequency variation below 1% and indicative of the uniformity enabled by DUV lithography. These results show that DUV-defined planar elements can enable compact, low-loss, near-quantum-limited TWPAs and provide a promising route toward high-density cryogenic microwave hardware for large-scale quantum systems.

</details>


### [89] [The Twin-World road to reality in quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14464)
*Daniel Braun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双世界随机演化的量子力学新解释，通过扩展grabit形式体系到两个孪生世界，在交集处重现标准量子力学


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子力学提供一个现实的随机解释，避免传统量子力学的概念困难，如测量问题和波函数坍缩，同时保持与实验观测的一致性

Method: 扩展grabit形式体系到两个具有相同随机演化规律的孪生世界，将我们的世界限制在两个世界的交集处，通过交集处的巧合事件自动后选择来重现量子现象

Result: 成功推导出每个孪生世界中的随机演化方程，完全重现任意粒子数和相互作用的薛定谔方程，正确再现隧穿等典型量子效应，包括玻恩规则和贝尔不等式违反

Conclusion: 提出的双世界随机方法为量子力学提供了一个完整的现实解释框架，无需引入波函数坍缩等概念，同时保持与标准量子力学的所有预测一致

Abstract: I introduce a novel realistic, stochastic approach to quantum mechanics by extending the recently proposed grabit formalism \cite{braun_stochastic_2022} to two Twin Worlds. According to the picture developed, we live at the intersection of two worlds with identical stochastic laws of evolution. Our World is limited to that intersection, and only coincidence events from the two Twin Worlds, post-selected automatically by our restriction to the intersection, have physical reality in Our World. This fully reproduces standard non-relativistic quantum mechanics, including Born's rule and the violation of Bell's inequality. I derive the stochastic evolution equation in each Twin World that fully reproduces Schrödinger's equation for an arbitrary number of particles with arbitrary interactions, and demonstrate that hall-mark quantum effects such as tunnling are correctly reproduced.

</details>


### [90] [Quantum Enhanced Pauli Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14485)
*S. Majumder,J. R. Garrison,L. Luo,B. Mitchell,M. Amico,A. Seif,M. Tran,K. Sharma,E. van den Berg,Z. Minev,L. C. G. Govia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuEPP是一种混合量子-经典算法，通过量子资源增强经典Pauli路径模拟的准确性，无需噪声表征即可提供渐进无偏估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子误差缓解技术通常依赖于不可验证的电路噪声假设，无法保证残余偏差误差的大小，需要一种无需噪声表征、适用于任意电路且能提供渐进无偏估计的方法

Method: 使用Clifford微扰理论构建经典可模拟的Clifford电路集合，通过量子硬件测量该集合的期望值来推断全局缩放因子，从而校正目标电路的量子执行结果

Result: 在IBM Heron硬件上对49量子比特、深度80的2D随机镜像电路以及Trotter化哈密顿演化进行演示，结果显示QuEPP超越了经典CPT和未缓解的量子结果

Conclusion: QuEPP为前容错时代提供了一种简单、可扩展且无模型的准确量子计算框架，通过量子资源增强经典模拟，实现渐进无偏估计

Abstract: Accurately estimating observables on noisy quantum devices remains a central challenge for near-term quantum algorithms. While quantum error mitigation techniques can reduce noise-induced bias, they often rely on unverifiable assumptions about the circuit noise, and cannot guarantee the magnitude of residual bias error. Here, rather than using classical resources to mitigate a noisy quantum circuit execution, we propose a hybrid algorithm that uses quantum resources to improve the accuracy of approximate classical Pauli-path simulation. Our protocol, Quantum Enhanced Pauli Propagation (QuEPP), uses Clifford perturbation theory (CPT) to construct a classically simulable ensemble of Clifford circuits from the low-order terms in CPT, which directly provide the approximate classical Pauli-path simulation of the target circuit. Noisy quantum expectation values of this ensemble are then used to infer a global rescaling factor that corrects quantum execution of the target circuit, providing higher-order contributions absent from the truncated low-order classical simulation. This approach requires no noise characterization, applies to arbitrary circuits, and provides a provable route to asymptotically unbiased estimates. Using IBM Heron hardware, we demonstrate QuEPP on 2D random mirror circuits of up to 49 qubits and circuit depth 80, as well as Trotterized Hamiltonian evolution, showing consistent improvements beyond classical CPT and unmitigated quantum results. QuEPP offers a simple, scalable, and model-free framework for enabling accurate quantum computation in the pre-fault-tolerant era.

</details>


### [91] [Parrondo-type enhancement of quantum-state transfer in spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14537)
*Rafael Vieira,Edgard P. M. Amorim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过周期性切换两种边界耦合配置的XX自旋链，利用Parrondo悖论思想实现量子态传输的相干增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限未调制的自旋链中，静态哈密顿量相关的色散和干涉效应通常限制了可实现的传输保真度。需要探索时间依赖的控制策略来增强量子态传输

Method: 使用Floquet理论和单激发子空间数值模拟，研究在两种具有不同边界耦合的哈密顿量之间周期性切换的XX自旋链，分析单量子比特和Bell态的传输

Result: 周期性驱动可以超越静态配置，实现更高的传输保真度。这种增强源于驱动哈密顿量的非对易性，反映了纯粹的相干干涉效应。协议在适度无序下保持稳定

Conclusion: 边界耦合的简单时间依赖控制为增强自旋链通信通道中的量子态传输提供了有效策略，可用于优化工程多体系统中的量子信息处理

Abstract: Spin chains have been widely studied as quantum channels for short-distance communication in quantum devices, where many-body dynamics can mediate quantum-state transfer between distant sites. In finite unmodulated chains, however, dispersion and interference effects associated with the static Hamiltonian often limit the achievable transfer fidelity. Here we investigate the transfer of single-qubit and Bell states in finite $XX$ spin chains under periodic switching between two Hamiltonians with different boundary couplings. Inspired by Parrondo's paradox, we examine whether alternating between two configurations that individually yield suboptimal transfer fidelities can generate enhanced coherent transmission. Using Floquet theory together with numerical simulations in the single-excitation subspace, we show that periodic driving can outperform static configurations and achieve higher transfer fidelities. This enhancement originates from the noncommutativity of the driven Hamiltonians and reflects a purely coherent interference effect. We further analyze the dependence of the protocol on system size and driving parameters and examine its robustness to asymmetric boundary couplings. Our results show that the transfer fidelity remains stable under moderate disorder, indicating that simple time-dependent control of boundary couplings provides an effective strategy to enhance quantum-state transfer in spin-chain communication channels and optimize quantum information processing in engineered many-body systems.

</details>


### [92] [Reversible Lifetime Semantics for Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14538)
*Simone Faro,Francesco Pio Marino,Gabriele Messina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于语义的逆计算（uncomputation）作为量子编程基础，引入作用域限定的活性引导逆计算模型，通过静态分析确定最早安全回收点，减少电路深度并限制峰值活跃量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子编程将逆计算视为技术性清理机制，本文旨在将其提升为语义基础，通过形式化模型实现更高效、安全的量子资源管理。

Method: 在Qutes语言中引入作用域限定的活性引导逆计算模型，利用词法作用域限定变量生命周期，通过静态活性和纠缠分析确定最早安全回收点，定义语义生命周期和恢复不变性。

Result: 证明了嵌套作用域下的组合正确性，早期回收可减少电路深度（避免关键路径开销）并限制峰值活跃量子比特（通过受控辅助比特重用）；参数传递语义从相同生命周期规则中自然产生。

Conclusion: 逆计算应作为量子编程的语义基础而非技术清理，作用域限定的活性引导逆计算模型能有效管理量子资源，自动逆计算在不可逆性、持久纠缠和别名等约束下需要限制。

Abstract: Reversible computation requires that intermediate data be explicitly undone rather than discarded. In quantum programming, this principle appears as uncomputation, usually treated as a technical cleanup mechanism. We instead present uncomputation as a semantic foundation. In the Qutes language, we introduce a formal model of \emph{Scope-Bounded Liveness-Guided Uncomputation}, where lexical scope bounds variable lifetime and static liveness and entanglement analysis determine the earliest safe reclamation point. We define semantic lifetime and a Restoration Invariant ensuring that temporary quantum information disappears once it becomes semantically irrelevant. We prove compositional correctness under nested scopes and show that early reclamation can reduce circuit depth by avoiding critical-path overhead and can bound peak live qubits through disciplined ancilla reuse. Finally, we show that parameter passing semantics emerges from the same lifetime discipline, with pass-by-value and pass-by-reference corresponding to different lifetime boundaries, and we characterize the constraints (irreversibility, persistent entanglement, and aliasing) under which automatic uncomputation must be restricted.

</details>


### [93] [IQP Born Machines under Data-dependent and Agnostic Initialization Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14576)
*Sacha Lerch,Joseph Bowles,Ricard Puig,Erik Armengol,Zoë Holmes,Supanut Thanasilp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于IQP电路的量子电路Born机器在最大均值差异损失下的优化问题，分析了不同初始化策略对损失函数景观的影响，包括随机初始化导致的贫瘠高原问题，以及数据无关和数据相关初始化的梯度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IQP-QCBMs作为量子生成模型的自然候选者，其训练使用基于低体Pauli-Z关联子的MMD损失，但初始化对优化景观的影响尚不清楚。需要理解不同初始化策略如何影响训练效率和收敛性。

Method: 1) 证明随机全角度范围初始化下MMD损失存在贫瘠高原；2) 为恒等初始化和无偏数据无关初始化建立损失方差下界；3) 提出与目标分布对齐的数据依赖初始化；4) 在适当假设下证明该初始化具有可证明的梯度；5) 在150量子位的基因组数据上训练电路验证效果。

Result: 1) 随机初始化导致贫瘠高原问题；2) 恒等和无偏数据无关初始化有损失方差下界保证；3) 数据依赖初始化在适当条件下具有可证明的梯度，收敛更快；4) 150量子位基因组数据训练验证了该初始化的有效性；5) 开发的方差下界框架适用于更广泛的非线性损失函数。

Conclusion: 初始化策略对IQP-QCBM训练至关重要，数据依赖初始化能避免贫瘠高原、提供可证明梯度并加速收敛。开发的方差下界框架为量子机器学习中的预热启动分析提供了更广泛的工具集。

Abstract: Quantum circuit Born machines based on instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits are natural candidates for quantum generative modeling, both because of their probabilistic structure and because IQP sampling is provably classically hard in certain regimes. Recent proposals focus on training IQP-QCBMs using Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) losses built from low-body Pauli-$Z$ correlators, but the effect of initialization on the resulting optimization landscape remains poorly understood. In this work, we address this by first proving that the MMD loss landscape suffers from barren plateaus for random full-angle-range initializations of IQP circuits. We then establish lower bounds on the loss variance for identity and an unbiased data-agnostic initialization. We then additionally consider a data-dependent initialization that is better aligned with the target distribution and, under suitable assumptions, yields provable gradients and generally converges quicker to a good minimum (as indicated by our training of circuits with 150 qubits on genomic data). Finally, as a by-product, the developed variance lower bound framework is applicable to a general class of non-linear losses, offering a broader toolset for analyzing warm-starts in quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [94] [Evaluating Calibration-Based Digital Twins for IBM Quantum Hardware Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14607)
*Edgars Bautra,Maksims Dimitrijevs,Abuzer Yakaryilmaz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于校准的IBM量子硬件数字孪生，通过校准CSV文件构建孪生体，在经典模拟器上复现硬件测量结果，并比较了四种孪生变体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为IBM量子硬件开发有效的数字孪生，以在经典模拟器上准确复现硬件测量结果，验证不同孪生构建方法的有效性，并评估其在特定执行设置下的适用性。

Method: 提出从可下载校准CSV文件构建数字孪生的工作流程：将相干时间、门和读出错误率、操作持续时间映射到热弛豫、退极化和读出错误通道，同时重建有向耦合图以在编译期间恢复连接约束。比较四种孪生变体：CSV构建、后端派生模拟器、后端派生噪声模型和假后端快照，在统一的执行和验证协议下进行评估。

Result: 在ibm_brisbane和ibm_sherbrooke两个IBM QPU上的实验表明，基于可下载校准CSV数据构建的孪生通常与硬件达成最接近的一致性，而后端派生孪生提供了具有竞争力的实用基线。加权Jaccard相似度显示一致性取决于目标设备和编译设置。

Conclusion: 数字孪生的有效性高度依赖于特定执行设置，不能假设跨设备的可转移性。基于校准CSV构建的孪生表现最佳，但需要针对具体设备和编译设置进行验证。

Abstract: We evaluate calibration-based digital twins for IBM Quantum hardware, aiming to reproduce hardware measurement outcomes on classical simulators. We present a workflow that builds twins from downloadable calibration CSV files by mapping coherence times, gate and readout error rates, and operation durations to thermal-relaxation, depolarizing, and readout error channels, while reconstructing a directed coupling map to restore connectivity constraints during transpilation. We compare four twin variants (CSV-built, backend-derived simulator, backend-derived noise model, and fake-backend snapshots) under a common execution and validation protocol. Experiments on two IBM QPUs, ibm_brisbane and ibm_sherbrooke, use randomized five-qubit circuits of depths 10, 20, and 30 across four optimization levels. Weighted Jaccard similarity indicates that twins constructed from downloadable calibration CSV data often achieved the closest agreement with hardware, while backend-derived twins provided competitive and practical baselines. The results further show that agreement depends on both the target device and the transpilation settings, underscoring the need to validate digital twins for the specific execution setup rather than assuming transferability across devices.

</details>


### [95] [GPU-Accelerated Quantum Simulation of Stabilizer Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14641)
*Muhammad Osama,Dimitrios Thanos,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QuaSARQ GPU加速稳定子模拟器，通过并行算法优化稳定子电路模拟，实现105倍加速和80%能耗降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定子电路模拟器在处理大规模量子比特和深度电路时存在性能瓶颈，特别是高斯消元和测量更新等关键操作存在顺序依赖，限制了GPU并行化效率

Method: 开发新的并行算法，将稳定子模拟中的关键瓶颈（如高斯消元和测量更新）重新设计为GPU优化的原语，消除顺序依赖并最大化内存合并；实现QuaSARQ GPU加速稳定子模拟器

Result: 在180,000量子比特、深度1,000（约1.3亿门）的基准测试中，QuaSARQ实现高达105倍加速，能耗降低超过80%；在多项基准测试中持续优于Stim、Qiskit-Aer、Qibo、Cirq和PennyLane等现有模拟器

Conclusion: 提出的并行算法显著提升了稳定子电路模拟的可扩展性，特别适用于涉及大量测量和采样的工作负载，为大规模量子电路模拟提供了高效的GPU加速解决方案

Abstract: We introduce new parallel algorithms for efficiently simulating stabilizer (Clifford) circuits on GPUs, with a focus on data-parallel tableau evolution and scalable handling of projective measurements. Our approach reformulates key bottlenecks in stabilizer simulation -- such as Gaussian elimination and measurement updates -- into GPU-tailored primitives that eliminate sequential dependencies and maximize memory coalescing. We implement these techniques in QuaSARQ, a GPU-accelerated stabilizer simulator designed for large qubit counts and many-shot sampling. Across a broad benchmark suite reaching 180,000 qubits and depth 1,000 (roughly 130M gates), QuaSARQ shows substantial runtime improvements, with up to 105$\times$ speedup, and over 80% energy reduction on demanding instances. Moreover, QuaSARQ consistently outperforms Stim, a state-of-the-art CPU-optimized stabilizer simulator, as well as Qiskit-Aer (CPU/GPU), Qibo, Cirq, and PennyLane. Finally, QuaSARQ exhibits a significant advantage in many-shot sampling on large workloads. These results demonstrate that our parallel algorithms can significantly advance the scalability of stabilizer-circuit simulation, particularly for workloads involving extensive measurements and sampling.

</details>


### [96] [Computing logical error thresholds with the Pauli Frame Sparse Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14670)
*Thomas Tuloup,Thomas Ayral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出稀疏经典表示、截断策略和高效采样方法，超越Clifford操作和Pauli误差，精确计算量子纠错阈值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要局限于Clifford操作和Pauli误差，无法准确预测实际量子系统中的相干噪声阈值和魔术态制备协议的性能

Method: 采用稀疏经典表示、截断策略和高效重要性采样方法，能够在电路层面（考虑完整综合征电路）分析非Clifford操作和相干噪声

Result: 1. 相干噪声阈值被Pauli-twirling近似高估约4倍；2. 魔术态培养协议中T门与S门注入误差率的乘性因子可达7，而非先前推测值

Conclusion: 该方法能够超越传统限制，为量子纠错阈值的精确计算提供了新工具，揭示了实际噪声模型与简化模型之间的显著差异

Abstract: We introduce a sparse classical representation, a truncation strategy and a shot-efficient sampling method to push the classical prediction of quantum error correction thresholds beyond Clifford operations and Pauli errors. As two illustrations of the potential of our method, we first show that coherent noise error thresholds, when computed at the circuit level (i.e taking into account full syndrome circuits) for distances up to d=9, are systematically overestimated (by a factor of about 4) by a Pauli-twirling approximation of the noise. We then apply our method to the recently introduced magic-state cultivation protocol. We show, through shot-efficient importance sampling, that, at distance d=5, the multiplicative factor between the T-gate and the S-gate injection error rate is not the one conjectured from low-d computations: it can be as large as 7.

</details>


### [97] [Adaptive Control of Stochastic Error Accumulation in Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14687)
*Tirtha Haque*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出Chronological Deep Q-Network (Ch-DQN)方法，将容错量子计算建模为随机控制问题，通过跟踪噪声演化和累积风险来延长逻辑量子比特的生存时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实量子硬件中的非平稳噪声和随机漂移导致逻辑错误来自误差的时间累积而非独立事件。静态解码和固定校准技术无法处理误差的时间相关性或控制引起的反作用，因此需要能够跟踪噪声演化的控制策略。

Method: 将容错量子计算建模为随机控制问题，使用潜在噪声参数随时间变化的简化量子动力学模型。提出Chronological Deep Q-Network (Ch-DQN)，维护跟踪噪声演化和累积风险的内部信念状态。训练时使用轨迹后向细化采样缓慢漂移的操作模式，运行时保持严格因果推理。采用分数元更新稳定非平稳、控制耦合动力学下的学习。

Result: 通过包含随机漂移和决策反馈的多距离模拟，Ch-DQN相对于静态和循环基线方法，能够抑制风险累积并延长逻辑生存时间。

Conclusion: 在这种机制下，纠错不再是静态解码任务，而是由底层噪声动力学随时间决定成功与否的控制过程。Ch-DQN方法为处理非平稳噪声环境下的容错量子计算提供了有效的控制框架。

Abstract: In realistic hardware for quantum computation that possesses fault-tolerance, non-stationary noise and stochastic drift lead to logical failure from the temporal accumulation of errors, not from independent events. Static decoding and fixed calibration techniques are structurally incompatible with this situation because they do not take into account temporal correlations between errors or control-induced back-action of errors. These effects motivate control policies that must track noise evolution across correction cycles, rather than respond to individual syndromes in isolation.
  We treat fault-tolerant quantum computation as a stochastic control problem, modelled using reduced quantum dynamics in which Pauli error processes are governed by latent noise parameters that vary temporally. From this perspective, logical failure arises through the accumulation of a hazard variable, and the corresponding control objective depends on the full history of observations.
  Operating under these conditions, a Chronological Deep Q-Network (Ch-DQN) maintains an internal belief state that tracks both noise evolution and accumulated hazard. During training, backward refinement of trajectories is used to sample slowly drifting modes of operation, while runtime inference remains strictly causal. A fractional meta-update stabilizes learning in the presence of non-stationary, control-coupled dynamics.
  Through multi-distance simulations that incorporate stochastic drift and feedback from decision-making, Ch-DQN suppresses hazard accumulation and extends logical survival time relative to static and recurrent baselines. Error correction in this regime is therefore no longer a static decoding task, but a control process whose success is determined over time by the underlying noise dynamics.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Quantum Operator Dynamics from Short-Time Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14699)
*Jinyang Li,Satoshi Iso,Shunji Matsuura,Lingxiao Wang,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于神经ODE的物理驱动框架，从短时测量重建量子可观测量长时动力学，用于克服量子设备退相干限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子设备因退相干限制只能进行短时演化，但量子可观测量实时动力学对获取激发谱和关联函数至关重要，需要从短时测量推断长时行为

Method: 采用神经ODE框架结合物理驱动设计：将可观测量在Pauli基下展开，利用局域性和对称性约束减少参数，从短时测量数据学习系数动力学

Result: 在横场Ising模型中，该方法能准确外推长时行为，从含噪短时信号解析激发谱，证明其可扩展性和数据效率

Conclusion: 该方法为从实际量子硬件提取动力学和谱信息提供了可扩展、数据高效的策略，克服了退相干限制

Abstract: Real-time dynamics of quantum observables provide direct access to excitation spectra and correlation functions in quantum many-body systems, but currently available quantum devices are limited to short evolution times due to decoherence. We propose a neural ordinary differential equation (Neural ODE) framework with physics-driven designs to reconstruct long-time operator dynamics from short-time measurements. By expanding observables in the Pauli basis and exploiting locality and symmetry constraints, the operator evolution is reduced to a tractable set of coefficients whose dynamics are learned from data. Applied to the transverse-field Ising model, the method accurately extrapolates long-time behavior and resolves excitation spectra from noisy short-time signals. Our results demonstrate a scalable and data-efficient strategy for extracting dynamical and spectral information from practical quantum hardware.

</details>


### [99] [Engineering walk-off-induced orbital angular momentum spectrum in spontaneous parametric downconversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14742)
*Yang Xu,Robert W. Boyd*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文定量分析了SPDC过程中泵浦光空间走效对轨道角动量守恒的破坏，推导了总OAM分布与泵浦走效角度的标度律，并探索了利用空间走效作为量子态工程机制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实实验中，泵浦光的空间走效会破坏系统的旋转对称性，导致总OAM守恒被破坏，从而降低生成量子态的保真度。虽然走效补偿已成为成熟的实验技术，但对SPDC中空间走效效应的系统模态分析仍不完整。

Method: 对SPDC过程中的泵浦走效效应进行定量分析，推导总OAM分布相对于泵浦走效角度的标度律，并探索利用空间走效作为量子态工程机制的可行性。

Result: 建立了总OAM分布与泵浦走效角度之间的标度关系，证明了空间走效可以作为工程化生成量子态的有效机制，为实际实验条件下OAM纠缠态的生成提供了指导。

Conclusion: 该研究系统分析了SPDC中泵浦走效对OAM守恒的破坏效应，建立了相关的标度律，并展示了利用空间走效进行量子态工程化的潜力，为实际实验条件下高维OAM纠缠态的生成提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Spontaneous parametric downconversion (SPDC) has been considered as a reliable source of high- dimensional entangled states in orbital angular momentum (OAM) basis. In real-world experiments, the spatial walk-off of the pump often degrades the fidelity of the generated quantum state. Since the walk-off effect breaks the rotational symmetry of the system, the conservation of total OAM is violated. Although the compensation of walk-off effects has become a well-established experimental technique, a systematic modal analysis of the spatial walk-off effect is still incomplete for SPDC. Here, we quantitatively analyze the violation of OAM conservation due to the pump walk-off effect in SPDC processes. We have derived a scaling law of the total OAM distribution with respect to the pump walk-off angle. We have also explored the feasibility of using the spatial walk-off as a mechanism to engineer the generated quantum state. Our study has provided guidelines for the generation of OAM-entangled state under realistic experimental conditions.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Exponential Quantum Improvements in Solving Cardinality-Constrained Binary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14744)
*Haomu Yuan,Hanqing Wu,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Bin Cheng,Crispin H. W. Barnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Grover算法的量子方法，用于基数约束的二元优化问题，相比非结构化搜索实现指数级加速，并开发了ADMM混合经典-量子框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基数约束的二元优化是机器学习、金融和科学计算中的基本计算原语，现有方法在处理大规模问题时效率有限，需要开发更高效的量子算法。

Method: 1. 引入基于Grover的量子算法，利用固定基数可行子空间的结构和最优解存在的自然承诺；2. 对于二次目标函数，算法实现O(√(C(n,k)/M))次Grover旋转；3. 基于ADMM算法开发混合经典-量子框架，保证输出ε-近似解。

Result: 1. 相比{0,1}^n上的非结构化搜索，Grover迭代次数实现指数级减少；2. 混合框架保证输出ε-近似解，一致性容差为ε+δ；3. 在某些参数范围内相比现有Grover方法实现指数级改进。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了量子资源在实际应用中的潜力，为约束二元优化中的量子优势铺平了道路，在特定参数范围内实现了指数级改进。

Abstract: Cardinality-constrained binary optimization is a fundamental computational primitive with broad applications in machine learning, finance, and scientific computing. In this work, we introduce a Grover-based quantum algorithm that exploits the structure of the fixed-cardinality feasible subspace under a natural promise on solution existence. For quadratic objectives, our approach achieves ${O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\binom{n}{k}}{M}}\right)$ Grover rotations for any fixed cardinality $k$ and degeneracy of the optima $M$, yielding an exponential reduction in the number of Grover iterations compared with unstructured search over $\{0,1\}^n$. Building on this result, we develop a hybrid classical--quantum framework based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm. The proposed framework is guaranteed to output an $ε$-approximate solution with a consistency tolerance $ε+ δ$ using at most $ {O}\left(\sqrt{\binom{n}{k}}\frac{n^{6}k^{3/2} }{ \sqrt{M}ε^2 δ}\right)$ queries to a quadratic oracle, together with ${O}\left(\frac{n^{6}k^{3/2}}{ε^2δ}\right)$ classical overhead. Overall, our method suggests a practical use of quantum resources and demonstrates an exponential improvements over existing Grover-based approaches in certain parameter regimes, thereby paving the way toward quantum advantage in constrained binary optimization.

</details>


### [101] [Almost perfect strategies for projection games are approximately tracial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14746)
*Eric Culf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将同步游戏的策略结构分析扩展到投影游戏，建立了量子模型和交换算子模型中近似完美策略与迹策略之间的转换关系，改进了约束系统游戏的舍入结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影游戏是一类重要的非局域游戏，但由于玩家之间的不对称性，它们通常不是同步的。因此，同步游戏中约束近似完美策略结构的强大结果无法直接应用于投影游戏。本研究旨在将Marrakchi和de la Salle的同步游戏结果扩展到投影游戏，以分析量子模型和交换算子模型中近似完美策略的结构。

Method: 作者将Marrakchi和de la Salle的同步游戏结果适配到投影游戏。他们证明了在量子模型和交换算子模型中，任何以概率1-ε获胜的策略都可以转化为一个迹策略，该迹策略以概率1-O((Lε)^{1/4})获胜，其中L是第二个玩家问题条件概率最小值的倒数。

Result: 对于投影游戏，在量子模型和交换算子模型中，任何获胜概率为1-ε的策略都可以转化为获胜概率为1-O((Lε)^{1/4})的迹策略。对于约束系统游戏，这一结果强化了Paddock的舍入结果：消除了对约束数量的依赖，改进了对约束大小的依赖，并推广到交换算子设置。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将同步游戏的策略结构分析扩展到投影游戏，建立了近似完美策略与迹策略之间的定量关系。这一结果为投影游戏的分析提供了新的工具，特别在约束系统游戏中取得了比现有结果更强的舍入保证，并推广到了更一般的算子模型。

Abstract: Projection games constitute an important class of nonlocal games where, for any answer from the first player, there is a unique correct answer for the second player. This class of games captures nonlocal games arising from constraint satisfaction problems, oracularisations, and unique games. However, due to the asymmetry between the players, projection games are in general not synchronous, and therefore the powerful results constraining the structure of almost perfect strategies for synchronous games do not apply. In this work, we adapt results of Marrakchi and de la Salle for synchronous games to show that, in both the quantum and commuting-operator models, any strategy that wins with probability $1-\varepsilon$ in a projection game gives rise to a tracial strategy that wins with probability $1-O((L\varepsilon)^{1/4})$, where $L$ is the inverse of the minimal conditional probability of a question for the second player being sampled given a question to the first. For constraint system games, this strengthens the rounding result of Paddock by eliminating the dependence on number of constraints and improving the dependence on constraint size, while also generalising to the commuting-operator setting.

</details>


### [102] [Scalable Self-Testing of Mutually Anticommuting Observables and Maximally Entangled Two-Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15018)
*Souradeep Sasmal,Ritesh K. Singh,Prabuddha Roy,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的自测试框架，用于同时认证高维最大纠缠态和反交换可观测量，通过n设置贝尔不等式实现设备无关的量子资源认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关量子信息的下一个前沿在于可扩展和并行量子资源的认证，这些资源支撑着先进的量子技术。需要一种能够同时测试高维最大纠缠态和反交换观测量的框架。

Method: 采用n设置贝尔不等式，Alice有2^{n-1}个测量设置，Bob有n个测量设置。推导局部本体论边界，使用平方和分解确定最优量子边界（无需预设状态或观测量的维度）。证明达到最大量子违反的物理实现必须对应于参考策略：局部维度至少为2^{⌊n/2⌋}的最大纠缠态，以及形成Clifford代数不可约表示的局部可观测量。

Result: 建立了最小维度与n个相互反交换可观测量之间的自然自测试关系：最大违反所提出的贝尔泛函自然地自测试了与n个相互反交换可观测量兼容的最小维度。分析了协议的鲁棒性，建立了观测贝尔值与实现策略和理想策略之间保真度的定量边界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可扩展、维度无关的路径，用于在完全设备无关的框架中认证高维纠缠和Clifford测量，为认证可扩展和并行量子资源提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The next frontier in device-independent quantum information lies in the certification of scalable and parallel quantum resources, which underpin advanced quantum technologies. We put forth a simultaneous self-testing framework for maximally entangled two-qudit state of local dimension $m_*=2^{\lfloor n/2 \rfloor}$ (equivalently $\lfloor n/2 \rfloor$ copies of maximally entangled two-qubit pairs), together with $n$ numbers of anti-commuting observables on one side. To this end, we employ an $n$-settings Bell inequality comprising two space-like separated observers, Alice and Bob, having $2^{n-1}$ and $n$ number of measurement settings, respectively. We derive the local ontic bound of this inequality and, crucially, employ the Sum-of-Squares decomposition to determine the optimal quantum bound without presupposing the dimension of the state or observables. We then establish that any physical realisation achieving the maximal quantum violation must, up to local isometries and complex conjugation, correspond to a reference strategy consisting of a maximally entangled state of local dimension of at least $2^{\lfloor n/2 \rfloor}$ and local observables forming an irreducible representation of the Clifford algebra. This construction thereby demonstrates that the minimal dimension compatible with $n$ mutually anticommuting observables is naturally self-tested by the maximal violation of the proposed Bell functional. Finally, we analyse the robustness of the protocol by establishing quantitative bounds relating deviations in the observed Bell value to the fidelity between the realised and the ideal strategies. Our results thus provide a scalable, dimension-independent route for the certification of high-dimensional entanglement and Clifford measurements in a fully device-independent framework.

</details>


### [103] [Experimental Demonstration of Twin-Field Quantum Digital Signatures over 504 km](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15174)
*Chun-Hui Zhang,Jing-Yang Liu,Wen-Xuan Zhang,Chang Liu,Hua-Jian Ding,Xing-Yu Zhou,Jian Li,Qin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文首次实验演示了GHz系统的孪生场量子数字签名(TF-QDS)，在504公里光纤上实现了单比特和多比特方案，签名速率比先前工作提高两个数量级，创造了长距离高速率QDS新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子数字签名(QDS)相比基于计算复杂度的经典数字签名，理论上能通过量子力学保证数据完整性、真实性和不可否认性，在密码学中具有巨大发展潜力。然而现有QDS系统在速率和距离上仍受限制，需要突破性能瓶颈。

Method: 采用孪生场量子数字签名(TF-QDS)技术，使用GHz系统进行实验。结合一次性通用哈希方法，在光纤距离上实现单比特和多比特签名方案。通过光纤盘进行传输距离测试。

Result: 实现了504公里光纤的最大传输距离（单比特和多比特方案），比现有最先进QDS实验超出200多公里。对于1Mbit文件，在302公里光纤距离上实现了每秒21.1次的最大签名速率。在相似距离下，单比特和多比特方案的签名速率都比先前工作提高两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该工作创造了长距离高速率量子数字签名的新纪录，代表了QDS发展的重要一步，为量子数字签名的实际应用提供了关键技术突破。

Abstract: Digital signatures are one of the security cornerstones of the current information age. Compared with classical digital signatures based on computational complexity, quantum digital signatures (QDS) theoretically guarantee data integrity, authenticity, and non-repudiation by quantum mechanics, showing great potential for development in cryptography and thus attracting widespread attention. However, the performance of existing QDS systems are still limited in rate and distance. Here we report the first experimental demonstration of twin-field QDS (TF-QDS) using a GHz system. We achieve a maximum transmission distance of 504 km fiber spools for both single-bit and multi-bit schemes, surpassing all existing state-of-the-art QDS experiments more than 200 km. Furthermore, by combining the one-time universal hash method, we achieve a maximum signature rate of 21.1 times per second for a 1 Mbit file over fiber distances up to 302 km. In this work, the signature rates of both single-bit scheme and multi-bit scheme are more than two orders of magnitude higher than that of previous works at similar distance. Our work provides a new record for long-distance and high-rate QDS, representing a significant step in the development of QDS.

</details>


### [104] [Asymptotically good bosonic Fock state codes: Exact and approximate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15190)
*Dor Elimelech,Arda Aydin,Alexander Barg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于截断振幅阻尼信道形式化，证明了Fock态码对随机光子损失的精确与近似纠错等价性，利用ℓ₁度量的经典码构造了具有有界单模占用的渐近好Fock态码，并推广到置换不变码和核态空间码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多模Fock态码在振幅阻尼噪声下的精确与近似纠错，旨在构建具有有界单模占用的渐近好量子纠错码，以增强系统稳定性并延长相干寿命。

Method: 1) 形式化截断振幅阻尼信道；2) 证明Fock态码对随机光子损失的精确与近似纠错等价性；3) 利用ℓ₁度量距离大的经典码构造Fock态码；4) 通过Fock态码与置换不变码的关系构造相关码类。

Result: 获得了具有有界单模占用的渐近好Fock态码（精确与近似），这些码能提高相干寿命、降低光子损失概率，并推广得到渐近好的qudit置换不变码和核态空间码。

Conclusion: 通过形式化截断振幅阻尼信道，建立了Fock态码精确与近似纠错的等价性，基于ℓ₁度量的经典码构造方法为构建具有有界单模占用的渐近好量子纠错码提供了有效途径，并扩展了置换不变码和核态空间码的构造。

Abstract: We examine exact and approximate error correction for multi-mode Fock state codes protecting against the amplitude damping noise. Based on a new formalization of the truncated amplitude damping channel, we show the equivalence of exact and approximate error correction for Fock state codes against random photon losses. Leveraging the recently found construction method based on classical codes with large distance measured in the $\ell_1$ metric, we construct asymptotically good (exact and approximate) Fock state codes. These codes have an additional property of bounded per-mode occupancy, which increases the coherence lifetime of code states and reduces the photon loss probability, both of which have a positive impact on the stability of the system. Using the relation between Fock state code construction and permutation invariant (PI) codes, we also obtain families of asymptotically good qudit PI codes as well as codes in monolithic nuclear state spaces.

</details>


### [105] [Asymmetric Linear-Combination-of-Unitaries Realization of Quantum Convolution via Modular Adders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15214)
*Chen Yang,Kodai Kanemaru,Norio Yoshida,Sergey Gusarov,Hiroshi C. Watanabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于非对称LCU框架的量子离散循环卷积实现方法，通过引入反射移位算子和对称化操作，构建了与QSVT兼容的厄米特卷积算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LCU框架实现卷积时存在相位信息丢失问题，且需要核相关的逆制备操作。论文旨在开发一种能保留核系数相位、避免核相关逆制备、并与QSVT等谱变换技术兼容的量子卷积实现方法。

Method: 采用非对称LCU框架，将循环卷积实现为后选择块电路；引入反转矩阵Jn和反射移位算子~Li,n=Li,nJn进行对称化；构建递归算子代数；提供透明递归构造和优化的比特级编译实现。

Result: 成功实现了保留核系数相位的量子卷积，避免了核相关的逆制备；构建了厄米特卷积算子Hn(b)，可直接与QSVT接口；提供了递归构造和优化编译两种等效实现；分析了实现权衡和资源缩放。

Conclusion: 非对称LCU框架为量子卷积提供了有效的实现途径，对称化操作产生的厄米特算子使其与QSVT等高级量子算法兼容，为量子信号处理和谱分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Discrete circular convolution over $\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}$ is a linear operator and can be implemented on quantum hardware within the linear-combination-of-unitaries (LCU) framework. In this work, we make this connection explicit through an asymmetric-LCU formulation: circular convolution is the postselected block of a circuit whose controlled-shift unitary is modular addition on computational-basis states. The asymmetry is essential: fixing the postselection state to the uniform state $|u\rangle$ while supplying the kernel state $|\mathbf{b}\rangle$ as the input ancilla naturally preserves the complex coefficients $b_i$ within the block, whereas a symmetric overlap would yield $|b_i|^2$ weights and erase their phases. Accordingly, when $|\mathbf{a}\rangle$ and $|\mathbf{b}\rangle$ are supplied by upstream quantum routines, the convolution subroutine requires only the fixed uncompute $\mathrm{PREP}_u^\dagger$, completely avoiding the need for a kernel-dependent inverse preparation $\mathrm{PREP}_b^\dagger$. We then introduce a reversal matrix $J_n=X^{\otimes n}$ and define reflected shifts $\widetilde{L}_{i,n}=L_{i,n}J_n$. This symmetrization yields a recursive operator algebra for convolution that is natively compatible with LCU/block-encoding workflows. The resulting symmetrized operator differs from circular convolution only by one known input-side $J_n$ layer. Crucially, for real-valued kernels, the resulting operator $H_n(\mathbf{b})=\sum_i b_i\widetilde{L}_{i,n}$ is Hermitian, providing a direct Hermitian interface for quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) and related spectral transformations. Based on this framework, we present a transparent recursive construction, paired with an exactly equivalent optimized bitwise compilation of the same $\mathrm{SELECT}$ block. Finally, we evaluate implementation trade-offs and resource scaling under explicit cost-model conventions.

</details>


### [106] [Gaussian superpositions for bosonic encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15258)
*Federico Centrone,Juan Pablo Paz,Augusto Roncaglia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种精确的有限流形编码方法，用于处理有限高斯分支支撑的非高斯玻色态，将无限维希尔伯特空间问题转化为有效密度矩阵，便于使用有限维量子信息工具进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯玻色态在相互作用光-物质系统、多体平台和相对论量子场论中普遍存在，但其定量表征受到无限维希尔伯特空间和Fock空间截断方法可扩展性差的阻碍。

Method: 引入精确的有限流形编码，用于支撑在有限高斯分支上的态，通过高斯重叠确定有效密度矩阵的矩阵元，从而能够直接使用标准有限维量子信息工具。

Result: 获得了熵和相对熵非高斯性的闭式且数值稳定的评估，推导了任意多模双分支高斯叠加态的双分纠缠负性的解析表达式，包括最小分支退相干模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为实验可访问的连续变量资源（如猫态和测量条件态）与离散变量信息度量之间提供了实用桥梁，在多个量子技术平台中具有直接的基准测试非高斯资源的应用。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian bosonic states are ubiquitous in interacting light--matter systems, many-body platforms, and relativistic quantum field settings, but their quantitative characterization is hindered by the infinite-dimensional Hilbert space and by the poor scalability of Fock-space truncation methods. We introduce an exact finite-manifold encoding for states supported on a finite span of Gaussian branches, enabling the use of standard finite-dimensional quantum-information tools directly on an effective density matrix whose entries are determined by Gaussian overlaps. As demonstrations, we obtain closed-form and numerically stable evaluations of entropies and relative-entropy non-Gaussianity, and derive an analytic expression for the bipartite entanglement negativity of arbitrary multimode two-branch Gaussian superpositions, including a minimal which-branch dephasing model. Our framework provides a practical bridge between experimentally accessible continuous-variable resources (e.g., cat-like and measurement-conditioned states) and discrete-variable information measures, with immediate applications to benchmarking non-Gaussian resources in several quantum technology platforms.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Liang Information Flow vs. Out-of-Time-Order Correlators as Chaos Diagnostics in the Mixed-Field Ising Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15315)
*Bin Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QLIF与OTOC作为量子混沌诊断工具在混合场伊辛链中的系统比较研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较量子梁信息流(QLIF)与时间无序关联函数(OTOC)作为量子混沌诊断工具的性能，探索它们在不同量子系统中的互补性

Method: 使用精确对角化和矩阵乘积态-时间演化块解耦方法，在一维混合场伊辛链中系统比较QLIF和OTOC的行为特征

Result: QLIF早期幂律增长和波前传播速度在可积和混沌参数下相同，仅由局域哈密顿结构控制；QLIF信号强度对初态敏感；时间积分QLIF可作为晚期混沌诊断工具

Conclusion: QLIF是OTOC的互补探针，具有不同的最佳操作区域，时间积分QLIF能有效区分混沌系统的不可逆热化和可积系统的可逆准粒子动力学

Abstract: We systematically compare Quantum Liang Information Flow (QLIF) a recently proposed causal information measure with the out-of-time-order correlator (OTOC) as diagnostics of quantum chaos in the one-dimensional mixed-field Ising chain. Using exact diagonalization and MPS-TEBD, we show that the early-time power-law growth and wavefront propagation velocity of QLIF are identical for integrable and chaotic parameters, being controlled solely by the local Hamiltonian structure. The QLIF signal strength depends sensitively on the initial state, spanning four orders of magnitude across product states, ground state eigenstate evolution, and quantum quench protocols. We identify the time-integrated QLIF as a late-time chaos diagnostic: it grows linearly (monotonically) in chaotic systems, reflecting irreversible thermalization, while saturating or oscillating in integrable systems, reflecting reversible quasiparticle dynamics. These findings establish QLIF as a complementary probe to OTOC, with distinct optimal operating regimes.

</details>


### [108] [Autonomous quantum heat engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15355)
*Tuomas Uusnäkki,Miika Rasola,Vasilii Vadimov,Priyank Singh,Ahmad Darwish,Mikko Möttönen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验实现了基于超导电路的自洽量子热机，通过热驱动产生相干微波功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前实验中的量子热机需要外部驱动，使得观测输出功变得困难。自洽量子热机仅需热流即可运行并产生功，但尚未在任何系统中实验实现，尽管已有大量理论研究。

Method: 构建基于超导电路的自洽量子热机：将两个超导谐振器与超导量子干涉器件耦合，实现近似奥托循环；将该系统与光谱滤波的热库和冷库耦合；通过改变实验条件观察系统内部动力学

Result: 观察到仅由热库驱动的系统内部动力学产生的相干微波功率生成，验证了先前对该电路的理论预测

Conclusion: 该实验为详细研究热机中的量子效应以及将热生相干微波应用于电路量子电动力学开辟了道路

Abstract: Quantum heat engines provide attractive means in quantum thermodynamics for studying the fundamentals of the flow of heat and work. Previous experimental implementations of heat engines operating at the level of a few excitation quanta have utilized external driving, which has made the observation of the produced work challenging. Conversely, autonomous quantum heat engines only require a flow of heat to operate and generate work. However, autonomous quantum heat engines have not yet been experimentally demonstrated in any system despite numerous theoretical investigations. Here, we experimentally realize an autonomous quantum heat engine based on superconducting circuits. We construct the engine circuit implementing an approximate Otto cycle by coupling two superconducting resonators with a superconducting quantum interference device, and coupling this system to spectrally filtered hot and cold reservoirs. By varying the experimental conditions, we observe coherent microwave power generation arising from the internal dynamics of the system driven only by the thermal reservoirs. Our results validate previous theoretical predictions for this circuit and pave the way for detailed studies of quantum effects in heat engines and for using heat-generated coherent microwaves in circuit quantum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [109] [Error semitransparent universal control of a bosonic logical qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15356)
*Saswata Roy,Owen C. Wetherbee,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于动态编码子空间的框架，使用简单线性驱动实现针对光子损耗的错误半透明通用逻辑门，在玻色编码量子比特中实现了扩展的主动操控寿命和误差缓解的通用控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色编码在硬件效率方面具有优势，已实现超越盈亏平衡点的逻辑量子存储，但现有成果仅限于空闲信息保护。实现容错逻辑操作是通用量子计算的关键瓶颈，而现有的错误透明门实验仅限于相位门，需要更通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于动态编码子空间的框架，通过简单线性驱动实现针对振荡器光子损耗的错误半透明通用逻辑门。构建了{X, H, T}逻辑门集，并展示了如何通过该门集中的八个门序列构建复合错误半透明非克利福德操作。

Result: 在光子损耗条件下观察到保真度提高了五倍；通过量子纠错展示了扩展的主动操控寿命；成功构建了复合错误半透明非克利福德操作。该方法与可检测辅助量子比特错误的方法兼容。

Conclusion: 该方法为标准量子控制工具包提供了误差缓解的玻色逻辑量子比特通用控制途径，为解决玻色编码系统中容错逻辑操作的关键瓶颈提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bosonic codes offer hardware-efficient approaches to logical qubit construction and hosted the first demonstration of beyond-break even logical quantum memory.However, such accomplishments were done for idling information, and realization of fault-tolerant logical operations remains a critical bottleneck for universal quantum computation in scaled systems. Error-transparent (ET) gates offer an avenue to resolve this issue, but experimental demonstrations have been limited to phase gates. Here, we introduce a framework based on dynamic encoding subspaces that enables simple linear drives to accomplish universal gates that are error semi-transparent (EsT) to oscillator photon loss. With an EsT logical gate set of {X, H, T}, we observe a five-fold reduction in infidelity conditioned on photon loss, demonstrate extended active-manipulation lifetimes with quantum error correction, and construct a composite EsT non-Clifford operation using a sequence of eight gates from the set. Our approach is compatible with methods for detectable ancilla errors, offering an approach to error-mitigated universal control of bosonic logical qubits with the standard quantum control toolkit.

</details>


### [110] [Using an SU(3)/U(2) Wigner Function to Represent Noisy Spin Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15387)
*Andrew Kolmer Forbes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于处理噪声自旋系综的SU(3)维格纳函数方法，将传统的SU(2)球面表示扩展为包含径向分量的实心球表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SU(2)维格纳函数在处理自旋系综时存在局限性：当系统受到局部噪声（如自发辐射、退极化、非相干光泵浦）影响时，量子态会转移到初始不可约表示之外，使得SU(2)维格纳函数不再适用。需要一种能够处理噪声引起的表示空间变化的方法。

Method: 将噪声自旋系综编码到SU(3)不可约表示中，并计算该表示的SU(3)维格纳函数。通过物理约束将输入参数简化为三个实数参数（极角、方位角和径向分量），从而得到"实心自旋维格纳函数"。

Result: 物理约束消除了维格纳函数输入中的大部分参数，仅保留三个实数参数，可解释为极角、方位角和径向分量。这使得维格纳函数能够在实心球而非空心球面上可视化，形成"实心自旋维格纳函数"。

Conclusion: SU(3)维格纳函数方法成功解决了噪声自旋系综的表示问题，通过引入径向分量扩展了传统SU(2)球面表示，为处理局部噪声引起的表示空间变化提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The SU(2) Wigner function represents a quantum state of a spin-$J$ as a real-valued function on the surface of a 2-sphere. For an ensemble of $N$ spin-1/2 particles, this representation is useful when the dynamics is restricted to a single SU(2) irrep, e.g., the symmetric subspace with $J=N/2$. Physically relevant noise sources tend to be local, such as spontaneous emission, depolarizing, and incoherent optical pumping, all of which transfer the state outside of the initial irrep, and as such the SU(2) Wigner function is no longer a useful representation. In this work, we address this issue by encoding a noisy spin ensemble in an SU(3) irrep, and evaluating the SU(3) Wigner function for that irrep. We find that physical constraints enforced by the noise eliminate all but three real parameters from the input to the Wigner function, which can then be interpreted as a polar, azimuthal, and radial component. This interpretation leads us to refer to the resulting Wigner function as the solid spin Wigner function, visualized on a solid ball rather than a hollow sphere.

</details>


### [111] [MACOR glass-ceramic based UHV cell for quantum technology applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15444)
*M. Proske,S. Boles-Herresthal,D. Latorre-Bastidas,I. Varma,R. Skanda,O. Hellmig,K. Sengstock,A. Wenzlawski,P. Windpassinger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于MACOR玻璃陶瓷的紧凑、低成本、超高真空兼容的真空室，适用于冷原子量子技术应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术（特别是基于冷原子的应用）需要紧凑、可定制、非磁性的真空系统，以满足现场应用需求

Method: 使用MACOR玻璃陶瓷材料制造真空室，提供CF法兰连接商业真空技术接口，以及高数值孔径视窗用于精密光学测量

Result: 真空压力稳定在<1×10⁻¹⁰ mbar超过一年，已成功集成到量子气体实验的真空系统中

Conclusion: 该技术证明了MACOR玻璃陶瓷真空室适用于一般量子技术应用，具有长期稳定性和实用性

Abstract: Compact, customizable, non-magnetic vacuum systems are a key requirement for many field applications of quantum technology based on cold atoms. We report on the development and construction of a compact, low-cost ultra-high vacuum compatible cell using the glass-ceramic MACOR. The cell offers a CF flange connection to commercial vacuum technology, as well as high numerical aperture viewports for precision optical measurements. The presented technology shows stable vacuum pressures of $< 1 \cdot 10^{-10}$ mbar for more than a year since the implementation into the vacuum system of a quantum gas experiment, further proving suitability for general quantum technology applications.

</details>


### [112] [An alternating-minimization method for preparing low-energy states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15495)
*Anurag Anshu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种交替算法来克服量子计算中低能态制备的障碍，通过利用局部哈密顿系统的多个哈密顿量在低能子空间的一致性，基于能量不确定性原理在高能区交替运行能量降低步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中制备低能态是一个核心挑战，现有方法常陷入次优状态（如高能本征态或低方差高能态）。需要开发新方法来突破这一障碍，特别是针对相对低阻挫的局部哈密顿系统。

Method: 提出交替算法：利用局部哈密顿系统存在多个在低能子空间一致的哈密顿量这一特性，基于能量不确定性原理（证明这些哈密顿量在高能区没有共同本征态），在高能区交替运行能量降低步骤。还提出了使用稀疏哈密顿量的能量不确定性原理版本。

Result: 通过数值模拟验证了交替算法在小型系统上的性能：针对1D AKLT模型和一般图上Heisenberg模型的实例。稀疏哈密顿量版本的能量不确定性原理显示在高能区方差呈二次方增长，导致更大的能量变化，可用于模拟具有高能垒的能量剖面。

Conclusion: 交替算法为突破量子低能态制备中的障碍提供了一种启发式方法，特别适用于相对低阻挫的局部哈密顿系统。能量不确定性原理及其稀疏版本为该方法提供了理论基础，数值模拟验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Preparing low energy states is a central challenge in quantum computing and quantum complexity theory. Several known approaches to prepare low energy states often get stuck in suboptimal states, such as high energy eigenstates (or low variance high energy states). We develop a heuristic method to go past this barrier for local Hamiltonian systems with relatively low frustration, by taking advantage of the fact that such systems come with multiple Hamiltonians that agree on the low-energy subspaces. We establish an energy-based uncertainty principle, which shows that these Hamiltonians in fact do not have common eigenstates in the high energy regime. This allows us to run energy lowering steps in an alternating manner over the Hamiltonians. We run numerical simulations to check the performance of the `alternating' algorithm on small system sizes, for the 1D AKLT model and instances of Heisenberg model on general graphs. We also formulate a version of the energy-based uncertainty principle using sparse Hamiltonians, which shows a quadratically larger variance at higher energies and hence leads to a larger energy change. We use this version to simulate the method on energy profiles with high energy barriers.

</details>


### [113] [Robust high-order quantum simulation using finite-width pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15502)
*Leeseok Kim,Milad Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个将一阶脉冲序列提升为高阶序列的通用框架，在有限脉冲宽度效应下保持鲁棒性，实现任意高阶误差缩放


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟中，有限脉冲宽度效应会限制脉冲序列的性能，需要开发既能实现高阶误差缩放又能保持对脉冲宽度效应鲁棒性的方法

Method: 将一阶脉冲序列映射到一阶Trotter公式，应用高阶Trotter公式构造，然后通过动态校正门将演化编译回物理可实现的有限宽度脉冲，还可利用多乘积公式进行更高效构造

Result: 生成的序列能在底层一阶序列的控制周期时间方面实现任意高阶误差缩放，同时保持对有限脉冲宽度效应的鲁棒性，数值验证了预测的误差缩放

Conclusion: 该框架为量子模拟提供了一种系统方法，可将一阶脉冲序列提升为高阶序列，在有限脉冲宽度下实现高阶误差缩放，为更高效的量子模拟任务提供了实用工具

Abstract: We present a general framework for promoting first-order pulse sequences in quantum simulation to higher-order sequences that maintain robustness in the presence of finite pulse-width effects. Our approach maps a given first-order pulse sequence to a first-order Trotter formula, applies higher-order Trotter-formula constructions, and then compiles the resulting evolution back into physically implementable finite-width pulses via dynamically corrected gates. The resulting sequences achieve arbitrarily high-order error scaling with respect to the control cycle time of the underlying first-order sequence while maintaining robustness to finite pulse-width effects. The framework also enables the use of multi-product formulas for more efficient constructions. We apply the framework to several physically motivated quantum-simulation tasks and numerically verify the predicted error scalings.

</details>


### [114] [Cavity elimination in cavity-QED: a self-consistent input-output approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15508)
*Eliott Rambeau,Loïc Lanco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种自洽方法，用于在非绝热区域消除腔量子电动力学系统中的腔自由度，获得有效的两能级原子模型，捕捉非马尔可夫特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化复合开放量子系统对于实现其分析和数值理解至关重要。在腔量子电动力学中，当腔记忆时间与原子动力学时间尺度相当时（非绝热区域），需要一种有效的方法来消除腔自由度，从而获得简化的原子模型。

Method: 采用输入-输出形式主义，考虑一个二能级原子与单模腔耦合的系统，两个子系统通过任意数量的端口与环境相互作用。推导出约化原子动力学的自洽方程，获得有效的Purcell增强发射率表达式，并在合理近似下得到有效的两能级原子的自洽动力学方程和输入-输出关系。

Result: 得到的约化模型捕捉了非马尔可夫特征，表现为具有两个退相干速率（一个正、一个负）的有效Lindblad方程。在连续波激发区域，该方法计算的有效稳态和输出通量表达式超越了低功率激发区域，与完整的腔量子电动力学模拟表现出良好的一致性（除了强耦合、高激发区域）。

Conclusion: 该研究为减小腔量子电动力学模型的尺寸提供了一个实用框架，可推广到更复杂的原子和腔配置，为非绝热区域中的腔量子电动力学系统简化提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Simplifying composite open quantum systems through model reduction is central to enable their analytical and numerical understanding. In this work, we introduce a self-consistent approach to eliminate the cavity degrees of freedom of cavity quantum electrodynamics (CQED) devices in the non-adiabatic regime, where the cavity memory time is comparable with the timescales of the atom dynamics. To do so, we consider a CQED system consisting of a two-level atom coupled to a single-mode cavity, both subsystems interacting with the environment through an arbitrary number of ports, within the input-output formalism. A self-consistency equation is derived for the reduced atom dynamics. This allows retrieving an exact expression for the effective Purcell-enhanced emission rate and, under reasonable approximations, a set of self-consistent dynamical equations and input-output relations for the effective two level atom. The resulting reduced model captures non-Markovian features, characterized through an effective Lindblad equation exhibiting two decoherence rates, a positive and a negative one. In the continuous-wave excitation regime, we benchmark our approach by computing effective steady states and output flux expressions beyond the low-power excitation regime, for which a semi-classical treatment is usually applied. We also compute two-time correlations and spectral densities, showing an excellent agreement with full cavity quantum electrodynamics simulations, except in the strong-coupling, high-excitation regime. Our results provide a practical framework for reducing the size of CQED models, which could be generalized to more complex atom and cavity configurations.

</details>


### [115] [End-to-end performance of quantum-accelerated large-scale linear algebra workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15515)
*Daiwei Zhu,Miguel Angel Lopez-Ruiz,François-Henry Rouet,Claudio Girotto,Willie Aboumrad,Robert Lucas,Ananth Kaushik,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种混合量子-经典框架，通过将量子求解器集成到LS-DYNA多物理场仿真软件中，加速有限元分析中的图划分问题求解，从而减少大规模稀疏线性系统的填充效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏线性系统求解面临填充效应带来的计算挑战，图划分问题(GPP)自然出现在最小化填充和加速求解器的过程中。研究旨在探索量子计算在有限元分析中的实际应用潜力。

Method: 采用混合量子-经典框架，将基于Iterative-QAOA（可扩展的非变分量子优化方法）的量子求解器集成到Synopsys/Ansys的LS-DYNA软件中。针对振动分析和瞬态仿真两类FEA问题，在NVIDIA CUDA-Q/cuTensorNet上进行最多150量子位的数值模拟，并在IonQ Forte量子硬件上实现。

Result: 在包含高达3500万个元素的网格上进行端到端性能测量，使用LS-DYNA在分布式内存模式下通过MPI在AWS和Synopsys计算集群上运行。结果表明，引入量子计算机后，LS-DYNA的摊销挂钟时间在特定情况下可提高达15%，在所有考虑模型中至少提高7%。

Conclusion: 这些结果突显了量子计算在NISQ时代减少大规模FEA仿真求解时间的显著潜力，提供了一种可扩展且可扩展到容错量子计算体系的方法。

Abstract: Solving large-scale sparse linear systems is a challenging computational task due to the introduction of non-zero elements, or "fill-in." The Graph Partitioning Problem (GPP) arises naturally when minimizing fill-in and accelerating solvers. In this paper, we measure the end-to-end performance of a hybrid quantum-classical framework designed to accelerate Finite Element Analysis (FEA) by integrating a quantum solver for GPP into Synopsys/Ansys' LS-DYNA multiphysics simulation software. The quantum solver we use is based on Iterative-QAOA, a scalable, non-variational quantum approach for optimization. We focus on two specific classes of FEA problems, namely vibrational (eigenmode) analysis and transient simulation. We report numerical simulations on up to 150 qubits done on NVIDIA's CUDA-Q/cuTensorNet and implementation on IonQ's Forte quantum hardware. The potential impact on LS-DYNA workflows is quantified by measuring the wall-clock time-to-solution for complex problem instances, including vibrational analysis of large finite element models of a sedan car and a Rolls-Royce jet engine, as well as transient simulations of a drill and an impeller. We performed end-to-end performance measurements on meshes comprising up to 35 million elements.
