Measurements were conducted using LS-DYNA in distributed-memory mode via Message Passing Interface (MPI) on AWS and Synopsys compute clusters. Our findings indicate that with a quantum computer in the loop, amortized LS-DYNA wall-clock time can be improved by up to 15% for specific cases and by at least 7% for all models considered. These results highlight the significant potential of quantum computing to reduce time-to-solution for large-scale FEA simulations within the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, offering an approach that is scalable and extendable into the fault-tolerant quantum computing regime.

</details>


### [116] [Product Weyl-Heisenberg covariant MUBs and Maximizers of Magick](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15550)
*Bogdan S. Damski,Rafał Bistroń,Diego Ponterio,Jakub Czartowski,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究乘积希尔伯特空间中的离散结构，引入基于乘积群的magick概念，构造出生成等纠缠MUBs的基准态，并推广了Klappenecker-Rötteler构造


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复合希尔伯特空间中的离散结构，将单部系统的Weyl-Heisenberg群推广到乘积群，探索具有极值性质的基准态如何生成高度对称的量子设计

Method: 引入基于乘积群[WH(p)]^⊗n的magick概念，构造所有素数幂维度p^n（p≥3）中的基准态，其中p≥5时推广Klappenecker-Rötteler构造，p=3时基于伽罗瓦环构造

Result: 在p^n维度（p≥3）中显式构造了基准态，这些态生成d个先验等纠缠的MUBs，加上恒等算符构成完整集；d=2^3时magick的全局最大值对应Hoggar的SIC-POVM

Conclusion: 高度对称的量子设计可以通过具有极值性质的基准态通过结构化群轨道构造获得，为乘积希尔伯特空间中的离散结构提供了统一视角

Abstract: In this work we investigate discrete structures in product Hilbert spaces. For monopartite systems of size $d$ one relies on the Weyl-Heisenberg group $WH(d)$, while in the case of composite Hilbert spaces we identify designs covariant with respect to the product group, $[WH(p)]^{\otimes n}$. In analogy with magic -a quantity attaining its maximum for states fiducial with respect to $WH(d)$ -we introduce a similar notion of magick, defined with respect to the product group. The maximum of this quantity over all equimodular vectors yields fiducial states that generate $d$ $\textit{a priori}$ isoentangled mutually unbiased bases (MUBs), which, when supplemented by the identity, form their complete set. Such fiducial states are explicitly constructed in all prime-power dimensions $p^n$ with $p\ge 3$. The result for $p\ge 5$ extends the construction of Klappenecker and Rötteler, whereas for $p=3$ it is mathematically distinct and is based on Galois rings. The global maximum of magick for $d=2^3$ yields fiducial states corresponding to the symmetric informationally complete (SIC) generalized measurement of Hoggar. Our approach feeds into a unifying perspective in which highly symmetric quantum designs emerge from fiducial states with extremal properties via structured group-orbit constructions.

</details>


### [117] [Optimizing and Comparing Quantum Resources of Statistical Phase Estimation and Krylov Subspace Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15552)
*Oumarou Oumarou,Pauline J. Ollitrault,Stefano Polla,Christian Gogolin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个框架，用于直接比较两种早期容错计算本征能量的方法（QKSD和SPE），两种方法都使用哈密顿量的切比雪夫多项式期望值作为输入，改进了两种方法的性能并分析了其在分子模拟中的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要建立一个统一的框架来直接比较两种有前景的早期容错量子计算方法（QKSD和SPE），这两种方法都基于哈密顿量的切比雪夫多项式期望值，但缺乏系统的比较基准。通过改进两种方法的性能并分析其可扩展性，为实际量子化学计算提供指导。

Method: 开发了一个统一的比较框架，其中两种方法都使用哈密顿量的切比雪夫多项式期望值作为输入。对于QKSD，提出了优化测量次数分配和确保Krylov空间状态足够非线性的方法。对于SPE，改进了严格的误差界限，将电路深度减少了约2/3。利用MPS/DMRG计算最大切比雪夫阶数（与电路深度线性相关）和所需重复次数，分析了在54电子36轨道分子活性空间模拟中的可扩展性。

Result: 建立了统一的比较框架，使QKSD和SPE方法能够进行直接有意义的比较。QKSD方面实现了测量次数的最优分配和Krylov空间非线性的保证。SPE方面改进了误差界限，电路深度减少了约2/3。通过MPS/DMRG模拟显示，该方法能够处理多达54电子36轨道的分子活性空间，并提供了最大切比雪夫阶数和所需重复次数的具体分析。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一个统一的比较框架，改进了两种早期容错量子本征能量计算方法。通过优化QKSD的测量分配和SPE的误差界限，提高了两种方法的实际性能。利用MPS/DMRG进行的可扩展性分析表明，这些方法能够处理中等规模的量子化学问题，为未来容错量子计算在分子模拟中的应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We develop a framework that enables direct and meaningful comparison of two early fault-tolerant methods for the computation of eigenenergies, namely \gls{qksd} and \gls{spe}, within which both methods use expectation values of Chebyshev polynomials of the Hamiltonian as input. For \gls{qksd} we propose methods for optimally distributing shots and ensuring sufficient non-linearity of states spanning the Krylov space. For \gls{spe} we improve rigorous error-bounds, achieving roughly a factor $2/3$ reduction of circuit depth. We provide insights into the scalability of and the practical realization of these methods by computing the maximum Chebyshev degree, linearly related to circuit depth, and the respective number of repetitions required for the simulation of molecules with active spaces up to 54 electrons in 36 orbitals by leveraging \gls{mps}/\gls{dmrg}.

</details>


### [118] [Interaction-Enabled Hartree Fixed Points in Fermionic Resetting Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15554)
*Jishad Kumar,Achilleas Lazarides,Tapio Ala-Nissila*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了将重置动力学框架扩展到弱相互作用系统的受控方法，通过Hartree平均场处理密度-密度相互作用，构建了完全正的高斯Lindblad嵌入，揭示了相互作用特有的稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重置动力学为研究非平衡稳态提供了强大框架，但现有方法主要适用于无相互作用系统。需要开发能够处理弱相互作用的扩展方法，以研究相互作用在非平衡动力学中的作用。

Method: 1. 引入Hartree平均场处理密度-密度相互作用，保持单粒子密度矩阵动力学的闭合性；2. 构建完全正的高斯Lindblad嵌入，在无相互作用极限下重现重置动力学，在有相互作用时提供环境热化的连续时间表示；3. 通过环分割几何和最小二点模型的数值研究进行验证。

Result: 1. Hartree平均场方法产生了真正的非线性行为；2. 高斯Lindblad嵌入成功再现了重置动力学；3. 数值研究揭示了相互作用特有的稳态，这些稳态在纯二次型设置中无法获得；4. 建立了将弱相互作用纳入重置动力学框架的物理一致路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为将弱相互作用纳入基于重置动力学的开放量子系统方法提供了一条通用且物理一致的途径，扩展了重置动力学框架的应用范围，使其能够研究相互作用在非平衡动力学中的独特作用。

Abstract: In resetting dynamics, a system is repeatedly coupled to and decoupled from ancillary degrees of freedom that are reinitialized between interactions. This provides a versatile route to engineer nonequilibrium steady states and constitutes a powerful and analytically transparent framework for studying nonequilibrium dynamics in quadratic fermionic models. The baseline noninteracting resetting scheme yields an affine evolution for the subsystem single-particle density matrix (SPDM), with a clear operational interpretation: a finite environment block E mediates the interaction between the subsystem S and an ideal external thermal reservoir. In this work, we develop a controlled extension of such a framework to weakly interacting systems. We introduce a Hartree mean-field treatment of density-density interactions that preserves closure of the SPDM dynamics while producing genuinely nonlinear behavior. We further construct a completely positive (CP-safe) Gaussian Lindblad embedding that reproduces the resetting dynamics in the noninteracting limit and yields a continuous-time representation of environmental thermalization when interactions are present. Our analytical results are complemented by numerical studies of a ring segmentation geometry and a minimal two-site model, revealing interaction-enabled steady states that cannot be obtained in any purely quadratic setting. Together, these results establish a general and physically consistent route for incorporating weak interactions ino resetting-based approaches to open quantum system.

</details>


### [119] [Velocity-Enabled Quantum Computing with Neutral Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15561)
*Ohad Lib,Hendrik Timme,Maximilian Ammenwerth,Flavien Gyger,Renhao Tao,Shijia Sun,Immanuel Bloch,Johannes Zeiher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用原子速度作为新自由度来实现量子纠错的中性原子架构，通过控制多普勒效应实现了对移动原子的选择性操作，减少了硬件开销并提高了计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机面临的主要挑战包括原子穿梭的时间开销和复杂控制硬件带来的扩展性问题。传统架构中，原子需要被停止才能进行状态操作，这限制了量子纠错的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 引入原子速度作为新自由度，利用受控多普勒效应实现：1）速度选择性的中电路状态制备和测量；2）通过全局控制光束的空间相位映射实现飞行中的局域单量子比特旋转；3）结合保真度99.86%的CZ纠缠门。

Result: 实验实现了：1）八量子比特纠缠簇态，平均稳定子值为0.830(4)；2）[[4,2,2]]错误检测码，逻辑贝尔态保真度99.0(3)%；3）使用飞行辅助量子比特进行稳定子测量。这些构成了量子纠错和基于测量的量子计算的关键原语。

Conclusion: 速度启用的架构通过仅使用全局光束对连续移动原子进行选择性操作，减少了硬件开销，同时最小化了穿梭和传输延迟，为快速、大规模原子量子计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Realizing error-corrected logical qubits is a central goal for the current development of digital quantum computers. Neutral atoms offer the opportunity to coherently shuttle atoms for realizing efficient quantum error correction based on long-range connectivity and parallel atom transport. Nevertheless, time overheads in shuttling atoms and complex control hardware pose challenges to scaling current architectures. Here, we introduce atom velocity as a new degree of freedom in neutral-atom architectures tailored to quantum error correction. Through controlled Doppler shifts, we demonstrate velocity-selective mid-circuit state preparation and measurement on moving atoms, leaving spectator atoms unaffected. Furthermore, we achieve on-the-fly local single-qubit rotations by mapping micron-scale atom displacements to the spatial phase of global control beams. Complementing these techniques with CZ entangling gates with a fidelity of 99.86(4)%, we experimentally implement key primitives for quantum error correction and measurement-based quantum computing. We generate an eight-qubit entangled cluster state with an average stabilizer value of 0.830(4), realize an [[4,2,2]] error-detection code with 99.0(3) % logical Bell-state fidelity, and perform stabilizer measurements using a flying ancilla. By enabling selective operations on continuously moving atoms using only global beams, this velocity-enabled architecture reduces hardware overhead while minimizing shuttling and transfer delays, opening a new pathway for fast, large-scale atom-based quantum computation.

</details>


### [120] [A direct controlled-phase gate between microwave photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15587)
*Adrian Copetudo,Amon M. Kasper,Tanjung Krisnanda,Gregoire Veyrac,Shushen Qin,Hui Khoon Ng,Yvonne Y. Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在超导腔之间实现直接光子-光子相互作用的Raman辅助交叉Kerr方案，避免了非线性耦合器的激发，从而抑制退相干，实现了保相干的两振子纠缠门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息处理需要在大希尔伯特空间中操作，谐波振子提供了高维空间和硬件高效的玻色编码。然而，振子之间的直接纠缠操作仍然是一个挑战，现有方法通常需要激发非线性耦合器，这会引入额外耗散和编码流形泄漏。

Method: 设计了Raman辅助的交叉Kerr相互作用，在两个超导腔的微波光子之间产生直接耦合，而不激发非线性元件。利用这种动力学在单光子和双光子子空间内实现受控相位门，并将存储腔的光子数宇称映射到辅助振子而非非线性元件上。

Result: 实现了两个振子之间的直接纠缠，抑制了耦合器引起的退相干。成功执行了受控相位门，并能够将光子数宇称映射到辅助振子，实现错误检测同时保护存储模式免受测量诱导的退相干。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了玻色电路量子电动力学工具箱，实现了振子间保相干的直接光子-光子相互作用。这实现了完全在玻色编码空间内操作的纠缠门，同时抑制了非线性辅助激发的退相干，为容错玻色量子计算提供了关键原语。

Abstract: Useful quantum information processing ultimately requires operations over large Hilbert spaces, where logical information can be encoded efficiently and protected against noise. Harmonic oscillators naturally provide access to such high-dimensional spaces and enable hardware-efficient, error-correctable bosonic encodings. However, direct entangling operations between oscillators remains an outstanding challenge. Existing strategies typically rely on parametrically activating interactions that populate the excited states of an ancillary nonlinear element. This induces an effective interaction between the oscillators, at the expense of introducing additional dissipation channels and potential leakage from the encoded manifold. Here, we engineer a Raman-assisted cross-Kerr interaction between microwave photons hosted in two superconducting cavities, without exciting the nonlinear element, thereby suppressing coupler-induced decoherence.This approach generates a direct coupling between microwave photons that is exploited to implement a controlled-phase gate within the single- and two-photon subspaces of two oscillators, directly entangling them. Finally, we harness this dynamics to map the photon-number parity of a storage cavity onto an auxiliary oscillator rather than a nonlinear element, enabling error detection while protecting the storage mode from measurement-induced decoherence. Our work expands the bosonic circuit quantum electrodynamics (cQED) toolbox by enabling coherence-preserving direct photon-photon interactions between oscillators. This realizes an entangling gate that operates entirely within a bosonic code space while suppressing decoherence from nonlinear ancilla excitations, providing a key primitive for fault-tolerant bosonic quantum computing.

</details>


### [121] [Universal Weakly Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computation via Code Switching in the [[8,3,2]] Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15610)
*Shixin Wu,Dawei Zhong,Todd A. Brun,Daniel A. Lidar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于[[8,3,2]]码的容错代码切换协议，通过在不同编码版本间切换实现通用量子计算，规避了Eastin-Knill定理的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理限制了单个量子纠错码无法实现通用的横向门集合。代码切换提供了一种规避此限制的途径，通过在不同编码方案间切换来实现通用量子计算。该研究旨在开发一种具体的容错代码切换协议。

Method: 提出了一种容错代码切换协议，在[[8,3,2]]码的两个版本之间切换：一个版本支持弱容错单量子比特Clifford门，另一个版本支持通过横向T/T†门实现逻辑CCZ门，以及逻辑CZ、CNOT和SWAP门。由于两个码的距离均为2，协议在基于后选择的错误检测机制下运行。

Result: 单个故障会导致可检测的结果，被接受的运行表现出逻辑错误率的二次抑制。这为后选择容错计算提供了一个通用方案。通过状态准备、代码切换以及三逻辑量子比特Grover搜索的数值模拟验证了协议的有效性。

Conclusion: 该代码切换协议成功实现了基于后选择的通用容错量子计算方案，通过在不同编码版本间切换规避了Eastin-Knill定理的限制，为容错量子计算提供了一种可行路径。

Abstract: Code-switching offers a route to universal, fault-tolerant quantum computation by circumventing the limitation implied by the Eastin-Knill theorem against a universal transversal gate set within a single quantum code. Here, we present a fault-tolerant code-switching protocol between two versions of the $[[8, 3, 2]]$ code. One version supports weakly fault-tolerant single-qubit Clifford gates, while the other supports a logical $\overline{\mathrm{CCZ}}$ gate via transversal $T/T^\dagger$ together with logical $\overline{\mathrm{CZ}}$, $\overline{\mathrm{CNOT}}$, and $\overline{\mathrm{SWAP}}$ gates. Because both codes have distance 2, the protocol operates in a postselected, error-detecting regime: single faults lead to detectable outcomes, and accepted runs exhibit quadratic suppression of logical error rates. This yields a universal scheme for postselected fault-tolerant computation. We validate the protocol numerically through simulations of state preparation, code switching, and a three-logical-qubit implementation of Grover's search.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Speed fluctuations of a stochastic Huxley-Zel'dovich front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13458)
*Evgeniy Khain,Baruch Meerson,Pavel V. Sasorov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究随机Huxley-Zel'dovich反应-扩散波前在长时间尺度下的速度波动，包括典型波动和大偏差行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应-扩散波前的经验速度会因反应和扩散的内在散粒噪声而产生波动。本文旨在研究随机Huxley-Zel'dovich波前在长时间尺度下的速度波动特性，包括典型波动和大偏差行为。

Method: 采用一维晶格上的快速连续时间随机行走模型，粒子A进行可逆反应2A⇌3A。使用微扰理论分析波前扩散，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论预测，同时研究系统的最优历史路径。

Result: 微扰理论预测平均波前速度的系统偏移δc和波前扩散系数Df均按1/N标度，其中N是过渡区域的典型粒子数。蒙特卡洛模拟支持这些渐近结果，但发现前几个粒子在达到预期扩散渐近行为前存在长寿命异常行为。大偏差分析显示系统最优历史表现为以不同于平均速度传播的波前。

Conclusion: 随机Huxley-Zel'dovich波前在长时间尺度下表现出复杂的波动行为：典型波动可用波前扩散描述，大偏差由最优历史路径主导。虽然微扰理论成功预测了渐近标度行为，但模拟揭示了前几个粒子的异常动力学，表明系统存在更丰富的时空波动结构。

Abstract: The empirical speed of travelling reaction-diffusion fronts fluctuates due to the intrinsic shot noise of the reactions and diffusion. Here we study the long-time front speed fluctuations of a stochastic Huxley-Zel'dovich front. It involves a population of particles $A$ which perform a fast continuous-time random walk on a one-dimensional lattice and undergo reversible on-site reactions $2A \rightleftarrows 3A$. This front describes an invasion of $A$-particles into an initially empty region of space which, in a deterministic description, is marginally stable but nonlinearly unstable with a zero instability threshold. Typical fluctuations of this front can be described as front diffusion in a reference frame moving with the average front speed. According to the existing perturbation theory, the shot-noise-induced systematic shift of the average front speed, $δc$, and the front diffusion coefficient, $D_f$, are both expected to scale with $N$ as $1/N$, where $N \gg 1$ is the typical number of particles in the transition region. Furthermore, $D_f$ can be determined perturbatively in the small parameter $1/\sqrt{N}$. Our Monte Carlo simulations support these asymptotic results, but also reveal a long-lived anomalous behavior of the first few particles before they reach the expected diffusion asymptotic. We also study large deviations of the empirical speed of the front at long times. These are dominated by optimal histories of the system in the form of a propagating front which travels with a speed different from the average speed, or even travel in the wrong direction.

</details>


### [123] [Energy Dynamics and Partial Consumption in Foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13481)
*Md Aquib Molla,Sanchari Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究部分消耗食物对觅食者寿命的影响，考虑阈值能量水平和生存时间约束，发现寿命随k/S增加而增加，存在临界阈值k*，寿命呈现幂律行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在阈值能量限制下部分消耗食物对觅食者生存策略的影响。传统模型通常假设觅食者完全消耗食物，但实际中部分消耗可能更优，这能延长觅食者寿命并影响食物资源动态。

Method: 建立数学模型，觅食者生存时间为S步，阈值k限制食物消耗（仅当能量低于k时才进食）。采用部分消耗策略，食物站点可能未被完全耗尽。通过模拟分析寿命τ、重访次数、食物统计等关键量的标度行为。

Result: 1. 寿命τ随k/S增加而增加，存在临界阈值k*≈√S，低于k*时寿命快速增加，高于k*时增加缓慢；2. 寿命呈现幂律τ∼S^β，k/S=0时β=4/3，随k/S增加β跳升至2以上后逐渐降至1.84；3. 觅食者死亡后被完全或部分耗尽食物的站点数N_eat在k/S≈0.5时出现交叉行为。

Conclusion: 部分消耗食物策略能显著延长觅食者寿命，阈值能量水平对生存策略有重要影响。临界阈值k*的存在表明存在最优能量管理策略。幂律行为和标度关系揭示了系统的基本物理机制，为理解受限资源环境中的觅食行为提供了新见解。

Abstract: In this work, we consider partial consumption of food by a forager in presence of a threshold energy level. The forager considered here can survive for $S$ steps without food, namely the survival time. The threshold limits the consumption of food in such a way that, the forager will only consume food, whenever its energy is below the threshold $k$. Due to partial consumption of food, a site containing food may not always be fully depleted, which in turn helps in increasing the lifetime of the forager. It has been observed that, in our case, the lifetime always increases with $k/S$, although there is a transition threshold $k^*$ below which the increase of lifetime is rapid and above is low. The transition threshold $k^* \sim \sqrt{S}$. The lifetime $τ$ shows a power law behavior as $τ\sim S^β$. For $k/S=0$, the value of $β$ is $4/3$, it then jumps above $2$ and decreases gradually to $1.84$ with increasing $k/S$. Other important quantities like number of revisits to a site, food statistics etc. have been studied and these also show some interesting scaling behavior. The collection of sites either fully or partially depleted of food after the death of the forager $N_{eat}$ shows a crossover behaviour for $k/S \sim 0.5$.

</details>


### [124] [On thermalization in many-body classical Floquet systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13631)
*Anton Kapustin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类无限多经典多体Floquet系统会热化到无限温度，并指出局部可观测量周期性是热化的唯一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然物理直觉认为周期性驱动的封闭多体系统最终会热化到最大熵状态，但这比遍历性或混合性更强，难以数学证明。论文旨在为特定系统提供严格的热化证明。

Method: 研究代数起源的无限多经典多体Floquet系统，分析其动力学行为。证明对于足够均匀的局部可微哈密顿量的Gibbs态，长时间后会加热到无限温度。

Result: 证明了这类系统会热化到无限温度，且热化的唯一障碍是存在随时间周期变化的局部可观测量。这与物理直觉一致。

Conclusion: 为特定经典多体Floquet系统提供了严格的热化数学证明，确认了物理直觉：除非存在周期性局部可观测量，否则系统会热化到最大熵状态。

Abstract: It is expected that a generic closed many-body system prepared in a well-behaved initial state and subjected to a periodic drive will eventually thermalize, i.e. approach the state of maximal entropy. This property, while compatible with and even demanded by the physical intuition, is much stronger than ergodicity or mixing and is difficult to justify mathematically. We describe an infinite set of classical many-body Floquet systems of algebraic origin for which thermalization of very general initial states can be proved. For example, we show that a Gibbs state of any sufficiently uniform local differentiable Hamiltonian heats up to infinite temperature at long times. We show that in agreement with the physical intuition, the only obstruction to thermalization is the existence of local observables which are periodic in time.

</details>


### [125] [Information-Driven Phase Transition on Weighted Graphs with Spontaneous Dimensional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13896)
*Valerio Dolci*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 加权图上的信息流模型通过谱曲率演化拓扑，发现相变、泊松关系和维度敏感性三个关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加权图上信息流的动力学特性，特别是拓扑演化与信息传播之间的相互作用关系，探索自组织系统中的相变和维度效应。

Method: 提出FIU模型：基于图格林函数对角线定义谱曲率R，通过曲率相关耗散传播能量，并以耦合参数g控制高曲率节点间的长程连接形成速率。

Result: 1. 在g_c≈0.023处发现尖锐相变：低于g_c时局部信息流σ与结构形成负相关，高于g_c时强正相关(r≈0.75)；2. 发现节点级离散泊松关系∇²R(i)=κσ_prev(i)，κ参数稳定；3. 泊松关系表现出自发维度敏感性：2D中信号在N≳576时衰减，3D中持续到N≲1728。

Conclusion: 谱曲率R是自组织系统的核心变量，系统表现出相变、稳定的泊松关系和维度敏感性，这些特性与引力中的维度特征有相似之处，揭示了拓扑挫败在介观尺度上的作用。

Abstract: We study information flow on a weighted graph whose topology evolves according to a spectral curvature measure $\mathcal{R}$. The model (FIU) defines $\mathcal{R}$ from the diagonal of the graph Green function, propagates energy with curvature-dependent dissipation, and creates long-range links between high-$\mathcal{R}$ nodes at a rate controlled by a coupling parameter $g$.
  We report three results. First, the system exhibits a sharp phase transition at $g_c \approx 0.023$: below $g_c$, local information flux $σ$ and structure formation are anti-correlated; above $g_c$, they become strongly correlated (Pearson $r \approx 0.75$, $p < 10^{-38}$), with signatures of a continuous transition and mean-field exponent $ν\approx 0.54$.
  Second, we identify a node-level discrete Poisson relation $\nabla^2\mathcal{R}(i) = κ\,σ_{\rm prev}(i)$, where $κ$ is stable across parameters (CV $= 3.1\%$ across independent configurations). Mediator analysis reveals this correlation is almost entirely mediated by $\mathcal{R}$ itself, identifying it as the central self-organizing variable.
  Third, the Poisson relation exhibits spontaneous dimensional sensitivity: in 2D lattices both signals decay for $N \gtrsim 576$, while in 3D they persist to $N \lesssim 1728$. This emerges without any dimensional parameter in the rules. The collapse mechanism is curvature homogenization at large $N$. We interpret this as topological frustration in a mesoscopic regime, and discuss analogies with dimensional signatures of gravity.

</details>


### [126] [Channel transport: gating, geometry, and heterogeneous diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13918)
*Sean D Lawley*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了考虑随机门控、通道几何形状和异质扩散三个因素的通道扩散通量显式估计公式，该估计在某些参数范围内精确，并通过随机模拟验证了其广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通道介导的运输在生物学中普遍存在，已有理论研究探讨扩散通量如何依赖于随机门控、通道几何形状和异质扩散三个因素，但缺乏综合考虑这些因素的显式估计公式。

Method: 推导了考虑随机门控、通道几何形状和异质扩散的通道扩散通量显式估计公式，并在某些参数范围内证明其精确性，进一步通过随机模拟验证估计的准确性。

Result: 得到了一个综合考虑三个因素的扩散通量显式估计，该估计在某些参数范围内精确，随机模拟表明该估计在非常广泛的参数范围内保持准确，且与物理学文献中的某些结果存在差异。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个能够同时考虑随机门控、通道几何形状和异质扩散的通道扩散通量估计公式，该公式在某些条件下精确且在广泛参数范围内准确，为相关研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Channel-mediated transport is ubiquitous in biology. A series of works by different theoreticians have sought to determine how the diffusive flux through a channel depends on (a) stochastic gating, (b) channel geometry, and (c) heterogeneous diffusion. In this paper, we derive an explicit estimate for the diffusive flux through a channel that accounts for these three factors. We show that our estimate is exact in certain parameter regimes. We further use stochastic simulations to confirm that our estimate remains accurate across a very broad range of parameters. Our estimate differs from some results in the physics literature.

</details>


### [127] [Possibilities of applying boundary functionals of random processes to nuclear safety problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14058)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 评估随机风险过程边界泛函在核电站核安全问题中的应用潜力，特别关注中子行为显著变化的情况


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在熔盐堆、高温气冷堆、粉状燃料堆、反应堆启动和事故分析等特定情况下，中子行为发生显著变化，中子聚类开始起重要作用，中子行为的分布特征发生变化，需要新的数学工具来精确计算功率分位数

Method: 使用随机风险过程的边界泛函方法，该方法能够处理中子行为分布从正态分布向稳定极限分布的转变，为抽象定向渗流与工程保护设置计算之间建立数学桥梁

Result: 边界泛函方法能够精确计算功率分位数，为核安全问题的定量分析提供数学工具，特别是在中子聚类起重要作用的情况下

Conclusion: 随机风险过程的边界泛函在解决核电站特定情况下的核安全问题方面具有应用潜力，特别是在中子行为分布发生显著变化、需要精确计算功率分位数的场景中

Abstract: The potential for using boundary functionals of random risk processes to solve nuclear safety problems at nuclear power plants is assessed. In certain situations (MSRs (Molten Salt Reactors), High-Temperature Gas-Cooled Reactors (HTGRs), pulverized fuel reactors, reactor startups, and accident analysis (core collapse)), neutron behavior changes significantly. Neutron clustering begins to play an important role, and the distributions characterizing neutron behavior change. The normal distribution is replaced by stable, but also limiting, distributions. Boundary functionals allow for precise calculation of the power quantile and provide a mathematical bridge between abstract directed percolation and engineering calculations of protection settings.

</details>


### [128] [Intrinsic Error Thresholds in Nearly Critical Toric Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14098)
*Zack Weinstein,Samuel J. Garratt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究近临界拓扑量子码的信息保护，发现在临界点附近，即使量子涨落很强，仍需要有限强度的泡利退相干才能不可逆地破坏编码信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑量子码在接近量子相变临界点时的信息保护能力，探索在强量子涨落环境下编码信息的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用横向场环面码模型，通过副本统计物理映射分析相干信息，将退相干视为三维副本统计物理模型中的二维副本间缺陷。

Result: 退相干引入的缺陷在体临界点处是微扰无关的，不能重整化横向场强度，导致存在有限的错误阈值。

Conclusion: 近临界拓扑量子码在多种退相干通道下都能保持有限错误阈值，表明这类编码在接近量子相变时仍具有鲁棒的信息保护能力。

Abstract: We study the protection of information in nearly critical topological quantum codes, constructed by perturbing topological stabilizer codes towards continuous quantum phase transitions. Our focus is on the transverse-field toric code subjected to local Pauli decoherence. Despite the strong quantum fluctuations of anyons when the transverse field is tuned infinitesimally close to the critical point, we show that a finite strength of Pauli decoherence remains necessary to irreversibly destroy information encoded in the ground-state manifold. Using a replica statistical physics mapping for the coherent information, we show that decoherence can be understood as introducing a two-dimensional inter-replica defect within a three-dimensional replica statistical physics model. A field theoretical analysis shows that this defect is perturbatively irrelevant to the bulk critical point, and cannot renormalize the transverse field strength, leading to a finite error threshold. We argue that a qualitatively similar conclusion can be drawn for a broad class of nearly critical topological codes, under a variety of decoherence channels.

</details>


### [129] [Parity superselection obstructs monogamy of mutual information in free fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14154)
*Aleksandrs Sokolovs*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 自由费米子在自旋（张量积）因子化下违反互信息单调性，I₃ˢᵖⁱⁿ > 0；强排斥相互作用恢复单调性；使用I₃作为诊断工具时必须指定算子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由费米子在自旋因子化下是否违反互信息单调性，并量化相互作用费米子中因子化贡献与真实相互作用贡献的相对重要性，强调使用I₃作为诊断工具时算子代数规范的必要性。

Method: 1. 基于精确算子恒等式证明：费米子和自旋约化密度矩阵在不相交区域中相差宇称插入(-1)^{N_B}；2. 严格熵界分析；3. 在t-V链上进行DMRG计算量化相互作用费米子的效应。

Result: 1. 自由费米子在自旋因子化下违反互信息单调性：I₃ˢᵖⁱⁿ > 0（对于宽度w=1,2的三个相邻条带在所有费米动量下，以及所有w在z=k_Fw<z*≈1.329时）；2. 相互作用费米子中，因子化贡献对I₃表观K依赖性的贡献超过真实相互作用贡献8倍（中等填充时），占自旋基数值中观测偏差的~80%；3. 强排斥相互作用（K≲0.7）在两个代数中恢复单调性。

Conclusion: 使用I₃作为全息对偶、量子混沌或费米面拓扑的诊断工具时，必须明确指定算子代数；没有此规范，I₃的符号是模糊的。因子化贡献在相互作用系统中可能主导表观效应，强排斥相互作用可恢复单调性。

Abstract: We prove that free fermions in the spin (tensor product) factorization violate monogamy of mutual information: $I_3^{\mathrm{spin}} > 0$ for three adjacent strips of width $w = 1, 2$ at all Fermi momenta, and for all~$w$ at $z = k_F w < z^* \approx 1.329$. The proof rests on an exact operator identity -- the fermionic and spin reduced density matrices of disjoint regions differ by the parity insertion $(-1)^{N_B}$ in the partial trace -- and a rigorous entropy bound.
  DMRG calculations on the $t$-$V$ chain quantify the effect for interacting fermions: the factorization contribution to the apparent $K$-dependence of $I_3$ exceeds the genuine interaction contribution by a factor of~8 at moderate filling, and accounts for ${\sim}80\%$ of the deviation observed in spin-basis numerics. Strong repulsion ($K \lesssim 0.7$) restores monogamy in both algebras.
  These results imply that any use of $I_3$ as a diagnostic -- whether for holographic duality, quantum chaos, or Fermi surface topology -- must specify the operator algebra; without this specification, the sign of $I_3$ is ambiguous.

</details>


### [130] [Breakdown of Linear Response Induced by Velocity-Dependent Stochastic Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14164)
*Yuto Takeishi,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 线性响应理论在速度依赖重置的随机系统中失效，导致非线性输运现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性响应理论在非平衡系统中失效的机制，特别是探索速度依赖的随机重置如何导致非线性输运

Method: 采用受驱动的朗之万粒子模型，引入重置速率随粒子速度幂次增长的随机重置机制，推导稳态速度分布并建立矩平衡关系

Result: 建立了连接外部驱动、粘性耗散和重置诱导耗散的矩平衡关系，揭示了响应由速度和重置速率之间的非线性耦合控制，平均速度服从精确幂律关系⟨v⟩ ∝ F^{1/(α+1)}

Conclusion: 速度依赖重置是产生非线性输运的最小动力学机制，为线性响应理论在非平衡稳态中的失效提供了可解析的例子

Abstract: Linear response theory lies at the foundation of transport phenomena, predicting that physical systems respond proportionally to weak external forces. Here we show that this principle can break down in a minimal nonequilibrium setting due to state-dependent stochastic resetting. We consider a driven Langevin particle subject to a resetting mechanism whose rate grows as a power of the particle velocity, motivated by transport processes where faster carriers experience more frequent scattering events. We derive the exact steady-state velocity distribution and establish a moment balance relation that links external driving, viscous dissipation, and resetting-induced dissipation. This relation reveals that the response is controlled by a nonlinear coupling between the velocity and the resetting rate, leading to nonlinear transport. In particular, the mean velocity obeys the exact power law $\langle v\rangle \propto F^{1/(α+1)}$, where $α$ characterizes the velocity dependence of the resetting rate. Our results provide a solvable example in which linear response fails at the level of the leading-order behavior and identify velocity-dependent resetting as a minimal dynamical mechanism for generating nonlinear transport in nonequilibrium steady states.

</details>


### [131] [Discrete Time Crystal Order in Spin-Chains Enabled by Floquet Flat-Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14307)
*Mahbub Rahaman,Analabha Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种利用平带驱动在无杂质自旋链中实现离散时间晶体的新协议，通过全局自旋翻转和双音平带段工程实现完全简并Floquet准能谱，抑制热化并稳定周期倍增亚谐响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间晶体通常依赖无序诱导的多体局域化(MBL)或动态多体局域化(DMBL)来抑制热化。本文旨在探索在清洁、无杂质系统中实现时间晶体的新途径，利用平带工程创造简并能谱，为实验实现提供更灵活可控的方案。

Method: 提出一种新颖的驱动协议：每个驱动周期包含全局自旋翻转和双音平带段。平带段工程产生完全简并的Floquet准能谱，抑制热化。通过精确时间演化分析序参量的傅里叶谱，识别半驱动频率处的亚谐峰。研究系统尺寸、相互作用强度和范围的影响，分析自旋转动误差的敏感性。

Result: 1) 在序参量傅里叶谱中观察到显著的半驱动频率亚谐峰，为稳定DTC的出现提供了明确证据；2) 所得相位对系统尺寸、相互作用强度和范围不敏感；3) 对自旋转动误差(ε_r)敏感，但可通过引入额外自旋-自旋相互作用来抑制；4) 相比MBL和DMBL DTC，平带协议提供更宽的驱动参数可调性，而MBL/DMBL DTC对ε_r更具鲁棒性；5) 在有限的自旋耦合强度和驱动频率窗口内实现鲁棒的DTC响应。

Conclusion: 平带驱动为无杂质自旋系统中的DTC序提供了一种多功能且实验相关的实现途径。该协议具有广泛的参数可调性，虽然对自旋转动误差敏感，但可通过适当的相互作用修正来增强鲁棒性。这些结果推动了非平衡相在清洁系统中的进一步探索。

Abstract: We propose a novel protocol to realize discrete time-crystal (DTC) order in clean, periodically driven spin-$1/2$ chains. In each drive cycle, a global spin flip is followed by a two-tone flat-band segment. This flat-band segment engineers a fully degenerate Floquet quasienergy spectrum, suppresses thermalization, and stabilizes a robust period-doubled subharmonic response. Using exact time evolution, we identify a pronounced subharmonic peak at half the drive frequency in the Fourier spectrum of the order parameter, thereby providing clear evidence for the emergence of stable DTC. The resulting phase is insensitive to system size, interaction strength, and interaction range; however, it remains sensitive to spin-rotation errors ($\varepsilon_r$), which can destabilize the subharmonic response. Compared with disorder-induced many-body localized (MBL) and disorder-free dynamically many-body localized (DMBL) DTCs, we find that the exact flat-band protocol offers a broader tunability of drive parameters, whereas MBL and DMBL based DTCs are more resistant to $\varepsilon_r$. In particular, the $\varepsilon_r$ sensitivity can be suppressed by incorporating additional spin-spin interactions that have modest deviations from the ideal flat-band protocol. This manifests itself in a robust DTC response over a finite window of spin-coupling strengths and drive frequencies. Our results establish flat-band driving as a versatile and experimentally relevant route to DTC order in disorder-free spin systems and motivate further exploration of non-equilibrium phases.

</details>


### [132] [Nonholonomic constraints at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14424)
*Eduardo A. Jagla,Anthony M. Bloch,Alberto G. Rojo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 非完整约束系统与热浴耦合时，简单的朗之万方法预测可提取有用功而违反热力学第二定律，但通过将约束实现为粘性相互作用的极限情况并引入涨落-耗散关系，可恢复第二定律的遵守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非完整约束系统与热浴耦合时的行为，特别是Chaplygin雪橇这一范例系统。发现简单的朗之万方法会导致违反热力学第二定律的预测，需要更物理的约束实现方式来消除这一悖论。

Method: 1) 首先采用简单的朗之万方法，在运动方程中直接添加随机和耗散项；2) 然后将非完整约束实现为粘性相互作用的极限情况；3) 在有限温度下，根据涨落-耗散关系，在接触点补充随机力；4) 分析这些修正如何恢复热力学第二定律的遵守。

Result: 简单的朗之万方法预测存在可提取有用功的机制，违反热力学第二定律。但通过将非完整约束实现为粘性相互作用的极限，并引入涨落-耗散关系所需的随机力，系统恢复了对热力学第二定律的遵守。这揭示了理想化非完整约束在物理实现上的基本限制。

Conclusion: 非完整约束系统与热浴耦合时，必须考虑约束的物理实现方式。简单的朗之万近似会导致违反热力学第二定律的悖论，而通过将约束视为粘性相互作用的极限并引入涨落-耗散关系，可恢复热力学一致性。这为理想化非完整约束的物理可实现性设定了基本限制。

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of dynamical systems with nonholonomic constraints when coupled to a thermal bath, focusing on the paradigmatic case of the Chaplygin sleigh. A straightforward Langevin-type approach obtained by naively adding stochastic and dissipative terms to the equations of motion predicts a regime in which useful work can be extracted, violating the second law of thermodynamics. To resolve this paradox, we resort to a physically motivated implementation of the nonholonomic constraint as the limiting case of a viscous interaction. However, at finite temperature, fluctuation-dissipation relations imply that the viscous force has to be complemented with stochastic forces acting at the contact. We show that their incorporation restores compliance with the second law. Therefore, our results place fundamental limits on the physical realizability of idealized nonholonomic constraints.

</details>


### [133] [First-return time in fractional kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14487)
*M. Dahlenburg G. Pagnini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究分数动力学下随机游走者的首次返回时间，发现当跳跃大小分布对称时，首次返回时间密度与跳跃分布无关，仅受等待时间分布影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分数扩散（连续时间随机游走框架下）中随机游走者的首次返回时间特性，探索马尔可夫与非马尔可夫设置下，以及有限或无限方差对称跳跃分布对首次返回时间的影响。

Method: 采用连续时间随机游走框架，在非耦合公式下使用Mittag-Leffler分布等待时间。考虑马尔可夫和非马尔可夫设置，以及所有对称跳跃大小分布（有限或无限方差）。分析两种情形："先跳跃后等待"和"先等待后跳跃"。

Result: 当跳跃大小分布对称时，首次返回时间密度与跳跃分布无关，仅受等待时间分布影响。提供了马尔可夫与非马尔可夫设置结果之间的关系，以及两种情形（先跳跃后等待 vs 先等待后跳跃）之间的差异。

Conclusion: 在分数扩散过程中，对称跳跃分布不影响首次返回时间密度，该特性完全由等待时间分布决定，体现了过程记忆特性。研究结果为分数动力学中的首次返回时间提供了精确分析框架。

Abstract: The first-return time is the time that it takes a random walker to go back to the initial position for the first time. We study the first-return time when random walkers perform fractional kinetics, specifically fractional diffusion, that is modelled within the framework of the continuous-time random walk on homogeneous space in the uncoupled formulation with Mittag-Leffler distributed waiting-times. We consider both Markovian and non-Markovian settings, as well as any kind of symmetric jump-size distributions, namely with finite or infinite variance. We show that the first-return time density is indeed independent of the jump-size distribution when it is symmetric, and therefore it is affected only by the waiting-time distribution that embodies the memory of the process. We perform our analysis in two cases: first jump then wait and first wait then jump, and we provide several exact results, including the relation between results in the Markovian and non-Markovian settings and the difference between the two cases.

</details>


### [134] [Observation-Time-Induced Crossover in Driven Anomalous Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14685)
*Masahiro Shirataki,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究弱恒定力在强异质介质反常输运中如何通过涨落被检测，发现力的关键特征出现在粒子位移方差中而非平均漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在强异质介质中，弱外力如何通过反常输运的涨落特性被检测到，而不是通过传统的平均漂移方法。

Method: 研究两种代表性模型：带偏置的连续时间随机游走（CTRW）和带偏置的淬火陷阱模型（QTM），分析位移方差对力的依赖关系，量化涨落对弱驱动的响应。

Result: 对于α<2的情况，响应表现出观测时间诱导的交叉：在固定偏置下，方差最初遵循无偏标度，仅在较晚时间过渡到力主导的非平衡态。存在阈值偏置ε_c(t)分离表观平衡态和可检测的非平衡响应，该阈值随t增加而降低。淬火无序进一步降低检测阈值。

Conclusion: 弱力在强异质介质中的可检测性通过位移方差而非平均漂移体现，存在观测时间与系统本征弛豫时间竞争导致的交叉行为，淬火无序增强了弱力的可观测性。

Abstract: We investigate how a weak constant force becomes detectable through fluctuations in anomalous transport in strongly heterogeneous media. Rather than focusing on the mean drift, we show that the key signature of the force appears in the variance of the particle displacement. As representative models, we study a biased continuous-time random walk (CTRW) with nearest-neighbor jumps and a biased quenched trap model (QTM) with a power-law waiting-time tail. By analysing the force dependence of the displacement variance, we quantify how fluctuations respond to weak driving. We find that for $α<2$, the response exhibits an observation-time-induced crossover: at fixed bias, the variance initially follows its unbiased scaling and only at later times crosses over to a force-dominated nonequilibrium regime. Equivalently, at fixed observation time $t$, there exists a threshold bias $\varepsilon_c(t)$ separating an apparently equilibrium-like regime from a detectable nonequilibrium response. This threshold decreases with increasing $t$, implying that weaker forces become observable over longer measurement windows. Quenched disorder further lowers the detection threshold compared with CTRW, and the crossover reflects a competition between the finite observation time and the intrinsic relaxation time of the driven heterogeneous system.

</details>


### [135] [Scaling Autoregressive Models for Lattice Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14695)
*Xiaochen Du,Juno Nam,Sulin Liu,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出结合任意顺序自回归模型和边缘化模型的框架，用于高效学习晶体晶格上原子构型的统计分布，克服传统采样方法的收敛慢和临界减速问题，并显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料在真实条件下的行为预测需要理解晶体晶格上原子构型的统计分布，这对合金设计、催化和相变研究至关重要。传统马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样存在收敛慢和相变附近的临界减速问题，而现有自回归模型存在固定顺序生成、内存和训练成本高等限制。

Method: 开发了一个结合任意顺序自回归模型（any-order ARMs）和边缘化模型（MAMs）的框架。任意顺序ARMs通过条件化于任意已知晶格位点子集来灵活生成构型，MAMs通过单次前向传播近似任何部分构型的概率，显著降低内存需求。该框架支持具有晶格感知位置编码的Transformer架构，并允许在小晶格上训练的模型重用于更大系统的采样。

Result: 基于Transformer的任意顺序MAMs在二维Ising模型和CuAu合金上比基于多层感知机的ARMs获得更精确的自由能，准确捕捉相变和临界行为。框架从10×10扩展到20×20 Ising系统，从2×2×4扩展到4×4×8 CuAu超晶胞，相比传统采样方法降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过结合任意顺序ARMs和MAMs，克服了传统采样方法和现有自回归模型的局限性，实现了对晶体晶格原子构型统计分布的高效学习，为材料科学中的合金设计、催化和相变研究提供了可扩展的计算工具。

Abstract: Predicting how materials behave under realistic conditions requires understanding the statistical distribution of atomic configurations on crystal lattices, a problem central to alloy design, catalysis, and the study of phase transitions. Traditional Markov-chain Monte Carlo sampling suffers from slow convergence and critical slowing down near phase transitions, motivating the use of generative models that directly learn the thermodynamic distribution. Existing autoregressive models (ARMs), however, generate configurations in a fixed sequential order and incur high memory and training costs, limiting their applicability to realistic systems. Here, we develop a framework combining any-order ARMs, which generate configurations flexibly by conditioning on any known subset of lattice sites, with marginalization models (MAMs), which approximate the probability of any partial configuration in a single forward pass and substantially reduce memory requirements. This combination enables models trained on smaller lattices to be reused for sampling larger systems, while supporting expressive Transformer architectures with lattice-aware positional encodings at manageable computational cost. We demonstrate that Transformer-based any-order MAMs achieve more accurate free energies than multilayer perceptron-based ARMs on both the two-dimensional Ising model and CuAu alloys, faithfully capturing phase transitions and critical behavior. Overall, our framework scales from $10 \times 10$ to $20 \times 20$ Ising systems and from $2 \times 2 \times 4$ to $4 \times 4 \times 8$ CuAu supercells at reduced computational cost compared to conventional sampling methods.

</details>


### [136] [A Formal Physical Framework for the Origin of Life: Dissipation-Driven Selection of Evolving Replicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15230)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于非平衡系统统计物理学的生命起源形式化物理框架，揭示了耗散历史概率偏差如何导致遗传机制的选择，并证明了模板复制相比简单自催化的超指数优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生命从无生命物质起源的热力学挑战：热力学第二定律要求全局无序增加，而生命却形成高度有序的局部结构。需要建立形式化物理框架来解释这一矛盾。

Method: 从耗散与过程概率的已知联系（如Crooks涨落定理）出发，构建系统历史概率的大偏差框架。该形式化揭示了更大累积耗散历史的概率偏差。通过严格数学命题证明模板复制相比简单自催化的超指数优势。

Result: 形式化框架显示：简单自催化导致耗散指数增长，而模板定向复制通过突变和适应能力（推导出有效适应率α）解锁超指数增长路径。这转化为其出现相对概率的双重指数放大，构成渐近主导的物理选择偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架描绘了从简单耗散结构到信息承载复制子的层次转变，其稳定性取决于超过保真度、动力学效率和资源供应的临界阈值。提出了精确定量可证伪的实验方案，定义了识别合成化学系统中进化过程开始的数学特征。

Abstract: The emergence of life from inanimate matter presents a thermodynamic challenge: the Second Law of Thermodynamics dictates a global trend towards disorder, yet life constitutes localized pockets of profound organization. This paper presents a formal physical framework for abiogenesis grounded in the statistical physics of non-equilibrium systems. We transition from the established connection between dissipation and process probability (e.g., Crooks Fluctuation Theorem) to a large-deviation framework for the likelihood of system histories. This formalism reveals a probabilistic bias towards histories with greater integrated dissipation. We then demonstrate how this bias leads to the selection of heredity. The core of our argument is a rigorous mathematical proposition showing that while simple autocatalysis leads to an exponential increase in dissipation, template-directed replication, via its capacity for mutation and adaptation (a process from which we derive an effective adaptation rate, alpha), unlocks a super-exponential growth pathway. This translates to a doubly-exponential amplification in the relative probability of its emergence over time, constituting an asymptotically dominant physical bias for its selection. This framework delineates a hierarchical transition from simple dissipative structures to information-bearing replicators, whose stability is contingent upon exceeding critical thresholds of fidelity, kinetic efficiency, and resource supply. We conclude by proposing a refined, quantitative, and falsifiable experiment, defining a precise mathematical signature for identifying the onset of evolutionary processes in synthetic chemical systems.

</details>


### [137] [Exact and limit results for the CTRW in presence of drift and position dependent noise intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15426)
*Marco Bianucci,Mauro Bologna,Riccardo Mannella*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对具有漂移和位置相关跳跃的连续时间随机游走模型，推导了两个精确解析结果：n-时间相关函数的闭式表达式和精确的非局域主方程，并发现了一个普适的局域主方程近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间随机游走（CTRW）模型在描述自然和工程系统中的随机过程方面具有广泛应用，但现有理论通常需要扩散极限、分数阶标度假设或闭合假设等近似。本文旨在为具有漂移和位置相关跳跃的CTRW模型建立精确的解析框架，无需这些传统近似。

Method: 采用随机微分方程（SDE）框架，其中驱动噪声ξ(t)由尖峰（shot）事件组成。使用G-累积量形式推导了n-时间相关函数的闭式表达式（命题2），并利用G-累积量形式推导了CTRW变量x(t)概率密度函数的精确非局域主方程（命题3）。通过相互作用表象分析，发现长期极限下非局域主方程可被一个仅含瞬时更新率R(t)的局域主方程精确近似。

Result: 1. 推导出ξ(t)的n-时间相关函数的精确闭式表达式，表示为观测时间所有2^(n-1)个有序划分的和。2. 获得了CTRW变量x(t)概率密度函数的精确非局域主方程，无需传统近似。3. 发现了一个普适的局域主方程近似：在长时间极限下，精确的非局域主方程可被一个仅含瞬时更新率R(t)的局域主方程精确近似，该近似在R为常数时退化为精确的泊松主方程，数值实验证实了其即使在超出时间尺度分离假设的范围内也具有显著精度。

Conclusion: 本文为具有漂移和位置相关跳跃的CTRW模型建立了精确的解析理论框架，推导了两个核心解析结果，并发现了一个普适的局域主方程近似。这一近似在长时间极限下具有普遍适用性和高精度，为复杂随机过程的建模和分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Continuous-time random walks (CTRWs) with drift and position-dependent jumps provide a highly general framework for describing a wide range of natural and engineered systems. We analyze the stochastic differential equation (SDE) associated with this class of models, in which the driving noise $ξ(t)$ consists of spike (shot) events, and we derive two exact analytical results. First, we obtain a closed-form expression for the $n$-time correlation functions of $ξ(t)$, expressed as a sum over all $2^{\,n-1}$ ordered partitions of the observation times (Proposition~2). Second, using the $G$-cumulant formalism, we derive an \emph{exact} non-local master equation (ME) for the probability density function of the CTRW variable $x(t)$, valid without invoking diffusive limits, fractional scaling assumptions, or closure hypotheses (Proposition~3). In interaction representation, this ME retains the same structural form as that of the standard CTRW without drift or position-dependent jumps. Our main result is the emergence of a \textbf{universal local master equation}: at long times, the exact non-local ME is universally and accurately approximated by a time-local ME whose only coefficient is the instantaneous renewal rate $R(t)$. This approximation reproduces the exact Poissonian ME when $R$ is constant, and numerical experiments confirm its remarkable accuracy even far beyond regimes where a naive time-scale separation would justify it.

</details>


### [138] [Glass and jamming transitions in a random organization model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15519)
*Leonardo Galliano,Ludovic Berthier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟探索了非布朗悬浮液中吸收相变的二维离格粒子模型，发现了非平衡玻璃转变、协议依赖的临界吸收转变，以及类似Gardner相的复杂能量景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究驱动非布朗悬浮液中吸收相变的物理机制，理解随机组织模型中玻璃转变、阻塞转变和吸收转变之间的相互关系，探索非平衡微观动力学对宏观物理性质的影响。

Method: 使用二维离格粒子模型，通过控制堆积分数φ和跳跃幅度ε来探索相图；采用二元混合物抑制结晶；数值模拟分析非平衡玻璃转变、吸收转变和阻塞转变；研究协议依赖性和能量最小化方法；分析超均匀性在不同相中的特征。

Result: 在高堆积分数下，吸收转变前出现非平衡玻璃转变到非扩散非晶态；临界吸收转变位置具有协议依赖性；ε→0的阻塞转变位置随制备协议连续变化；在阻塞附近观察到类似Gardner相的复杂能量景观和边缘稳定性；临界指数与能量最小化方法和平均场复制理论预测一致；流体和玻璃相中的超均匀性具有不同特征。

Conclusion: 随机组织模型与经历玻璃和阻塞转变的热软粒子系统具有深刻的物理相似性，微观动力学的非平衡性质对涌现的物理性质影响很小，阻塞转变不能作为随机密堆积的唯一定义。

Abstract: We study a two-dimensional, off-lattice particle model introduced to describe absorbing phase transitions in driven non-Brownian suspensions. We numerically explore the $(φ,ε)$ phase diagram, where $φ$ is the packing fraction and $ε$ controls the amplitude of particle jumps. We use a binary mixture to suppress crystallization, which allows us to disentangle the model's distinct phase transitions between amorphous states. At large $φ$, we find that the approach to the absorbing transition is preceded by a non-equilibrium glass transition to a non-diffusive amorphous state. This dynamic arrest makes the location of the critical absorbing transitions protocol-dependent. The $ε\to 0$ end-point of the transition line defines a jamming transition whose location is shown to vary continuously with the preparation protocol, and cannot serve as a unique definition of random close packing. Near jamming, we observe a complex landscape and marginal stability, reminiscent of Gardner phases found in thermal glasses. The critical exponents characterizing packings at the jamming transition numerically agree with alternative approaches based on energy minimization, and with analytic predictions from mean-field replica theory. We analyze hyperuniformity in fluid and glass phases, where it emerges with qualitatively distinct signatures, and show that random organization dynamics does not determine the hyperuniformity observed in jammed packings, which is found to be non-universal. Our results show that random organization models share deep physical similarities with thermal soft-particle systems undergoing glass and jamming transitions, with little impact of the non-equilibrium nature of the microscopic dynamics on emerging physical properties.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Microscopic geometric theory for gapped excitations in fractional topological fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13489)
*Yuzhu Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种描述分数量子霍尔相中所有带隙激发的几何框架，揭示了Laughlin相中自旋2的类引力子激发与无理Haffnian共形场论中非阿贝尔准空穴之间的对偶关系，并构建了微观波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分数量子霍尔相中带隙激发的几何描述，揭示拓扑基态高阶几何形变产生的高自旋中性模式，为理解所有带隙激发提供具体的几何解释。

Method: 提出几何描述框架，构建Laughlin相中多个高自旋带隙中性模式的微观波函数，分析这些模式在Gaffnian共形希尔伯特空间中的渐近行为，研究高自旋模式之间的散射过程。

Result: 发现ν=1/3 Laughlin相中多个自旋2类引力子激发的希尔伯特空间与无理Haffnian共形场论的非阿贝尔准空穴之间存在对偶关系，证明了分数量子霍尔相的完整多体希尔伯特空间可以通过这类几何形变的叠加生成。

Conclusion: 分数量子霍尔相中的所有带隙激发都可以通过拓扑基态的几何形变来理解，高自旋中性模式可能具有较长寿命，并提出了实验探测方法，为拓扑相中的激发提供了统一的几何解释框架。

Abstract: We propose a geometric description of all gapped excitations in fractional quantum Hall phases that reveals several fundamental understandings with experimental consequences. These include a duality between the Hilbert space of multiple gapped ``graviton-like" spin 2 excitations at $ν=1/3$ Laughlin phase, and that of non-Abelian quasiholes of an irrational Haffnian conformal field theory. This leads us to construct microscopic wave functions for multiple higher-spin gapped neutral modes in the Laughlin phase. Carrying spin $s \ge 2$, they emerge from higher-order geometric deformations of the topological ground state, and live within the Gaffnian conformal Hilbert space asymptotically. We show that the full many-body Hilbert space of an FQH phase can be generated from superpositions of such geometric deformations, supporting a concrete geometric interpretation of all gapped excitations. We analyse the scattering between these higher spin modes and conjecture that they can have a long lifetime, and propose methods for their experimental detection.

</details>


### [140] [Effective band-projected description of interacting quasiperiodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13548)
*Flavio Riche,Raul Liquito,Bruno Amorim,Eduardo V. Castro,Pedro Ribeiro,Miguel Gonçalves*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了电子相互作用与准周期性在一维窄带系统中的相互作用，发现一阶带投影方法在准周期系统中失效，需要引入二阶带间贡献才能准确描述相关相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期系统中电子相互作用与准周期性的相互作用，特别是窄带系统中的基态和低能激发特性。传统的一阶带投影方法在平移不变平带系统中成功，但在准周期系统中可能失效，需要探索更有效的理论框架。

Method: 采用带投影作为低能有效方法，首先尝试一阶相互作用强度的投影，发现无法重现相关相图。然后通过微扰理论引入二阶带间贡献，构建有效的哈密顿量。使用密度矩阵重整化群计算验证结果，并进一步研究低能中性激发和光学电导率。

Result: 一阶带投影在准周期系统中失败，无法重现相关相图。引入二阶带间贡献后，得到的有效哈密顿量能够定量重现密度矩阵重整化群计算得到的精确相图，包括Luttinger液体与电荷密度波相之间的转变，以及在强准周期性下向准分形电荷密度波区域的交叉。还识别了区分不同相态的清晰动力学特征。

Conclusion: 二阶带投影是研究相关准周期窄带系统的可靠工具，为研究超越一维的相互作用准周期和莫尔材料提供了有前景的途径。强调了在准周期设置中涉及远程能带的虚拟过程的重要作用。

Abstract: We study the interplay between electronic interactions and quasiperiodicity in a one-dimensional narrow-band system, focusing on ground-state and low-energy excitation properties. Using band projection as low-energy effective approach, we show that a projection restricted to first order in the interaction strength fails to reproduce the correlated phase diagram. This contrasts with the standard success of first-order band projection in translationally invariant flatband systems and highlights the essential role of virtual processes involving remote bands in quasiperiodic settings. By incorporating second-order interband contributions perturbatively, we obtain an effective Hamiltonian that quantitatively reproduces the exact phase iagram previously obtained using density matrix renormalization group calculations, including the transition between a Luttinger liquid and a charge-density-wave phase and the crossover to a quasifractal charge-density-wave regime at strong quasiperiodicity. We further use this controlled framework to investigate low-energy neutral excitations and the optical conductivity, identifying clear dynamical signatures distinguishing the different phases. Our results establish second-order band projection as a reliable tool for correlated quasiperiodic narrow-band systems and suggest a promising route for studying interacting quasiperiodic and moiré materials beyond one dimension.

</details>


### [141] [Superradiant strongly correlated quantum states in cavity Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13657)
*Kang Wang,Wei-Xuan Chang,Cheng-Yu Bi,Zi Cai,Zi-Xiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 开发无符号问题的费米子-光子混合量子蒙特卡洛算法，研究二维腔哈伯德模型的基态相图，发现电子关联与光子凝聚相互作用产生超辐射反铁磁态、手性/π-通量态等新奇量子相，以及一阶超辐射相变和Gross-Neveu普适类的连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔量子材料中强关联电子与量子光的纠缠为探索传统固态材料中不存在的新奇量子相和相变提供了独特机会。需要开发有效计算方法来研究腔哈伯德模型等复杂系统的基态性质。

Method: 开发了一种无符号问题的费米子-光子混合量子蒙特卡洛算法，用于系统研究二维腔哈伯德模型的基态相图。该方法可以推广到更复杂的腔强关联模型。

Result: 电子关联与光子凝聚的相互作用产生了多种有趣的量子相，包括超辐射反铁磁态和手性/π-通量态。发现了不同类型的量子相变：一阶超辐射相变和具有Gross-Neveu普适类的连续相变。

Conclusion: 腔量子材料中电子-光子耦合系统展现出丰富的量子相和相变行为，开发的混合QMC算法为研究更复杂的腔强关联模型提供了有效工具，揭示了光-物质相互作用在强关联系统中的重要作用。

Abstract: In cavity quantum materials, entangling strongly correlated electrons with quantum light provides a unique opportunity to explore novel quantum phases and phase transitions absent in conventional solid-state materials. In this study, we develop a sign-problem-free fermion-photon hybrid Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) algorithm, and use it to systematically investigate the ground-state phase diagram of a two-dimensional cavity Hubbard model. It is shown that the interplay between the electron correlation and photon condensation gives rise to intriguing quantum phases ({\it e.g.} superradiant antiferromagnetic and chiral/$π$-flux states), and different quantum phase transitions, such as a first-order superradiant phase transition and a continuous phase transition with Gross-Neveu universality class. The methodology can be readily generalized to more complicated cavity strongly correlated models.

</details>


### [142] [Entanglement Rényi Negativity across the Finite-Temperature Transition in the O(3) Universality Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13711)
*Dong-Xu Liu,Yi-Ming Ding,Zhe Wang,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在三维海森堡反铁磁体的O(3)相变中，量子纠缠在临界点呈现纯面积律，热涨落完全破坏了零温下的长程纠缠，但纠缠的温度导数却编码了完整的普适标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限温度相变中量子纠缠的命运，特别是在连续对称性破缺的情况下，零温戈德斯通模式会产生长程关联，但热涨落对量子纠缠的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 使用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究三维海森堡反铁磁体中O(3)相变的三阶Rényi负性，这是首次对具有连续对称性的热临界点进行此类研究。

Result: 1. 负性在临界点呈现纯面积律，次领头常数项在统计不确定性内消失，表明热涨落完全破坏了零温下的长程纠缠，发散的相关长度对量子纠缠本身没有影响。
2. 尽管负性本身没有奇异行为，但其温度导数遵循比热的精确标度，得到临界指数-α/ν=0.190(1)和1/ν=1.350(5)，与O(3)普适类精确一致。

Conclusion: 量子纠缠对热临界性"视而不见"，但其热力学导数编码了完整的普适标度，揭示了纠缠与经典相变之间意想不到的联系。同时证明Rényi负性在具有连续对称性的系统中仍能有效屏蔽经典关联。

Abstract: The fate of quantum entanglement at finite-temperature phase transitions remains an open question, particularly for continuous symmetry breaking where zero-temperature Goldstone modes generate long-range correlations. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we investigate the third Rényi negativity across the O(3) transition in the three-dimensional Heisenberg antiferromagnet. The first such study for a thermal critical point with continuous symmetry. We uncover two fundamental results. First, the negativity exhibits a pure area law at the critical point, with the subleading constant term vanishing within statistical uncertainty. This demonstrates that thermal fluctuations completely destroy the long-range entanglement present at zero temperature. The divergent classical correlation length leaves no imprint on quantum entanglement itself. Second, despite this absence of singular behavior in the negativity, its temperature derivative follows the exact scaling of the specific heat, yielding critical exponents -α/ν=0.190(1) and 1/ν=1.350(5) in precise agreement with the O(3) universality class. Our work establishes that while quantum entanglement is blind to thermal criticality, its thermodynamic derivatives encode the full universal scaling, revealing an unexpected connection between entanglement and classical phase transitions. Furthermore, this also provides further evidence that Rényi negativity can still effectively shield classical correlations in systems with continuous symmetry.

</details>


### [143] [Pressure induced redistribution of oxygen hole states in La$_{4}$Ni$_{3}$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13808)
*Guiwen Jiang,Liang Si,George A. Sawatzky,Mi Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函计算和多轨道多原子团簇精确对角化，研究了三层La₄Ni₃O₁₀的局域低能电子态。发现在常压下，两个额外空穴分别形成Zhang-Rice单重态和与三自旋极化子杂化的态；高压下空穴重新分布形成不同电荷自旋有序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三层镍酸盐La₄Ni₃O₁₀的局域低能电子态，探索其在不同压力条件下的电荷和自旋有序行为，并与双层La₃Ni₂O₇体系进行对比，理解层状镍氧化物的电子结构特性。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算和多轨道多原子团簇精确对角化方法，构建最小Ni₃O₁₄团簇模型，包含局域交换和库仑相互作用，研究La₄Ni₃O₁₀的电子态。

Result: 常压下：两个额外空穴中一个在中心NiO₂层形成Zhang-Rice单重态（d_{x²-y²}轨道），另一个主要占据两个层间O p_z轨道的反键组合，并与d_{z²}轨道形成的三自旋极化子杂化；高压下：两个空穴分别集中在外层和中心层，形成不同的电荷自旋有序态。

Conclusion: La₄Ni₃O₁₀在不同压力下表现出不同的电荷和自旋有序态，其电子结构与双层La₃Ni₂O₇存在相似性，团簇计算结果为理解层状镍氧化物的电子行为提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Using density functional calculations and multi-orbital, multi-atom cluster exact diagonalization that includes local exchange and Coulomb interactions, we explored the local low-energy electronic states of trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ via a minimal Ni$_3$O$_{14}$ cluster. We find that, at ambient pressure, starting with all three Ni being nominally 2+ valence, one of the two extra holes is localized in the central NiO$_2$ layer forming a Zhang-Rice singlet (ZRS) with $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital. The other hole mainly occupies the antibonding combination of the two interplane O $p_z$ orbitals and thereby hybridizes with an out-of-plane three-spin-polaron (3SP) formed by the $d_{z^2}$ orbitals of three NiO$_2$ layers. In this way, the in-plane spin orientation alternation is carried by the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals of two outer layers with interlayer antiferromagnetic correlation. Simultaneously, the central layer is insulating like with negligible magnetic moment. At high pressure, however, the two extra holes are concentrated on one of two outer layers and the inner layer separately forming the ZRS with $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals or in-plane 3SP with neighboring cluster. We highlight the similarities between the bilayer La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ and trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ via possible charge and spin ordered states suggested by our cluster results.

</details>


### [144] [Crystal structure, magnetic and resonant properties of decorated spin kagome system (CsCl)Cu$_5$As$_2$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13865)
*Ilya V. Kornyakov,Marina V. Likholetova,Irina E. Lezova,Sergey V. Krivovichev,Harald O. Jeschke,Yasir Iqbal,Alexey V. Tkachev,Sergey V. Zhurenko,Andrey A. Gippius,Larisa V. Shvanskaya,Alexander N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 合成了averievite型砷酸盐(CsCl)Cu₅As₂O₁₀，研究了其晶体结构、相变和磁性，发现该化合物在室温以上发生结构相变，在21K进入倾斜反铁磁态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究averievite型砷酸盐(CsCl)Cu₅As₂O₁₀的晶体结构、相变行为和物理性质，特别是其磁性行为，并与V和P类似物进行比较。

Method: 通过合成样品，结合晶体结构分析、热力学测量、核磁共振(NMR)实验以及密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，系统研究了该化合物的结构和物理性质。

Result: 该化合物在室温以上发生从高温三方相(P-3m1)到低温单斜相(I2/a)的结构相变；在TN=21K时进入倾斜反铁磁态；DFT计算显示其kagome交换相互作用的能量尺度介于V和P类似物之间。

Conclusion: (CsCl)Cu₅As₂O₁₀表现出丰富的相变行为，包括结构相变和磁性相变，其kagome交换相互作用特征使其成为研究量子磁性材料的潜在候选体系。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and investigation of the crystal structure and physical properties of the averievite-like arsenate, $(\mathrm{CsCl})\mathrm{Cu}_5\mathrm{As}_2\mathrm{O}_{10}$. Just above room temperature, this compound undergoes a structural phase transition from the high-temperature trigonal $P\bar{3}m1$ $(a=b=a_0$ and $c=c_0)$ to the low-temperature monoclinic $I2/a$ phase $(a \approx \sqrt{3}a_0$, $b=a_0$, $c \approx 2c_0$ and $β\approx 90.6^\circ)$. According to thermodynamic and nuclear magnetic resonance measurements, it experiences a phase transition into a canted antiferromagnetic state at $T_N = 21~\mathrm{K}$. The density functional theory calculations place the energy scale of kagome exchange interaction parameter in $(\mathrm{CsCl})\mathrm{Cu}_5\mathrm{As}_2\mathrm{O}_{10}$ in between those in V- and P-analogs.

</details>


### [145] [Variance reduction for forces and pressure in variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14521)
*David Linteau,Saverio Moroni,Giuseppe Carleo,Markus Holzmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出简单实用的策略来降低蒙特卡洛估计量的方差，特别针对变分蒙特卡洛计算中的原子力和压力，方法也适用于更广泛的观测量和计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决变分蒙特卡洛计算中原子力和压力估计量方差过高的问题，特别是Pulay型贡献中的幂律发散和Hellmann-Feynman力中的方差控制。

Method: 1) 对于Pulay型贡献：基于Metropolis接受比进行微小修改，将方差幂律发散软化至对数发散；使用廉价正则化进一步抑制异常值。2) 对于Hellmann-Feynman力：推导紧凑的方差减少估计量，适用于周期性系统，易于在标准蒙特卡洛代码中实现。

Result: 方法在高压金属氢（包含超过一百个原子）的神经量子态描述中得到验证，包括在改进力驱动的分子动力学中的应用，展示了方差显著降低的效果。

Conclusion: 提出的方差减少策略简单实用，能有效改善变分蒙特卡洛计算中原子力和压力估计的统计精度，为大规模量子系统模拟提供了更可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: We present simple and practical strategies to reduce the variance of Monte Carlo estimators. Our focus is on variational Monte Carlo calculations of atomic forces and pressure in electronic systems, although we show that the underlying ideas apply more broadly to other observables, like pair-correlation and angular-distribution functions, and other methods, including molecular dynamics. For Pulay-type contributions, we show that a minor modification based on the Metropolis acceptance ratio softens the power-law divergence of the variance to a logarithmic one, and that inexpensive regularizations can further suppress outliers at the price of a controlled small bias. For Hellmann-Feynman forces, we derive compact variance-reduced estimators for periodic systems that are straightforward to implement in standard Monte Carlo codes. The approach is illustrated for high-pressure metallic hydrogen with more than a hundred atoms described by neural quantum states, including an application to molecular dynamics driven by the improved forces.

</details>


### [146] [Microwave spin resonance in epitaxial thin films of spin liquid candidate TbInO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14545)
*Sandesh S. Kalantre,Johanna Nordlander,Margaret A. Anderson,Julia A. Mundy,David Goldhaber-Gordon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用超导谐振器技术探测自旋液体候选材料TbInO3薄膜的磁激发，发现其磁序在极低温下仍保持高度阻挫，为理解该材料的量子自旋液体行为提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋液体（QSL）是量子阻挫系统中的奇异量子态，缺乏传统序参量使得实验识别困难。需要发展新测量技术来探测薄膜材料的磁激发，特别是对于体积过小无法应用传统体材料技术的样品。

Method: 采用共面超导谐振器技术，借鉴电路量子电动力学中的微波技术，测量外延生长的TbInO3薄膜的磁响应。通过自旋共振信号提取面内磁化率，并进行晶体场分析确定基态双态本征态。

Result: 测量显示TbInO3的磁序在低至20 mK的温度下仍保持极端阻挫，比居里-外斯能标低两个数量级。晶体场分析表明，由于非本征铁电性，Tb磁矩分裂为两种具有不同g因子的类型，反映了每个位点的局域晶体场环境。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了使用超导谐振器探测阻挫磁体薄膜的测量技术，并将该技术应用于构建对TbInO3磁性的连贯理解。自旋轨道耦合、晶体场、磁阻挫和非本征铁电性的独特组合共同塑造了TbInO3的磁性基态。

Abstract: Minimizing the energy of a many body system tends to favor order, but classical frustration and quantum fluctuations destabilize that order. The tension between these effects can produce exotic quantum states of matter. Quantum spin liquid (QSL) states emerge in models of localized magnetic moments where the crystal lattice connectivity frustrates ordering, and the exchange interaction of neighboring spins strengthens quantum fluctuations. Experimentally identifying a QSL in a real material is challenging from the lack of an order parameter. Piecing together evidence from varied techniques is necessary for diagnosing the nature of the ground state -- QSL or otherwise -- of a frustrated spin system. In this work, we use coplanar superconducting resonators to probe magnetic excitations in epitaxially grown thin films of a spin liquid candidate TbInO3. Adapting microwave techniques from the field of circuit quantum electrodynamics, we measure responses of these thin films whose volume is too low for applying conventional bulk techniques. In-plane susceptibility extracted from the spin resonance signal indicates extreme frustration of magnetic order down to 20 mK, over two orders of magnitude lower than the Curie-Weiss energy scale. Through a crystal field analysis, we identify the doublet eigenstates comprising the ground state. As a consequence of improper ferroelectricity, Tb moments split into two flavors with distinct g-factors reflecting the local crystal field environment of each site. Spin-orbit coupling, crystal fields, magnetic frustration and improper ferroelectricity distinctively combine to shape the magnetic ground state of TbInO3. This work establishes a measurement technique using superconducting resonators to probe thin films of frustrated magnets, and applies this technique towards building a coherent understanding of the magnetic properties of TbInO3.

</details>


### [147] [Synergistic doping and stabilization of magnetically tunable LnTi$_3$(Sb,Sn)$_4$ (Ln:Ce--Gd) kagome metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14571)
*Brenden R. Ortiz,Ramakanta Chapai,German Samolyuk,Milo Sprague,Arun K. Kumay,Hu Miao,Karolina Gornicka,Xiaoping Wang,Qiang Zhang,Madhab Neupane,David Parker,Jiaqiang Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: LnTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄系列可解理kagome金属的合成与表征，通过(Sb,Sn)协同掺杂稳定结构并调控磁性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LnTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄系列材料的结构稳定机制和磁性可调性，探索通过协同掺杂开发新型可调谐金属间化合物的途径

Method: 合成LnTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄系列材料，结合第一性原理密度泛函理论(DFT)和晶体轨道哈密顿布居(COHP)计算分析结构稳定性，重点表征SmTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄系列的磁性

Result: (Sb,Sn)合金化通过调整费米能级、填充成键态、减少反键态占据以及使态密度趋向局部最小值来稳定结构；SmTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄系列表现出多种可调的磁基态，其他稀土化合物也显示出类似的磁性可调性

Conclusion: (Sb,Sn)协同掺杂既能稳定LnTi₃(Sb,Sn)₄结构，又能调控其磁性，为开发具有化学、磁性和电子可调性的新型金属间化合物提供了潜在途径

Abstract: Here we present our synthesis and characterization of the LnTi$_3$(Sb,Sn)$_4$ (Ln: Ce, Pr, Nd, Sm, Gd) family of cleavable kagome metals. While these materials are isostructural to the LnTi$_3$Bi$_4$ family, they only form as (Sb,Sn) solid-solutions with no corresponding LnTi$_3$Sb$_4$ or LnTi$_3$Sn$_4$ phases. We use a combination of first-principles density functional theory (DFT) and Crystal Orbital Hamilton Population (COHP) calculations to show that (Sb,Sn) alloying has a stabilizing effect on the structure by adjusting the Fermi level, filling bonding states, depopulating antibonding states, and adjusting the density-of-states (DOS) towards local minima, an effect we call ``synergistic doping.'' The tunable Fermi level also has a profound effect on the magnetism, which we demonstrate through a detailed characterization of the SmTi$_3$(Sb,Sn)$_4$ series. The series hosts multiple magnetic ground states resulting from competing magnetic interactions that are tunable by the (Sb,Sn) ratio. While the focus of this work is on SmTi$_3$(Sb,Sn)$_4$, we briefly comment on the (Sb,Sn) solubility range and the conferred magnetic tunability in the other rare-earths compounds (Ln: Ce, Pr, Nd, Gd) as well. Our work demonstrates how the (Sb,Sn) synergistic pair can be used to stabilize the LnTi$_3$(Sb,Sn)$_4$ structure while simultaneously providing a means to tune the magnetism, ultimately providing a potential route to develop new intermetallics with chemical, magnetic, and electronic tunability.

</details>


### [148] [Evidence for ferroaxial order in 1T-TiSe$_2$ via elastoresistivity measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14613)
*Qianni Jiang,Ezra Day-Roberts,Benito Gonzalez,Awadhesh Das,Darius H. Torchinsky,Turan Birol,Rafael M. Fernandes,Ian R. Fisher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过弹阻测量探测1T-TiSe₂中的铁轴序，发现CDW态中的异常非对角线性弹阻响应，构建了铁轴序的有效共轭场，揭示了铁轴磁化率在临界温度附近的发散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁轴序作为一种隐藏序，其特征是打破镜像对称性但保持时间反演和空间反演对称性，在实验上极难探测。理解1T-TiSe₂中电荷密度波序的对称性长期存在争议，需要开发新的探测方法来研究隐藏序及其对称性。

Method: 采用弹阻测量技术研究1T-TiSe₂，通过测量电阻率对施加应变的响应来探测铁轴序。构建对称性破缺应变ε_{x²-y²}和ε_{xy}的适当组合作为铁轴序的有效共轭场，并扫描该有效场观察弹阻的滞后行为。同时进行详细的弹热测量以探测相变。

Result: 在CDW态中观察到异常的非对角线性弹阻响应，这为铁轴序提供了确凿证据。扫描有效共轭场时观察到弹阻的滞后行为，与铁轴畴壁运动相关。某些非线性弹阻系数在临界温度以上发散，与铁轴磁化率在T_CDW~200K附近的发散一致。弹热测量揭示了在T_CDW以下数十开尔文处存在额外的相变。

Conclusion: 该研究为1T-TiSe₂有序态的对称性提供了新见解，确立了弹阻测量作为探测隐藏序及其对称性的有力工具。研究结果表明铁轴序在1T-TiSe₂的CDW态中起关键作用，为理解复杂量子相中的隐藏序开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The study of spontaneous symmetry breaking and electronic order is fundamental in condensed matter physics. Hidden order, symmetry-breaking states that elude conventional probes, potentially plays a crucial role in understanding complex quantum phases in a wide range of materials. Ferroaxial order, a state characterized by broken mirror symmetries while maintaining time-reversal and inversion symmetries, is one of the hidden orders that have proven most challenging to detect experimentally. Here, we demonstrate a new approach for investigating both the ferroaxial order parameter and ferroaxial susceptibility using elastoresistivity measurements. We do this for 1T-TiSe$_{2}$, a material that exhibits charge density wave order that has eluded comprehensive understanding for a long time. These measurements reveal an anomalous off-diagonal linear elastoresistivity in the CDW state. We discuss why this provides a smoking gun for ferroaxial order. Furthermore, we construct an appropriate combination of the symmetry-breaking strains $ε_{x^2-y^2}$ and $ε_{xy}$ that acts as an effective conjugate field for the ferroaxial order, and demonstrate how sweeping this effective field in the CDW state results in a hysteretic behavior of the elastoresistivity, associated with the movement of ferroaxial domain walls. Finally, we reveal a divergence of certain nonlinear elastoresistivity coefficients above the critical temperature, and discuss how this is consistent with a divergence of the ferroaxial susceptibility near T$_{\rm{CDW}}$ $\sim$ 200K. Our study also includes detailed elastocaloric measurements, which reveal the presence of an additional phase transition several tens of Kelvin below T$_{\rm{CDW}}$. Our results provide new insight into the symmetry of the ordered state in 1T-TiSe$_2$ and establish elastoresistivity as a powerful probe of hidden order and its symmetry.

</details>


### [149] [Ferroaxial and nematic transitions in the charge density wave phase of 1T-TiSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14614)
*Sarah Edwards,Elliott Rosenberg,Ilaria Maccari,Jiaqin Wen,Chaowei Hu,Xiaodong Xu,Jong-Woo Kim,Philip J. Ryan,Rafael M. Fernandes,Fernando de Juan,Maria N. Gastiasoro,Jiun-Haw Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 1T-TiSe₂中的电荷密度波（CDW）不是手性的，而是铁轴性的，它打破了垂直镜面对称但保留了反演对称性，通过弹阻率和弹热测量证实了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于1T-TiSe₂中CDW对称性破缺的争议，特别是关于其是否具有手性特征（同时打破镜面和反演对称性）的矛盾实验结果。

Method: 使用对称性分辨的弹阻率测量检测自发出现的非对角弹阻率系数，这些系数满足反对称关系（m_{xx-yy,xy} ≈ -m_{xy,xx-yy}），同时进行弹热测量以确定相变温度。

Result: 发现CDW相具有铁轴序（打破垂直镜面对称但保留反演对称性），在CDW转变温度以下立即出现铁轴序，并在更低温度下观察到发散向列相敏感性，表明在铁轴CDW态内部存在额外的旋转对称性破缺不稳定性。

Conclusion: 1T-TiSe₂中提出的"手性"CDW实际上是中心对称的铁轴态，这调和了先前表面敏感观测与体对称性约束之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Charge density waves (CDWs) with multi-component order parameters can break unexpected symmetries through the interplay of nearly degenerate instabilities. In the widely investigated material 1T-TiSe$_2$, a central question is whether the observed CDW has a chiral character, which would manifest as the spontaneous breaking of mirror and inversion symmetries. Previous experiments have reported conflicting results about the broken symmetries in the CDW phase of 1T-TiSe$_2$. Here, we resolve this controversy by identifying the bulk broken symmetry as ferroaxial, corresponding to the breaking of vertical mirrors while preserving inversion symmetry. Using symmetry-resolved elastoresistivity, we detect the spontaneous emergence of intrinsic off-diagonal elastoresistivity coefficients that satisfy an antisymmetric relation ($m_{xx-yy,xy} \approx -m_{xy,xx-yy}$), providing an unambiguous bulk transport signature of a macroscopic electric toroidal moment. Simultaneous elastocaloric measurements reveal that the onset of ferroaxial order occurs just below the CDW transition. As the temperature is lowered further, a diverging nematic susceptibility signals a distinct rotational symmetry-breaking instability inside the ferroaxial CDW state. Our findings demonstrate that the proposed ``chiral'' CDW in 1T-TiSe$_2$ is actually a centrosymmetric ferroaxial state, reconciling previous surface-sensitive observations with bulk symmetry constraints.

</details>


### [150] [Giant anomalous Hall conductivity in frustrated magnet EuCo2Al9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14682)
*Sheng Xu,Jian-Feng Zhang,Shu-Xiang Li,Junfa Lin,Xiaobai Ma,Wenyun Yang,Jun-Jian Mi,Zheng Li,Tian-Hao Li,Yue-Yang Wu,Jiang Ma,Qian Tao,Wen-He Jiao,Xiaofeng Xu,Zengwei Zhu,Yuanfeng Xu,Hanjie Guo,Tian-Long Xia,Zhu-An Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在受挫Eu基磁体中发现了巨大的反常霍尔效应，反常霍尔电导率高达31000 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，反常霍尔角达12%，远超传统机制两个数量级，源于自旋手性涨落散射和间接RKKY相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究传导电子与局域磁矩之间的相互作用如何影响材料的电学和磁学性质，探索超越传统机制的反常霍尔效应新机制，为设计非常规自旋电子学系统提供理论基础。

Method: 结合磁输运测量、量子振荡实验、中子衍射技术和第一性原理计算，系统研究了Eu基受挫磁体的电输运性质、磁结构和电子结构，建立了反常霍尔效应与自旋手性涨落散射的关联。

Result: 发现了巨大的反常霍尔效应：反常霍尔电导率31000 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹，反常霍尔角12%；证实该效应源于Eu-4f磁矩的间接RKKY相互作用产生的自旋手性涨落散射；同时观察到Hund耦合引起的巨大交换劈裂和温度依赖的费米面重构。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了受挫磁体平台用于调控反常霍尔效应，阐明了交换相互作用和自旋织构在量子输运中的主导作用，为利用涌现自旋织构动力学设计非常规自旋电子学系统提供了框架。

Abstract: The interaction between conduction electrons and localized magnetic moments profoundly influences the electrical and magnetic properties of materials, giving rise to a variety of fascinating physical phenomena and quantum effects. Here, we discover a giant anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in a frustrated Eu-based magnet, exhibiting a giant anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) of 31000 Ω-1cm-1 and a remarkable anomalous Hall angle (AHA, tanθH) of 12 %--surpassing conventional mechanisms (either intrinsic or extrinsic) by two orders of magnitude. Combining magnetotransport, quantum oscillations, neutron diffraction and ab initio calculations, we establish that the giant AHC originates from fluctuating spin chirality skew scattering, generated by indirect Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interactions of Eu-4f moments. Simultaneously, Hund's coupling of itinerant electrons and localized Eu-4f spins triggers giant exchange splitting, evidenced by temperature-dependent Fermi surface reconstruction. This work establishes a frustrated magnetic platform for engineering the AHE and elucidates the governing role of exchange interactions and spin textures in quantum transport, while also providing a framework for designing unconventional spintronic systems that harness emergent spin-texture dynamics.

</details>


### [151] [Non-Abelian fractional Chern insulators from an exactly solvable two-body model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14722)
*Joseph R. Cruise,Alexander Seidel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 构建具有任意数量精确简并平带且能重现前p个朗道能级解析结构的晶格哈密顿量，为分数陈绝缘体提供精确可解的无阻挫父哈密顿量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在晶格系统中实现多朗道能级物理，为研究陈数C>1的分数陈绝缘体提供新框架，同时保持指数局域跳跃的晶格兼容性

Method: 构造一类晶格哈密顿量，其单粒子谱包含任意数量的精确简并平带，这些平带重现了前p个朗道能级在晶格上的解析结构；结合局域玻色接触相互作用，成为精确可解的无阻挫父哈密顿量

Result: 通过精确对角化确认了模型变体稳定玻色Jain-21态以及非阿贝尔22-和33-态的预期零模计数，这些态分别支持Ising型和Fibonacci型任意子

Conclusion: 该构造提供了多朗道能级物理的精确可解晶格实现，为研究C>1的分数陈绝缘体提供了新框架，同时提供了捕获连续朗道能级解析结构且保持指数局域跳跃的理想化晶格模型族

Abstract: We construct a class of lattice Hamiltonians whose single-particle spectrum consists of an arbitrary number of exactly degenerate flat bands that reproduce the analytic structure of the first $p$ Landau levels restricted to the lattice. When combined with local bosonic contact interactions, these models become exactly solvable frustration-free parent Hamiltonians for FCIs that realize both Abelian and non-Abelian parton quantum Hall states. Using exact diagonalization, we confirm the expected zero-mode counting for variants of the model stabilizing the bosonic Jain-21 state as well as the non-Abelian 22- and 33-states, which are expected to support Ising- and Fibonacci-type anyons, respectively. Our construction provides an exactly solvable lattice realization of multi Landau-level physics and offers a new framework for studying FCIs with Chern number $C > 1$. More broadly, it supplies a family of idealized lattice models that capture the analytic structure of continuum Landau levels while remaining compatible with exponentially local hopping.

</details>


### [152] [Study of the triangular-lattice Hubbard model with constrained-path quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14808)
*Shu Fay Ung,Ankit Mahajan,David R. Reichman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: CPMC方法在三角晶格Hubbard模型上的基准测试表明，对称性适应的试探波函数对定量精度至关重要，特别是在半填充强关联区域需要对称性投影


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估约束路径蒙特卡洛（CPMC）方法在三角晶格Hubbard模型上的性能，探索该方法在强关联、受挫系统中的实用性

Method: 使用CPMC方法对三角晶格Hubbard模型进行基准测试，比较不同试探波函数（自由电子基、对称性投影、对称破缺Hartree-Fock）的性能，并与精确对角化和密度矩阵重整化群结果对比

Result: 远离半填充时，保持基态对称性的简单自由电子基试探波函数能量偏差≲1%；半填充时，中等到大U区域需要对称性投影试探波函数才能达到可比精度，自由电子和对称破缺Hartree-Fock试探波函数存在显著约束偏差

Conclusion: 由于对称性投影CPMC的计算成本随系统尺寸多项式增长，该方法为研究强关联受挫系统中的竞争基态提供了实用途径

Abstract: We benchmark constrained-path Monte Carlo (CPMC) on the triangular-lattice Hubbard model for several fillings and $U$ values and show that symmetry-adapted trial wave functions are essential for quantitative accuracy. Away from half-filling, simple free-electron-based trials that preserve the ground state symmetry yield energy deviations $\lesssim 1\%$ from exact diagonalization and density matrix renormalization group results. At half-filling, strong frustration in the intermediate to large $U$ regimes necessitates symmetry-projected trials to reach comparable accuracy, where both free-electron and symmetry-broken Hartree-Fock trials incur substantial constraint bias. Since the computational cost of CPMC with symmetry projection scales polynomially with system size, our results motivate its use as a practical route for studying competing ground states in strongly correlated, frustrated systems.

</details>


### [153] [Lee-Yang Zeros and Pseudocritical Drift in J-Q Néel-VBS Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15116)
*Chunhao Guo,Zhe Wang,Danhe Wang,Zenan Liu,Haiyuan Zou,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过Lee-Yang零点分析J-Q模型的Néel-VBS量子相变，发现零点标度行为随系统尺寸漂移，表明存在扩展的伪临界区域，最终支持弱一级相变的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究J-Q模型中Néel反铁磁态与柱状价键固体之间的量子相变性质，特别是该相变是一级还是连续相变，以及是否存在伪临界行为。

Method: 采用随机级数展开量子蒙特卡洛方法，通过在复源场存在下重加权临界点附近的构型，计算Lee-Yang零点的标度行为。首先在二聚化海森堡模型和棋盘J-Q模型上进行基准测试，然后应用于J-Q模型分析。

Result: 1. 基准测试验证了方法有效性：二聚化海森堡模型显示稳定的O(3)临界标度，棋盘J-Q模型显示清晰的时空体积标度（一级相变特征）。2. J-Q模型中，主导零点的标度随系统尺寸增大出现显著系统性漂移，表明存在扩展的伪临界区域。3. SO(5)序参量场的有效标度维数随尺寸减小，在热力学极限下趋于零，这低于2+1维幺正共形场论的标量幺正界，强制零点呈现反比于时空体积的标度，这是一级相变的特征。

Conclusion: Lee-Yang零点分析支持J-Q模型中Néel-VBS相变是弱一级相变的解释，并建立了有限尺寸Lee-Yang零点作为J-Q家族中伪临界性和相变阶数的敏感、对称性分辨的诊断工具。

Abstract: Square-lattice J-Q models provide a sign-problem-free setting for probing the quantum phase transition between Néel antiferromagnet and columnar valence-bond solid. We analyze this transition through the scaling of Lee-Yang zeros, computed within stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo by reweighting configurations sampled near criticality in the presence of complex source fields. Benchmark studies of the dimerized Heisenberg model and the checkerboard J-Q model validate the method, yielding stable O(3) critical scaling in the former and clear spacetime-volume scaling in the latter, as expected for a first-order transition. Applying the same analysis to the J-Q models, we find a pronounced and systematic drift of the leading-zero scaling with increasing system size, consistent with an extended pseudocritical regime. The Lee-Yang scaling implies an effective scaling dimension of the SO(5) order-parameter field that decreases with size and is consistent with vanishing in the thermodynamic limit. Such behavior lies below the scalar unitarity bound of any unitary relativistic conformal field theory in 2+1 dimensions and enforces inverse spacetime-volume scaling of the zeros, the hallmark of a first-order transition. These results support a weakly first-order interpretation of the Néel-VBS transition and establish finite-size Lee-Yang zeros as a sensitive, symmetry-resolved diagnostic of pseudocriticality and transition order in the J-Q family.

</details>


### [154] [Nanoscale electronic variations in altermagnetic $α$-MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15225)
