*Zeyu Ma,Yidi Wang,Gal Tuvia,Kevin Hauser,Jiaqiang Yan,Jennifer E. Hoffman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: α-MnTe作为候选交变磁体存在显著纳米尺度电子不均匀性，通过STM/STS揭示其包含两种不同电子区域和电荷调制结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体兼具铁磁体的自旋分裂能带和反铁磁体的磁补偿特性，但候选材料如α-MnTe的体相特征（如反常霍尔响应）在不同样品间存在不一致性，表明不均匀性可能影响其在电子器件中的功能

Method: 使用低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）和扫描隧道谱（STS）技术，在原子级平整解理的单晶α-MnTe表面，对局域电子景观进行纳米尺度映射

Result: 识别出两种不同的电子区域：区域A的化学势位于价带边缘附近，在纳米尺度上变化约100 meV；区域B的化学势位于更宽带隙的中部。仅在区域A观察到周期为(2.5±0.1)a的非公度电荷调制

Conclusion: α-MnTe表现出显著的电子非均匀性，表明纳米尺度表征对其在电子应用中的可靠使用至关重要，这解释了先前体相测量结果的不一致性

Abstract: Altermagnets exhibit spin-split electronic bands like ferromagnets, yet they are magnetically compensated like collinear antiferromagnets. Altermagnets thus combine the benefits of ferromagnets and antiferromagnets, opening routes to tailored materials and applications. However, reported bulk signatures such as the anomalous Hall response in candidate altermagnets have been inconsistent across samples, suggesting that inhomogeneity may affect their functionality in electronic devices. Here, we use low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy to map the local electronic landscape of $α$-MnTe on atomically flat cleaved single crystals. We resolve two distinct electronic regions. In Region A, the chemical potential lies near the valence-band edge and varies by $\sim$100 meV on the nanometer length scale. In Region B, the chemical potential lies near the middle of a wider band gap. We further identify an incommensurate charge modulation with periodicity (2.5$\pm$0.1)$a$, observed exclusively in Region A. Our work establishes that $α$-MnTe can exhibit significant electronic non-uniformity, suggesting that nanoscale characterization is essential for its reliable use in electronic applications.

</details>


### [155] [Motivic GUT Part I: Grand Unified Theory of Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15361)
*Masahiko G. Yamada*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于Grothendieck-Kitaev-Lurie motivic yoga的拓扑相统一框架，将d维有隙拓扑序定义为幺正融合(∞,d)-范畴数据，并指出现有分类方案应视为本质∞-范畴对象的低范畴阴影实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有隙拓扑相建立统一框架，解决分类问题需要识别正确的高范畴环境空间，以展现其完整丰富性。采用Grothendieck的"上升海"精神，认为现有分类方案仅捕捉了更高范畴结构的低维阴影。

Method: 基于Grothendieck-Kitaev-Lurie motivic yoga，提出d维有隙拓扑序的统一定义：幺正融合(∞,d)-范畴数据，考虑Morita等价。对于d=2恢复幺正模张量范畴，d>2则自然导向真正的高范畴结构。

Result: 建立拓扑相理论的哥白尼式转向：许多现有分类方案应重新解释为本质∞-范畴对象的低范畴阴影实现。为d维拓扑序提供统一范畴定义，将传统模张量范畴方法推广到更高维度。

Conclusion: 拓扑序分类需要在高范畴环境中理解，提出的(∞,d)-范畴框架为统一处理各种拓扑相提供了理论基础，暗示现有低维分类是更高范畴结构的近似或限制情况。

Abstract: In the series of papers Motivic GUT Part I: Grand Unified Theory of Topological Order, Motivic GUT Part II: Grand Unified Theory of Symmetry-Protected Topological Order, and Motivic GUT Part III: Grand Unified Theory of Symmetry-Enriched Topological Order, we propose a unified framework for gapped topological phases based on the Grothendieck-Kitaev-Lurie motivic yoga. In the spirit of Grothendieck's rising sea, we argue that the classification problem can only be properly addressed after identifying the correct higher-categorical ambient space in which its full richness appears. In this first part, we propose a unified definition of gapped topological order in spatial dimension $d$ in terms of unitary fusion $(\infty,d)$-categorical data, considered up to Morita equivalence. For $d=2$, this framework recovers unitary modular tensor categories. For $d>2$, it naturally leads to genuinely higher-categorical structures. This suggests a Copernican turn in the theory of topological phases: many existing classification schemes should be reinterpreted as lower-categorical shadow realizations of intrinsically $\infty$-categorical objects.

</details>


### [156] [Single-Crystal Growth and Magnetic, Electronic Properties of the FCC Antiferromagnet Ba_2CoMoO_6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15485)
*A. R. N. Hanna,M. M. Ferreira-Carvalho,S. H. Chen,C. F. Chang,C. Y. Kuo,A. T. M. N. Islam,R. Feyerherm,L. H. Tjeng,B. Lake*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对双钙钛矿Ba₂CoMoO₆的结构、磁性和电子性质进行综合研究，发现其在20.1K以下呈现反铁磁有序，具有自旋翻转转变和J_eff=1/2有效基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有强自旋轨道耦合的面心立方晶格反铁磁体的物理性质，探索其在自旋电子学和能量转换应用中的技术潜力。

Method: 采用浮区法和提拉法生长单晶，通过X射线衍射、磁化率测量、热容测量、熵分析、X射线吸收光谱和表面光电压光谱等多种互补方法进行表征。

Result: 确认BCMO结晶为Fm3m空间群的面心立方结构；在20.1K以下呈现反铁磁有序，在2.65T处观察到自旋翻转转变；热容和熵分析支持Co²⁺离子的J_eff=1/2有效基态；XAS分析得到g=4.52的朗德因子；表面光电压光谱在2.65eV处显示强光学响应。

Conclusion: 该研究增进了对具有强自旋轨道耦合的面心立方晶格反铁磁体的理解，表明BCMO在自旋电子学和能量转换应用中具有技术前景。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive investigation of the structural, magnetic, and electronic properties of the double perovskite Ba$_2$CoMoO$_6$ (BCMO). Single crystals were grown via floating-zone and Czochralski techniques and characterized using a set of complementary methods. X-ray diffraction analysis confirmed that BCMO crystallizes in a face-centered cubic structure with space group $Fm\bar{3}m$. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal antiferromagnetic ordering below $T_\mathrm{N} = 20.1(1)$~K, with a spin-flop transition at $μ_0 H = 2.65$~T. Heat-capacity measurements and entropy analysis, $ΔS \approx 0.95\,R\ln 2$, are consistent with a $J_\mathrm{eff} = \tfrac{1}{2}$ effective ground state for Co$^{2+}$ ions. X-ray absorption spectroscopy at the Co~$L_{2,3}$ edges provides insight into the local electronic structure, revealing spin-orbit and crystal-field splitting effects; cluster-model calculations constrained by the XAS spectra yield a Landé $g$ factor $g = 4.52$, consistent with a spin-orbit-entangled $J_\mathrm{eff} = \tfrac{1}{2}$ ground state. Surface photovoltage spectroscopy reveals a strong optical response with a prominent feature at $2.65$~eV. These findings advance the understanding of face-centered cubic lattice antiferromagnets with strong spin-orbit coupling and suggest the technological promise of BCMO for spintronic and energy-conversion applications.

</details>


### [157] [Pseudogapped Fermi liquids from emergent quasiparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15560)
*Andreas Gleis,Gabriel Kotliar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一个在任意空间维度可精确求解的相互作用模型，该模型产生具有赝隙单粒子谱函数和半填充处准粒子权重为零的费米液体，不涉及莫特物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不依赖于莫特物理的赝隙费米液体机制，探索通过纯费米子机制产生赝隙的可能性，为理解强关联电子系统提供新视角。

Method: 构建可精确求解的相互作用模型，使用适当的相干态基推导配分函数的高斯路径积分表示，利用标准多体技术（如图形微扰理论或平均场理论）系统处理对高斯极限的偏离。

Result: 在正方晶格上明确展示了精确可解极限的多个性质：温度依赖的赝隙打开机制、自能的奇异行为、Luttinger求和规则的违反、Luttinger面和费米面的作用，并探索了赝隙费米液体与朗道费米液体之间的量子相变。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个不依赖莫特物理的赝隙费米液体模型，通过关联跳跃相互作用产生的涌现准粒子机制实现赝隙，为研究强关联系统中的赝隙现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose an interacting model that is exactly solvable in any spatial dimension and gives rise to a Fermi liquid (FL) featuring a pseudogapped (PG) single-particle spectral function and a vanishing quasiparticle (QP) weight at half-filling, without invoking Mott physics. The PG originates from a purely fermionic mechanism through emergent QPs arising from a correlated hopping interaction. By employing an appropriate coherent-state basis, we derive a Gaussian path-integral representation of the partition function, which enables systematic treatments of deviations from the Gaussian limit using standard many-body techniques, such as diagrammatic perturbation theory or mean-field theory. We explicitly demonstrate and discuss several properties of the exactly solvable limit on the square lattice, including the mechanism for temperature-dependent PG opening, the singular behavior of the self-energy, the violation of the Luttinger sum rule, and the role of Luttinger and Fermi surfaces. Finally, we explore quantum phase transitions between PG-FLs and Landau FLs.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Wilson loops with oppositely oriented plaquettes as a probe of center vortex structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13757)
*Ji-Chong Yang,Xiang-Ning Li,Zhan Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 研究具有非平凡取向结构的Wilson环作为中心涡旋图像的探针，发现垂直构型符合涡旋图像预期，而平行构型偏离简单面积律，但仍可在涡旋框架内解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Wilson环的取向结构如何作为中心涡旋图像的探针，验证涡旋图像对QCD禁闭的解释能力

Method: 在格点规范理论中构造包含两个相反取向plaquette的Wilson环，采用垂直和平行两种几何构型，并引入简单的定性涡旋模型进行解释

Result: 垂直构型行为与涡旋图像预期一致，而平行构型显示出无法仅由plaquette相反取向解释的面积律偏离，但通过涡旋模型仍可在涡旋框架内理解

Conclusion: 具有非平凡取向结构的Wilson环可作为中心涡旋的有效探针，平行构型的异常行为仍可在涡旋框架内解释，支持涡旋图像对QCD禁闭的描述

Abstract: We study Wilson loops with a nontrivial orientation structure in lattice gauge theory as a probe of the center vortex picture. The observable is a single Wilson loop containing two plaquettes with opposite orientations, realized in two geometries referred to as the vertical and parallel configurations. The vertical case behaves consistently with expectations from the vortex picture. In contrast, the parallel configuration shows a deviation from the naive area-law expectation which cannot be explained solely by the opposite orientations of the plaquettes. We introduce a simple qualitative vortex model which accounts for this behavior and shows that the observed effect can still be understood within the vortex framework.

</details>


### [159] [Electric Polarizability of Charged Pions from nHYP Four-Point Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15231)
*Benjamin Luke,Sudip Shiwakoti,Shayan Nadeem,Andrei Alexandru,Walter Wilcox,Frank X. Lee*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 使用改进的晶格QCD方法（包括动力学作用量、更小的π介子质量、可变晶格尺寸）计算带电π介子的电磁极化率，以进行无限体积外推


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子的电磁极化率可以获取其内部结构信息，传统方法使用外场两点函数，但最近研究表明四点函数方法对计算带电和中性强子的极化率更有效

Method: 采用改进的晶格QCD方法：1）使用动力学作用量（nHYP）；2）更小的π介子质量（220 MeV和315 MeV）；3）可变晶格尺寸以进行无限体积外推；相比之前研究（淬火Wilson作用量，π介子质量1100-370 MeV）有显著改进

Result: 论文呈现了初步结果，但未提供具体数值结果，表明研究仍在进行中

Conclusion: 通过改进的晶格QCD方法计算带电π介子的电磁极化率，为获取强子内部结构信息提供了更精确的计算框架，但需要进一步完成计算以获得最终结果

Abstract: Understanding a hadron's electric and magnetic polarizabilities allows one to access internal structural information. Traditionally, the external field two-point function method has been used to calculate polarizabilities. However, recent work has demonstrated the effectiveness of using four-point functions for computing polarizabilities of charged and neutral hadrons. Our previous study on the electric polarizability of the charged pion used a quenched Wilson action on a lattice with pion mass from 1100 MeV to 370 MeV. In this work, we employ a number of improvements, including a dynamical action (nHYP), smaller pion masses (220 MeV and 315 MeV), and a variable lattice size in order to extrapolate to infinite volume. Preliminary results are presented.

</details>


### [160] [Standard Model tests with smeared experiment and theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15487)
*Andreas Jüttner*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出在有限宽度涂抹下进行标准模型测试的方法，用于包含中间强子态贡献的过程，避免传统谱重构技术中需要无限小涂抹宽度的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱重构技术需要计算能量涂抹谱密度，并在连续和无限体积极限后通过涂抹宽度趋近于零来恢复物理振幅。然而，当前格点模拟难以实现足够小的涂抹宽度进行可控外推，这构成了显著挑战。

Method: 提出标准模型测试方法，其中实验数据和理论预测都在有限宽度下进行涂抹处理，类似于先前文献中在μ子(g-2)μ背景下对实验和格点R比数据的处理方式。

Result: 以包含介子衰变和稀有半轻介子衰变中的长程贡献作为具体示例，展示了该方法的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 通过有限宽度涂抹的标准模型测试方法，可以绕过传统谱重构技术中需要无限小涂抹宽度的限制，为包含中间强子态贡献的过程提供更可行的格点QCD预测途径。

Abstract: For Standard Model processes in which on-shell intermediate hadronic states contribute - including inclusive semileptonic decays and long-distance effects in rare exclusive decays such as $D\to π\ell\ell$ and $B\to K^{(\ast)}\ell\ell$ - spectral-reconstruction techniques provide a promising route to model-independent lattice QCD predictions for use in phenomenological predictions. The central ingredient is the computation of the energy-smeared spectral density. Following the continuum and infinite-volume limits, the physical amplitude is recovered as the limit of vanishing smearing width. However, achieving sufficiently small smearing for a controlled extrapolation remains a significant challenge for current lattice simulations. In this paper, we therefore propose Standard Model tests, in which both experimental results and theory predictions are smeared with finite width, similar to what has previously been done in the literature for experimental and lattice $R$-ratio data in the context of the muon $(g-2)_μ$. As concrete examples, we discuss the cases of inclusive meson decay and long-distance contributions to rare semileptonic meson decay.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Diffusion-based Generative Machine Learning Model for Predicting Crack Propagation in Aluminum Nitride at the Atomic Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13445)
*Jiali Lu,Shengfeng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发基于扩散的生成式机器学习模型，仅通过初始微结构嵌入预测氮化铝的原子尺度裂纹扩展，相比分子动力学模拟显著加速，并能准确预测动态断裂过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮化铝作为关键半导体材料，其原子尺度裂纹扩展预测对可靠性至关重要，但分子动力学模拟计算成本过高，需要开发更高效的预测方法。

Method: 开发基于扩散的生成式机器学习模型，仅以初始微结构嵌入为条件进行训练，使用分子动力学模拟的单裂纹系统数据进行训练，无需辅助应力或能量数据。

Result: 模型在保持物理保真度的同时实现显著加速，准确预测应力驱动裂纹萌生、裂纹分叉和原子尺度桥接韧带等动态断裂过程，并能泛化到未见的多裂纹配置。

Conclusion: 该模型能够在不依赖辅助数据的情况下捕捉复杂断裂物理，为半导体可靠性优化中的裂纹介导失效快速探索提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Predicting atomic-scale crack propagation in aluminum nitride (AlN) is critical for semiconductor reliability but remains prohibitively expensive via molecular dynamics (MD). We develop a diffusion-based generative machine learning model to predict atomic-scale crack propagation in AlN, a critical semiconductor material, by conditioning solely on initial microstructure embeddings. Trained on MD simulations of single-crack systems, the model achieves a significant speedup while accurately forecasting dynamic fracture processes, including stress-driven crack initiation, crack branching, and atomic-scale bridging ligaments. Crucially, it demonstrates inherent physical fidelity by reproducing material-intrinsic mechanisms while disregarding periodic boundary artifacts, and generalizes to unseen multi-crack configurations. Validation against MD ground truth confirms the capability of the model to capture complex fracture physics without auxiliary stress or energy data, enabling rapid exploration of crack-mediated failure for semiconductor reliability optimization.

</details>


### [162] [Accurate electronic and optical properties of bulk antiferromagnet CrSBr via a tuned hybrid density functional with on-site corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13494)
*Ashwin Ramasubramaniam,Daniel Hernangómez Pérez,Javier Junquera,María Camarasa-Gómez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于广义Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的替代方法，用于准确预测CrSBr的电子结构、光学吸收谱以及激子与磁序的耦合，相比传统自洽多体微扰理论更简单高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrSBr作为层状反铁磁体是研究低维光学与磁性强耦合的重要平台，但现有的从头算方法（自洽多体微扰理论）计算成本高、技术挑战大，需要更简单高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用基于广义Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的替代方法，通过最小化两参数集（可调至复现已知实验/理论基准，如基本带隙和光学带隙）来实现准确预测。

Result: 调优后的泛函展现出优异的预测能力，能够准确描述CrSBr的电子结构、光学吸收谱，并定量预测激子与磁序的耦合。

Conclusion: 该方法可广泛应用于其他磁性半导体材料，补充并简化当前对这些材料的建模方法，为研究低维磁性材料的光磁耦合提供更高效的计算工具。

Abstract: CrSBr, a layered antiferromagnet, is emerging as a versatile platform for exploring strong coupling between optical and magnetic properties in low dimensions. While experimental research on this material has progressed at a rapid pace, reliable results on the ab initio front are limited to the domain of self-consistent, many-body perturbation theory, which is both computationally expensive and technically challenging. We present an alternate, less-demanding approach -- rooted in generalized Kohn-Sham density functional theory -- that can deliver accurate electronic structure and optical absorption spectra of CrSBr, as well as quantitively accurate predictions of coupling of excitons to magnetic order in CrSBr. Using a minimal two-parameter set that can be tuned to reproduce a couple of well-known experimental and/or theoretical benchmarks, such as fundamental and optical gaps, we demonstrate excellent predictive capability for the tuned functional. The approach presented here can potentially be applied broadly to other magnetic semiconductors, complementing and simplifying current approaches to modeling these materials.

</details>


### [163] [Smoothed Boundary Method Framework for Electrochemical Simulation of Li-ion Battery Cathode with Complex Microstructure: Model, Formulation and Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13527)
*Hui-Chia Yu,Bernardo Orvananos,Scott Cronin,Martin Bazant,Scott Barnett,K. Thornton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于平滑边界法（SBM）的电化学模拟框架，用于在复杂电池电极微结构上建模电化学动力学，使用实验重建的3D微结构作为输入几何，解决了传统方法在微结构层面模拟的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可充电电池电极具有高度复杂的微结构，包括不均匀的电极颗粒、曲折的电解质通道和不规则的颗粒-电解质界面。电化学过程涉及多种耦合物理机制，这些几何和机制复杂性为使用传统方法在微结构层面进行电化学模拟带来了挑战性障碍。

Method: 引入平滑边界法（SBM）电化学模拟框架，将传统控制方程重新表述为SBM版本，并使用均匀笛卡尔网格求解。模拟利用基于图像的实验重建3D微结构作为输入几何，并使用实验测量的物理参数。研究了两种锂化机制模型：固溶和两相模型。

Result: 通过模拟揭示了复杂阴极微结构的详细动力学。两种模型的比较表明，使用菲克扩散建模两相锂化会高估电极性能。模拟框架为探索微结构层面的电化学动力学提供了创新途径。

Conclusion: 提出的SBM电化学模拟框架能够有效处理复杂电池电极微结构的几何和机制复杂性，为微结构层面的电化学动力学研究提供了创新工具，并揭示了不同锂化机制模型对性能评估的影响。

Abstract: Rechargeable battery electrodes have highly complex microstructures, consisting of nonuniform electrode particles, tortuous electrolyte channels, and irregular particle-electrolyte interfaces. Moreover, the electrochemical processes involve several coupled physical mechanisms, including mass transport in the electrode particles and electrolyte, current continuity in the solid and liquid, and electrochemical surface reactions. These geometric and mechanistic complexities create a challenging barrier of electrochemical simulations at the microstructural level using conventional methods. In this paper, we introduce a smoothed-boundary method (SBM) electrochemical simulation framework for modeling the electrochemical dynamics of complex battery electrode microstructures. The conventional governing equations are reformulated into SBM versions, and are solved using uniform Cartesian grids. The simulations utilize an image-based, experimentally reconstructed 3D microstructure as the input geometry, and the physical parameters acquired from experimental measurements. Two models of lithiation mechanisms, solid-solution and two-phase, are examined under potentiostatic discharging of a Li$_x$CoO$_2$ composite cathode. Detailed dynamics of the complex cathode microstructure are revealed through the simulations. The comparison between the two models indicates that modeling two-phase lithiation with Fick's diffusion will overestimate the electrode's performance. The presented simulation framework provides an innovative avenue in exploring the electrochemical dynamics at the microstructural level.

</details>


### [164] [Co2SeO3Cl2: Studies of Emerging Magnetoelectric Coupling in a Polar, Buckled Honeycomb Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13568)
*Faith O. Adeyemi,Xudong Huai,Mohamed Kandil,Pradip Karki,Wencan Jin,Thao T. Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在极性褶皱蜂窝磁体Co2SeO3Cl2中发现了四个磁性相变，磁熵恢复仅为理论值一半，二次谐波测量显示磁电耦合，表明极性褶皱蜂窝磁体为磁电材料提供了非常规相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发磁电材料需要化学设计策略，将结构极性与支持竞争性自旋相互作用的磁性晶格相结合。本研究旨在探索极性褶皱蜂窝磁体作为实现磁电耦合的新平台。

Method: 使用磁化率和热容测量研究Co2SeO3Cl2的磁性行为，通过二次谐波生成测量探测磁电耦合和晶体对称性变化。

Result: 在25.4K、16.8K、11K和3K观察到四个连续的磁性相变，磁熵恢复仅为预期值2Rln(2)的一半，表明存在持续的自旋涨落。二次谐波测量显示在11K、17K和26K出现强度异常，与磁性相变重合，但晶体对称性保持不变。

Conclusion: 极性褶皱蜂窝磁体为磁电材料中磁偶极子和电偶极子的耦合提供了非常规的相空间，是开发新型磁电材料的理想平台。

Abstract: The development of magnetoelectric materials requires chemical design strategies that integrate structural polarity with magnetic lattices capable of supporting competing spin interactions. Here, we demonstrate such an approach in the polar, buckled honeycomb magnet Co2SeO3Cl2. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements reveal strong magnetic anisotropy and four successive magnetic transitions at 25.4, 16.8, 11, and 3 K. The recovered magnetic entropy through the ordering regime is only around half of the expected 2Rln(2), indicating persistent spin fluctuations. Second-harmonic generation measurements show three pronounced intensity anomalies at 11, 17, and 26 K that coincide with magnetic transitions while revealing that the crystallographic symmetry is preserved. Together, these results demonstrate that polar, buckled honeycomb magnets offer an unconventional phase space for coupling magnetic and electric dipoles in magnetoelectric materials.

</details>


### [165] [Analyzing coherent phonon mode-conversion in gradient superlattices with atomistic wave-packet simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13572)
*Evan Wallace Doe,Theodore Maranets,Yan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过原子声子波包模拟研究梯度超晶格中相干声子的表现和传输特性，探索结构参数对相干模式转换的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度超晶格中相干声子的行为特性，探索如何通过调控长程无序性来定制超晶格结构的声子热导率

Method: 采用原子声子波包模拟方法，研究梯度超晶格中三个关键结构参数的影响：(1)不同周期尺寸的数量，(2)每种周期尺寸的周期数，(3)周期尺寸的排列方式（升序或降序），并与周期性超晶格和非周期性超晶格进行比较

Result: 梯度超晶格中的相干声子表现出介于完全有序和无序结构之间的中间态行为；短程有序性变化对传输影响不显著，而长程无序性对相干模式转换更为重要

Conclusion: 调控界面的长程无序性可能是定制超晶格结构声子热导率的有效策略

Abstract: In this study, we have used atomistic phonon wave-packet simulations to investigate the manifestation of coherent phonons and phonon transmission in gradient superlattices (SL) based on ordered arrangements of varied SL period sizes. We specifically explore how coherent mode-conversion in these quasi-periodic structures changes as function of three key structural parameters: (1) the number of distinct period sizes, (2) the number of periods present for each distinct period size, and (3) the arrangement of period sizes in either an ascending or descending arrangement. Comparisons to periodic SLs and aperiodic SLs are highlighted, revealing that coherent phonons in gradient SLs generally exhibit behaviors characteristic of intermediate states between the fully ordered and disordered structures. Interestingly, changes to the short-range order of GMLs does not significantly influence transmission, indicating that long-range disorder is far more important to coherent mode-conversion. Our results indicate that manipulating the long-range disorder of interfaces could be an effective strategy to tailor phonon thermal conductivity of SL architectures.

</details>


### [166] [Competing Magnetic Ground States in Copper-Doped Pb$_{10}$P$_{6}$O$_{25}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13650)
*Lin Hou,Kevin Allen,Christopher Lane,Jian-Xin Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 铜掺杂Pb10(PO4)6O的电子与磁性质研究：结合密度泛函理论和多体微扰理论，发现费米能级处的平半填充电子带导致非公度反铁磁不稳定性，铜原子间交换耦合约1meV，磁性局域于杂质铜位点，无长程有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究铜掺杂Pb10(PO4)6O（铅磷灰石）的电子和磁性质，探究铜作为磁性杂质在铅磷灰石基质中的行为，特别是磁性是否局域化以及是否存在长程磁有序。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）与多体微扰理论相结合的方法，通过随机相位近似（RPA）分析磁不稳定性，计算铜原子间的海森堡交换耦合强度。

Result: 费米能级处的平半填充电子带导致非公度反铁磁不稳定性，波矢为(0.28π,±0.47π,π)，主要源于Cu-dyz和Cu-dxz轨道；相邻铜原子间的海森堡交换耦合约为1meV；磁性局域于杂质铜位点，无长程磁有序。

Conclusion: 铜在铅磷灰石基质中表现为磁性杂质，磁性高度局域化，无长程磁有序，支持铜作为磁性杂质的图像。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic and magnetic properties of copper-doped Pb$_{10}$(PO$_4$)$_6$O using a combination of density functional theory and many-body perturbation theory. The flat half-filled electronic band at the Fermi level is found to give way to an incommensurate antiferromagnetic instability with wave vector $(0.28π,\pm 0.47π,π)$ within the random phase approximation arising predominantly from Cu-$d_{yz}$ and Cu-$d_{xz}$ orbitals. Moreover, the Heisenberg exchange coupling between neighboring copper atoms is estimated to be $\sim 1$ meV. Our results suggest that magnetism in copper-doped Pb$_{10}$(PO$_4$)$_6$O is localized on the impurity copper site, with no long-range ordering. These findings support the picture that copper behaves as a magnetic impurity within the Pb-apatite matrix.

</details>


### [167] [Cobalt Binary Compounds for Advanced Interconnect Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13713)
*Gyungho Maeng,Yeonghun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高通量筛选发现钴基二元化合物有望替代铜作为下一代互连材料，解决纳米尺度下铜电阻率急剧增加的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业标准铜互连在更细线宽下面临电阻率大幅增加的问题，限制了整体器件性能。钴因其在亚纳米尺度下具有增强可靠性和低电阻率的潜力而被视为有前景的替代材料。二元化合物的组合提供了超越元素金属限制、设计新特性的可能性。

Method: 采用高通量筛选方法，系统评估钴基二元化合物作为铜互连替代材料的潜在性能，重点关注其在减薄厚度下的电子传输特性和可靠性。

Result: 筛选出多种具有优异电子传输特性和可靠性的钴基二元化合物，这些材料在减薄厚度下表现出色，显示出克服缩放互连性能限制的显著潜力。

Conclusion: 特定钴基二元化合物具有显著潜力，能够克服缩放互连的性能限制，为下一代互连材料开发提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: The industrial standard copper (Cu) interconnects face a substantial resistivity increase at thinner linewidths, posing a well-known challenge to limit overall device performance. To address this issue, we have evaluated the potential properties of cobalt (Co) based binary compounds as replacements for Cu. Co is considered as a promising alternative due to its potential for enhanced reliability and low resistivity at sub-nanoscale dimensions. Furthermore, the combination of elements provides a possibility to engineer novel properties, transcending the limitations of elemental metals and expanding the search space for next-generation interconnects. In this study, a high-throughput screening method was used to identify several Co-based binary compounds with superior electronic transport and reliability at reduced thickness. The findings demonstrate that specific Co-based binary compounds hold significant potential to overcome the performance limitations of scaled interconnects.

</details>


### [168] [Giant Full-Space Anomalous Hall Effect Induced by Non-Coplanar Spin State in Mn-Rich Mn3Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13718)
*Yiming Liu,Xin Liu,Jiayao Zhu,Fengxian Ma,Li Ma,Dewei Zhao,Guoke Li,Congmian Zhen,Denglu Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Mn富集驱动Mn₃Sn从共面120°自旋构型转变为非共面态，通过四自旋环交换相互作用打破时间反演对称性，实现全三维空间的巨大反常霍尔电导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₃Sn作为非共线反铁磁体具有大反常霍尔效应，但其特定自旋构型禁止(0001)基面的反常霍尔电导，限制了实际应用。需要寻找实现全空间反常霍尔电导的新途径。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论计算，研究Mn富集对Mn₃Sn磁性的影响。分析Mn自掺杂（Mn₃.₁₂₅Sn₀.₈₇₅）引起的磁相变机制，重点关注四自旋环交换相互作用在驱动非共面态形成中的作用。

Result: Mn富集驱动Mn₃Sn从共面120°自旋构型转变为非共面态，自旋矩向c轴倾斜。四自旋环交换相互作用打破时间反演对称性，产生全三维空间的巨大本征反常霍尔电导。预测σ_(0001)高达~-468 Ω⁻¹·cm⁻¹，σ_(01-10)增强至~-229 Ω⁻¹·cm⁻¹。

Conclusion: 通过本征成分调控稳定非共面磁基态，可在反铁磁体中实现强全空间反常霍尔效应，为高性能、低功耗自旋电子器件提供了新途径。该方法不依赖外磁场或应变，具有内在优势。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets are promising candidates for next-generation spintronic devices owing to their negligible stray fields and ultrafast spin dynamics. The noncollinear antiferromagnet $\mathrm{Mn}_{3}\mathrm{Sn}$ exhibits a large anomalous Hall effect (AHE). However, its specific noncollinear spin configuration leads to the forbiddance of the anomalous Hall conductivity from the (0001) basal plane, $σ_{(0001)}$, limiting practical applications. Here, using first-principles density functional theory, we demonstrate that Mn enrichment in $\mathrm{Mn}_{3}\mathrm{Sn}$ drives a magnetic transition from the coplanar $120^\circ$ spin configuration to a non-coplanar state with moments tilted toward the $c$-axis. This transition is primarily mediated by four-spin ring exchange interaction in the local triangular lattice, which breaks the time-reversal symmetry and generates a giant intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity over the full three-dimensional space in $\mathrm{Mn}_{3}\mathrm{Sn}$. We predict that $σ_{(0001)}$ reaches as high as $\sim\!-468~Ω^{-1}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$, and an enhanced $σ_{(01\bar{1}0)}$ of $\sim\!-229~Ω^{-1}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$ is expected in light Mn self-doping of $\mathrm{Mn}_{3}\mathrm{Sn}$ ($\mathrm{Mn}_{3.125}\mathrm{Sn}_{0.875}$). Unlike previously reported mechanisms relying on external magnetic fields or strain, our approach exploits intrinsic compositional tuning to stabilize a non-coplanar magnetic ground state for realizing a strong full-space AHE in antiferromagnets, providing another viable pathway toward high-performance, low-power spintronic devices.

</details>


### [169] [Nitrogen-Vacancy-Mediated Magnetism in Sputtered GdN Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13817)
*Pankaj Bhardwaj,Jyotirmoy Sarkar,Bubun Biswal,Subhransu Kumar Negi,Arijit Sinha,Anirudh Venugopalrao,Sharath Kumar C,Sreelakshmi M Nair,R. S. Patel,Deepshika Jaiswal Nagar,Abhishek Mishra,Srinivasan Raghavan,Umesh Waghmare,Dhavala Suri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过直流溅射在SiO2/AlN衬底上制备了厚度18-180 nm的GdN薄膜，发现氮空位缺陷通过束缚磁极化子机制增强铁磁性，使居里温度从68 K提升至82 K，为自旋电子学应用提供了缺陷工程优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GdN因其半导体特性、强交换作用和软铁磁性成为自旋电子学有前景的材料，但需要低成本、均匀且成分可控的薄膜生长方法。研究旨在通过缺陷工程优化GdN薄膜的磁性能。

Method: 采用直流溅射在SiO2/AlN衬底上沉积厚度18-180 nm的GdN薄膜，在反应性氮化条件下生长。系统研究薄膜的结构和磁性能，结合理论分析建立拉曼模式与氮空位诱导磁化降低的直接关联。

Result: 薄膜表现出软铁磁有序，矫顽场约200 Oe，居里温度约70 K。结构分析显示晶格畸变和局部应变与氮空位缺陷相关。氮空位通过缺陷介导的铁磁性使Tc从68 K提升至82 K，磁行为可用束缚磁极化子模型描述。

Conclusion: 氮空位缺陷是GdN薄膜铁磁有序的关键贡献者，同时保持了其固有的软磁特性。缺陷工程在优化GdN薄膜自旋电子学应用中起着关键作用。

Abstract: Among rare-earth nitrides (RENs), gadolinium nitride (GdN) stands out as a promising material for spintronics owing to its distinctive combination of semiconducting behavior, strong exchange interactions, and intrinsically soft ferromagnetism. Its relatively high Curie temperature and large saturation magnetization make it an attractive candidate for device concepts such as non-volatile memory elements and spin-based transistors, motivating efforts toward low-cost, uniform, and compositionally controlled thin-film growth. In this work, we deposited GdN thin films on SiO2/AlN substrates using DC sputtering under reactive nitridation conditions, with thicknesses varying from 18 to 180 nm, and systematically investigated their structural and magnetic properties. The films exhibit soft ferromagnetic ordering, characterized by a coercive field of approximately 200 Oe and a Curie temperature (Tc) near 70 K. Structural analysis reveals lattice distortions and local strain associated with nitrogen-vacancy defects, whose concentration varies with film thickness. Our theoretical studies establish a direct correlation between the observed Raman modes of the GdN lattice and the reduced magnetization induced by nitrogen vacancies. These vacancies give rise to defect-mediated ferromagnetism, leading to a measurable enhancement of Tc from 68 K to 82 K across the studied thickness range. The observed magnetic behavior is well described by the bound magnetic polaron (BMP) model, confirming that nitrogen vacancies are key contributors to ferromagnetic ordering while preserving the soft-magnetic character intrinsic to GdN. This study underscores the pivotal role of defect engineering in optimizing GdN thin films for spintronics applications.

</details>


### [170] [Generative Inverse Design of Cold Metals for Low-Power Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13920)
*Kedeng Wu,Yucheng Zhu,Yan Chen,Bizhu Zhang,Shuyu Liu,Xiaobin Deng,Yabei Wu,Liangliang Zhu,Hang Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个基于MatterGPT的逆向设计工作流，用于生成三维冷金属材料，成功发现了257种相对于Materials Project数据库全新的冷金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷金属因其在费米能级附近具有本征能隙，可用于陡坡晶体管和低功耗电子应用。虽然高通量筛选已发现252种三维冷金属，但数据库搜索仅限于已知化合物，需要开发新方法来扩展冷金属的化学空间。

Method: 使用基于SLICES（可逆且对称不变的晶体字符串表示）训练的MatterGPT条件自回归Transformer进行逆向设计。训练集包含26,309个金属结构，标注了能量高于凸包和统一的带边距离描述符。通过针对热力学稳定性和50-500 meV带边距离的条件生成，产生148,506个候选结构，经过重建、对称性筛选、唯一性和新颖性过滤，最后进行高通量DFT验证。

Result: 成功生成148,506个独特候选结构，92.1%成功重建为三维结构。通过DFT验证发现了257种相对于Materials Project数据库全新的冷金属，其能隙在50-500 meV范围内。代表性候选材料的声子、电子结构和功函数计算确认了其动力学稳定性和接触相关的功函数特性。

Conclusion: 基于SLICES的生成式Transformer能够超越高通量筛选的限制，扩展冷金属的化学空间，为低功耗电子材料发现提供了新途径。

Abstract: Cold metals are a class of metals with an intrinsic energy gap located close to the Fermi level, which enables cold-carrier injection for steep-slope transistors and is therefore promising for low-power electronic applications. High-throughput screening has revealed 252 three-dimensional (3D) cold metals in the Materials Project database, but database searches are inherently limited to known compounds. Here we present an inverse-design workflow that generates 3D cold metals using MatterGPT, a conditional autoregressive Transformer trained on SLICES, an invertible and symmetry-invariant crystal string representation. We curate a training set of 26,309 metallic structures labeled with energy above hull and a unified band-edge distance descriptor that merges p-type and n-type cold-metal characteristics to address severe label imbalance. Property-conditioned generation targeting thermodynamic stability and 50-500 meV band-edge distances produces 148,506 unique candidates; 92.1% are successfully reconstructed to 3D structures and down-selected by symmetry, uniqueness and novelty filters, followed by high-throughput DFT validation. We identify 257 cold metals verified as novel with respect to the Materials Project database, with gaps around the Fermi level spanning 50-500 meV. First-principles phonon, electronic-structure, and work-function calculations for representative candidates confirm dynamical stability and contact-relevant work functions. Our results demonstrate that SLICES-enabled generative transformers can expand the chemical space of cold metals beyond high-throughput screening, providing a route to low-power electronic materials discovery.

</details>


### [171] [Non-isothermal flow of Al-, Co- and Cu-based alloys made in different spatial configurations or structural states: model and experimental study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14000)
*A. D. Berezner,V. A. Fedorov,N. S. Perov,J. C. Qiao,V. E. Gromov,M. Yu. Zadorozhnyy,G. V. Grigoriev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通用的非等温行为建模方法，结合新型变形模型，用于不同合金材料，并验证了模型与实验结果的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种通用的建模方法来预测不同合金在非等温条件下的变形行为，特别是针对颈缩、起皱等塑性变形现象，以更好地理解材料在热-力耦合作用下的响应。

Method: 提出了通用的非等温行为建模方法，结合新型变形模型；通过实验验证模型准确性；计算了带材和棒材的颈缩轮廓和临界起皱厚度；对多晶和非晶带材的起皱褶皱进行了分形分析；使用显微镜研究塑性变形阶段的结构特征。

Result: 模型与实验结果显示出强相关性，能够准确估计线性热膨胀系数等关键参数；成功计算了颈缩轮廓和临界起皱厚度；分形分析揭示了起皱褶皱的尺寸特征；显微镜观察揭示了塑性变形阶段的结构变化。

Conclusion: 提出的通用建模方法能够有效预测不同合金的非等温变形行为，为材料在热-力耦合条件下的性能评估提供了可靠的理论工具，特别是在颈缩和起皱分析方面具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: The universal generalising approach for non-isothermal behaviour of different alloys has been provided together with the novel deformation modelling. Strong correlation between the model approach and experimental results is shown that permits estimation of main applied parameters such as the linear thermal expansion coefficient and others. Necking contours and critical thickness at corrugation for ribbon and rod specimens are also calculated. Fractal analysis of corrugation folds (their main size) has been carried out for polycrystalline and amorphous ribbon specimens. Structural peculiarities at the plastic deformation stage are investigated with microscopy.

</details>


### [172] [Linear dichroic soft X-ray microscopy of ferroelectric stripe domains in epitaxial K$_\mathbf{0.6}$Na$_\mathbf{0.4}$NbO$_\mathbf{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14079)
*M. Schneider,T. A. Butcher,S. Wagner,D. Metternich,C. Klose,E. Malm,R. Battistelli,V. Deinhart,J. Fuchs,S. Wittrock,T. Karaman,K. Puzhekadavil Joy,M. Patras,F. Büttner,S. Wintz,M. Weigand,C. M. Günther,D. Engel,P. Gaal,J. Schwarzkopf,B. Pfau,S. Eisebitt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文展示了如何通过局部背减薄TbScO₃衬底实现软X射线透明，从而利用软X射线显微镜成像应变稳定的铁电薄膜纳米畴结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软X射线显微镜具有元素和电子敏感性，可用于成像铁电畴结构，但以往受限于软X射线在衬底中的吸收，无法应用于应变稳定的铁电薄膜。

Method: 通过局部背减薄(110) TbScO₃衬底，使K₀.₆Na₀.₄NbO₃铁电薄膜在O K-edge（约530 eV）处实现软X射线透明，利用扫描透射X射线显微镜和相干衍射成像，基于O 2p-Nb 4d杂化态的X射线线性二色性来解析畴结构。

Result: 成功分辨出周期低至44 nm的应变诱导铁电条纹畴，实现了对平面内极化分量的敏感性，建立了软X射线显微镜用于外延铁电畴纳米尺度成像的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法克服了软X射线在衬底中吸收的限制，为外延铁电畴结构的纳米尺度成像开辟了新途径，并为时间分辨研究提供了前景。

Abstract: Functional properties of ferroelectric thin films are governed by domains that can be engineered by epitaxial strain. Soft X-ray microscopy can image domain structures with elemental and electronic sensitivity, but hitherto its application to strain-stabilized domains has been hindered by the absorption of soft X-rays in epitaxial substrates. Here, it is demonstrated how this limitation can be overcome by locally back-thinning the (110) TbScO$_3$ substrate of epitaxial K$_{0.6}$Na$_{0.4}$NbO$_3$ ferroelectric thin films to achieve soft X-ray transparency at the O K-edge around 530 eV. Strain-induced ferroelectric stripe domains with periods down to 44 nm were resolved by scanning transmission X-ray microscopy and coherent diffractive imaging by exploiting the X-ray linear dichroism of hybridized O 2p-Nb 4d states, providing sensitivity to in-plane polarization components under normal incidence. The results establish soft X-ray microscopy for nanoscale imaging of epitaxial ferroelectric domains structures and open perspectives for time-resolved studies thereof.

</details>


### [173] [Vacuum Wannier Functions for First-Principles Scattering and Photoemission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14105)
*Tyler Wu,Tomás Arias*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出统一固体-真空界面紧束缚和近自由电子描述的第一性原理真空Wannier函数理论，通过3D Wannier紧密堆积原理实现界面全Born级数散射态的密集k空间构造，用于预测性光电子发射计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光电子发射计算依赖半经验真空势，缺乏统一描述固体-真空界面的第一性原理方法。需要建立能够同时处理紧束缚和近自由电子描述的理论框架，实现无半经验参数的预测性计算

Method: 建立真空Wannier函数的第一性原理理论，推导任意维度规范Wannier函数的解析解和1D解缠函数。提出并数值验证3D Wannier紧密堆积原理，通过密集k空间构造实现界面全Born级数散射态

Result: 理论成功应用于石墨烯和六方氮化硼体系，揭示了超越第一Born近似的修正效应。实现了无需半经验真空势的预测性光电子发射计算

Conclusion: 该工作建立了统一固体-真空界面描述的理论框架，通过Wannier紧密堆积原理实现了第一性原理光电子发射计算，为界面电子结构研究提供了新方法

Abstract: We establish a first-principles theory of vacuum Wannier functions unifying tight-binding and nearly-free-electron descriptions across solid-vacuum interfaces. Analytic solutions for canonical Wannier functions in arbitrary dimension and disentangled functions in 1D motivate a numerically verified 3D Wannier close-packing principle, enabling dense k-space construction of full Born-series scattering states at interfaces and thus predictive photoemission calculations without semiempirical vacuum potentials. Applications to graphene and h-BN reveal corrections beyond the first-Born approximation.

</details>


### [174] [Effects of uniaxial strain on monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14138)
*Igor Evangelista,Abdul Saboor,Muhammad Zubair,Intuon Chatratin,Ruiqi Hu,Dai Q. Ho,Shoaib Khalid,Ioanna Fampiou,Anderson Janotti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函计算研究单层1H相过渡金属二硫化物在单轴应变下的能带结构演化，发现拉伸应变导致带隙显著减小，导带和价带极值点从K点漂移形成间接带隙，解释了MoS₂光致发光强度随应变减弱的实验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单层过渡金属二硫化物在应变下的能带结构变化，旨在理解应变如何调控这些材料的电子和光学性质，为光电子和量子缺陷应用提供理论指导。

Method: 采用包含自旋轨道耦合的杂化密度泛函理论计算，对MX₂（M=Mo,W；X=S,Se,Te）单层材料进行单轴应变下的能带结构计算，并构建最小紧束缚模型解释能谷漂移现象。

Result: 拉伸单轴应变导致带隙显著减小，导带边缘对应变更敏感；导带和价带极值点保持简并但从K点漂移，形成间接带隙；MoS₂的间接带隙程度随拉伸应变增加，解释了实验观测的光致发光强度减弱现象。

Conclusion: 应变可有效调控过渡金属二硫化物的能带结构，特别是通过能谷漂移机制实现直接到间接带隙的转变，为光电子器件和量子缺陷工程提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Using hybrid density functional calculations including spin-orbit coupling, we compute the strain evolution of the band structure of monolayer 1H-phase transition-metal dichalcogenides, MX$_2$ (M= Mo, W; X= S, Se, Te), emphasizing an accurate reproduction of the quasiparticle band gap (as opposed to the excitonic optical gap). We show that tensile uniaxial strain applied along either the armchair or zigzag directions leads to a pronounced reduction of the fundamental gap, with the conduction-band edge generally exhibiting the stronger strain response. Both the conduction-band electron valleys (CBM) and the valence-band hole valleys (VBM) remain degenerate under uniaxial strain, while simultaneously drifting away from the high-symmetry $K$ point under strain ("valley drift"), such that the band extrema occur at nearby off-symmetry wave vectors. A minimal tight-binding model rationalizes the valley drift and the unequal electron- and hole-valley drift rates in the presence of strain, leading to indirect band gaps. In particular, for MoS$_2$ the indirectness increases with tensile strain, providing a natural explanation for the experimentally observed decrease in photoluminescence intensity under uniaxial deformation. These results provide quantitative guidance for tailoring band structures for optoelectronic and quantum-defect applications.

</details>


### [175] [Digital Hydrogen Platform (DigHyd): A Rigorously Curated Database for Hydrogen Storage Materials Empowered by AI-Assisted Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14139)
*Seong-Hoon Jang,Di Zhang,Xue Jia,Hung Ba Tran,Linda Zhang,Ryuhei Sato,Yusuke Hashimoto,Toyoto Sato,Kiyoe Konno,Shin-ichi Orimo,Hao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了Digital Hydrogen Platform (DigHyd)，这是一个包含超过30,000个数据条目、涵盖4,000多个实验文献源的固态储氢材料数据库，通过AI辅助文献挖掘和人工验证构建，重点关注热力学参数ΔH和ΔS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态储氢材料是安全紧凑储氢的有前景候选材料，但该领域的数据驱动研究受到大规模、高质量数据集可用性的限制。需要构建一个经过严格整理的热力学数据库来支持储氢材料的系统分析。

Method: 采用AI辅助文献挖掘结合人工验证的方法构建数据库，通过手动分析多温度压力-组成-温度(PCT)数据并使用van't Hoff分析获得热力学参数(ΔH和ΔS)。数据库包含>4,000个实验文献源和>30,000个数据条目。

Result: DigHyd数据库揭示了不同材料类别热力学参数的独特分布特征，以及在代表性氢化物系统中的广泛组成变异性。物理可解释的符号回归和黑盒XGBoost模型在预测储氢密度(w)和室温平衡压力(Peq,RT)方面表现出相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: DigHyd提供了一个经过严格整理的热力学数据集，为储氢材料的数据驱动分析提供了可靠基础，支持结构-性能关系的系统探索，通过关注ΔH和ΔS而非固定温度下的平衡压力，能够灵活评估特定应用条件下的平衡行为。

Abstract: Solid-state hydrogen storage materials are promising candidates for safe and compact hydrogen storage; however, data-driven discovery in this field remains limited by the availability of large-scale, well-curated datasets. Here, we present the Digital Hydrogen Platform (DigHyd: www.dighyd.org), a rigorously curated database comprising $>4,000$ experimental literature sources and $>30,000$ data entries on hydrogen storage materials, constructed through AI-assisted literature mining combined with human-in-the-loop validation. In addition to gravimetric hydrogen storage density ($w$), DigHyd also covers thermodynamic parameters, specifically the enthalpy ($ΔH$) and entropy ($ΔS$) changes associated with hydrogenation reactions, primarily defined as $M + \frac{1}{2} {\rm H}_2 \rightleftarrows M{\rm H}$. These parameters were obtained by manually analyzing multi-temperature pressure-composition-temperature (PCT) data using van't Hoff analysis. By focusing on $ΔH$ and $ΔS$ rather than fixing equilibrium pressure at a single temperature, DigHyd enables flexible evaluation of equilibrium behavior under application-specific operating conditions. Statistical analyses reveal distinct distributions of thermodynamic parameters across material classes, together with broad compositional variability within representative hydride systems. Furthermore, both physically interpretable symbolic regression and black-box XGBoost models achieve comparable predictive performance for $w$ and equilibrium pressure at room temperature ($P_{\rm eq,RT}$), demonstrating internal consistency and learnable composition-property relationships within the curated dataset. Overall, DigHyd provides a rigorously curated thermodynamic dataset that serves as a reliable basis for data-driven analyses of hydrogen storage materials and supports systematic exploration of structure-property relationships.

</details>


### [176] [Emergent giant topological Hall effect in twisted Fe3GeTe2 metallic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14192)
*Hyuncheol Kim,Kai-Xuan Zhang,Yu-Hang Li,Giung Park,Ran Cheng,Je-Geun Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在保持全局反演对称性的扭曲Fe3GeTe2金属系统中，在0.45°-0.75°的"魔角"范围内发现了巨大的拓扑霍尔效应，该效应源于局部反演对称性破缺诱导的斯格明子晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑霍尔效应通常出现在全局反演对称性破缺的系统中，但本研究旨在探索在保持全局反演对称性的扭曲范德华磁体中是否也能产生拓扑霍尔效应，以拓展拓扑磁结构的工程化平台。

Method: 通过扭曲Fe3GeTe2金属系统，在特定"魔角"范围(0.45°-0.75°)内测量拓扑霍尔效应，并利用微磁模拟分析其物理起源，揭示局部反演对称性破缺导致的交替面内和层间Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用。

Result: 在扭曲Fe3GeTe2系统中，仅在0.45°-0.75°的窄"魔角"窗口内观测到巨大的拓扑霍尔效应，该效应源于局部反演对称性破缺诱导的斯格明子晶格，而在此范围外完全不存在该效应。

Conclusion: 扭曲Fe3GeTe2为在金属扭曲范德华磁体中工程化和控制拓扑磁结构提供了一个多功能平台，为下一代自旋电子器件开辟了新途径，展示了局部对称性破缺在拓扑效应产生中的关键作用。

Abstract: The topological Hall effect, driven by the exchange interaction between conduction electrons and topological magnetic textures such as skyrmions, is a powerful probe for investigating the topological properties of magnetic materials. Typically, this phenomenon arises in systems with broken global inversion symmetry, where Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions stabilize such textures. Here, we report the discovery of an emergent giant topological Hall effect in the twisted Fe3GeTe2 metallic system, which notably preserves the general global inversion symmetry. This effect manifests exclusively within a narrow window of "magic" twist angles ranging from 0.45° to 0.75°, while it is absent identically outside of that range, highlighting its unique and emergent nature. Micromagnetic simulations reveal that this topological Hall effect originates from a skyrmion lattice induced by alternating in-plane and layer-contrasting Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions that result from local inversion symmetry breaking. Our findings underscore twisted Fe3GeTe2 as a versatile platform for engineering and controlling topological magnetic textures in metallic twisted van der Waals magnets, thereby opening up new avenues for next-generation spintronic devices.

</details>


### [177] [Millimeter-Scale, Atomically Controlled 2D Topological Insulators Revealed by Multimodal Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14199)
*Woojoo Lee,Qiang Gao,Yufei Zhao,Hui Li,Albert Tsui,Yichao Zhang,Yunhe Bai,Haoran Lin,Khanh Duy Nguyen,Gabriele Berruto,Gangbin Yan,Jianchen Dang,Tongyao Wu,Hossein Rokni,Thomas S. Marchese,Ying Shirley Meng,Chao-Xing Liu,Xiao-Xiao Zhang,Chong Liu,Pinshane Y. Huang,Mark C. Hersam,Binghai Yan,Shuolong Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究建立了双层Bi2Te3和MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3异质结构作为原子级控制、毫米级二维拓扑绝缘体，具有约100-150 meV的大反转带隙，可在近室温下工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维拓扑绝缘体（量子自旋霍尔绝缘体）因其受拓扑保护的边缘态而备受关注，但现有的大带隙二维拓扑绝缘体要么化学稳定性极差，要么无法在宏观尺度上实现原子级精确制备。需要开发一种可扩展、稳定的材料平台。

Method: 采用精确的逐层生长技术，制备出毫米级、地毯状形态的双层Bi2Te3和MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3异质结构薄膜。通过机械剥离和湿法/干法转移处理样品。使用多模态光谱学和显微技术（包括光子能量依赖的光电子能谱、时间分辨光电子能谱、扫描隧道谱）表征电子结构、带反转、带动力学和拓扑边缘态。

Result: 成功制备出原子级控制、毫米级二维拓扑绝缘体。双层Bi2Te3具有~100 meV的反转带隙，MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3具有~150 meV的反转带隙。实验观测到整数层调控的电子结构、带反转、带动力学以及拓扑边缘态，与理论预测高度一致。这些大带隙表明可在近室温下工作。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个可扩展的材料平台，用于下一代低损耗量子器件和节能器件。双层Bi2Te3和MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3异质结构作为原子级控制、宏观尺度的二维拓扑绝缘体，具有大反转带隙，为实际应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum spin Hall insulators, or synonymously known as 2D topological insulators, are crucial 2D systems hosting topologically protected edge states. The working temperature of this topological quantum phase is dictated by the inverted bandgap. However, the previously identified large-gap 2D topological insulators are either extremely chemically unstable, or cannot be made with atomistic precision over macroscopic scales. Here, we establish two-quintuple-layer Bi2Te3 and MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3 heterostructures as atomically controlled, millimeter-scale 2D topological insulators, enabled by precision layer-by-layer growth that yields a carpet-like morphology extending coherently over macroscopic distances. This carpet-like growth mode renders the films amenable to mechanical exfoliation and subsequent wet or dry transfer. Multimodal spectroscopies and microscopies reveal the integer-layer tuned electronic structure of (Bi2Te3)n with excellent agreement to theory. Photon-energy-dependent photoemission and time-resolved photoemission identify band inversion and band dynamics, respectively, while scanning tunneling spectroscopy resolves topological edge states, characteristic of the 2D topological insulator phase. Thickness- and photon-energy-dependent photoemission further validates MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3 as a robust 2D topological insulator. The large inverted gaps of ~100 meV in (Bi2Te3)2 and ~150 meV in MnBi2Te4/Bi2Te3 suggest operation near ambient temperature. These results define a scalable materials platform for next-generation, low-loss quantum and energy-efficient devices.

</details>


### [178] [Cut-and-Project Density Functional Theory for Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14590)
*Gavin N. Nop,Jonathan D. H. Smith,Thomas Koschny,Durga Paudyal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于切割投影方法的新理论，用于精确描述准晶体中的局部物理相互作用，通过密度泛函理论(DFT++)处理薛定谔方程，实现了对准晶体量子态的从头计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法只能处理准晶体的晶体近似结构，无法直接描述准晶体的量子态。需要一种既能保持数学严谨性又具有计算可行性的方法，来准确描述准晶体中的局部物理相互作用。

Method: 采用新颖的局域化程序，将准晶体中的局部物理相互作用通过切割投影方法从高维空间的对应物理相互作用中描述。结合密度泛函理论(DFT++)公式，使切割投影方法能够处理准晶体中的薛定谔方程。

Result: 开发了一种既严格又计算可行的理论框架，实现了对准晶体量子态的从头计算。该方法超越了仅能处理准周期结构晶体近似的先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过切割投影方法与密度泛函理论的结合，建立了一种能够精确描述准晶体局部物理相互作用和量子态的严谨理论，为准晶体的量子力学研究提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Cut-and-project from a symmetric structure in a higher-dimensional space is a standard method for describing the structure of a large class of quasicrystals. By means of a novel localization procedure, we now show how local physical interactions within these quasicrystals are also accurately described by cut-and-project, from corresponding physical interactions in the higher-dimensional space. A density functional theory (DFT++) formulation allows the cut-and-project method to handle the Schroedinger equation for interactions in quasicrystals. The theory is both rigorous and computationally tractable. The resulting ab initio approach specifies quasicrystalline quantum states, in contrast to previous approaches which only worked with crystalline approximants of the quasi-periodic structures.

</details>


### [179] [Evolution of Phonon Transport Across Structural Phase Transitions in MgAgSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14213)
*Luman Shang,Yu Wu,Yufan Liu,Shuming Zeng,Gang Tang,Chenhan Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过第一性原理计算系统分析了MgAgSb三种相（α、β、γ）的晶格热导率，发现其随相变递增（α<β<γ），揭示了不同相中粒子状和波状声子输运的竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MgAgSb作为一种有前景的热电材料，其可逆相变显著影响热输运行为。理解不同相中热导率的微观机制对于优化热电性能至关重要，但目前对相变如何影响声子输运机制缺乏系统认识。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，系统研究MgAgSb三种相（α、β、γ）的晶格热导率。分析包括四声子散射和电子-声子散射对粒子状热导率的抑制效应，以及相干声子隧穿对波状热导率的贡献。

Result: 1. 晶格热导率呈现α<β<γ的递增趋势；2. 四声子散射显著抑制β和γ相的粒子状热导率；3. α相中波状热导率贡献高达44%；4. 温度依赖性因相而异：β相中粒子状热导率变化微弱源于Grüneisen参数随温度降低，α相中波状热导率随温度升高抵消了粒子状热导率的衰减。

Conclusion: 研究建立了MgAgSb热输运的完整图像，强调了相变对粒子状和波状声子贡献相互作用的决定性影响，为理解相变材料的热输运机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: MgAgSb, a promising thermoelectric material, undergoes reversible phase transitions that drastically alter its thermal transport behavior. Using first-principles calculations, we systematically investigate the lattice thermal conductivity ($κ_L$) of its three phases: $α$, $β$, and $γ$, revealing a progressive increase following $α< β< γ$. This trend originates from distinct scattering mechanisms. Four-phonon scattering substantially suppresses the particle-like conductivity ($κ_p$) in the $β$ and $γ$ phases, while electron-phonon scattering provides a minor additional reduction. In contrast, the wave-like conductivity ($κ_c$) from coherent phonon tunneling is highest in the complex $α$ phase, contributing up to 44\% of $κ_L$. Notably, the temperature dependence of $κ_L$ differs fundamentally between phases: in $β$, the weak $κ_p$ variation arises from a decreasing Grüneisen parameter with temperature; in $α$, the strong rise in $κ_c$ with temperature counteracts the decay of $κ_p$. Our findings establish a comprehensive picture of thermal transport in MgAgSb, highlighting the phase-dependent interplay between particle-like and wave-like phonon contributions.

</details>


### [180] [Diagonal Curvature in Second Order Jahn Teller Theory Can Be Negative: An Analytic Proof with First-Principles Confirmation in NH3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14291)
*Zhian Li,Hanxiang Mi,Xiyue Cheng,Jurgen Kohler,Shuiquan Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SOJTT中假设对角曲率为正，但本文证明该曲率可为负，颠覆了该理论的核心前提。通过多体展开和Kohn-Sham框架分析，发现声子诱导的本征能变化仅源于电子-核、Hartree和交换关联项，动能项完全抵消。NH3计算表明D3h构型位于A2"鞍点，HOMO-LUMO混合贡献小于总能量下降的0.2%，99.8%来自驱动N(2s-2pz)电子重排的对角电子-核项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶Jahn-Teller理论(SOJTT)基于对角曲率为正的基本假设，但该假设缺乏严格的量子力学原理支撑。本文旨在检验这一核心前提，探究对角曲率是否确实必须为正，从而重新审视SOJTT的理论基础。

Method: 采用冻结声子多体展开方法，将问题重新表述到Kohn-Sham框架中。通过第一性原理计算分析NH3分子，将D3h参考构型置于A2"鞍点，并运用Schwarz不等式上界分析量化HOMO-LUMO混合贡献。详细分解声子诱导的本征能变化来源，识别电子-核、Hartree、交换关联和动能项的各自贡献。

Result: 1. 对角曲率可为负值，否定了SOJTT的正性假设；2. 声子诱导的本征能变化完全源于电子-核、Hartree和交换关联项，动能项贡献完全抵消；3. NH3的D3h构型位于A2"鞍点；4. HOMO-LUMO混合贡献仅占总能量下降的<0.2%，而99.8%来自驱动N(2s-2pz)电子重排的对角电子-核项。

Conclusion: 本文推翻了SOJTT的核心信条，证明对角曲率不必为正。自发结构对称破缺需要先验证鞍点的存在，而不能假设对角曲率为正。这一发现要求重新评估SOJTT的理论基础和应用条件。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the diagonal curvature in second-order Jahn-Teller theory (SOJTT) can be negative, invalidating its foundational positivity assumption. Using a frozen-phonon many-body expansion, we show no quantum-mechanical principle enforces its positivity; recasting into the Kohn-Sham framework further reveals that phonon-induced eigen-energy changes arise solely from electron-nuclear, Hartree, and exchange-correlation terms, with kinetic-energy contributions canceling identically. First principles calculations on NH3 place the D3h reference configuration at an A2" saddle point; Schwarz-inequality upper-bound analysis confines the HOMO-LUMO mixing contribution to < 0.2% of the total energy drop,while 99.8% originates from the diagonal electron-nuclear term driving N (2s-2pz) electron redistribution. These findings overturn a core SOJTT tenet and establish that spontaneous structural symmetry breaking demands prior verification of a saddle point, not assumed positivity of the diagonal curvature.

</details>


### [181] [Self-Assembled H2NC Molecular Lattices as a Platform for Substrate-Tunable Quantum Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14446)
*Adrian Bahri,Zhibo Kang,Ziyan Zhu,Eric I. Altman,Yu He,Chunjing Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过DFT理论计算结合ARPES和STM实验，系统分析了金属自由萘酞菁(H2Nc)分子自组装单层晶体在自由状态及金属衬底上的电子结构，揭示了金属衬底如何通过轨道杂化、电荷转移和对称性破缺将分子系统转化为可调谐的二维晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相比范德华莫尔系统，分子自组装为异质集成超晶格工程提供了新平台。然而，金属自由萘酞菁(H2Nc)自组装单层分子晶体的电子能带色散及其在金属衬底上的可调谐性尚未充分探索，需要系统研究金属衬底如何调控分子晶格的电子性质。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)计算，结合角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)和扫描隧道显微镜(STM)实验验证。比较自由态H2Nc单层与在贵金属衬底(Ag(100)、Au(111))上组装的H2Nc晶格的电子结构，构建各向异性紧束缚哈密顿量描述能带色散，并进行轨道成分分析。

Result: 自由态H2Nc单层存在分子局域的近平坦态和色散较强的能带，可用各向异性紧束缚模型描述，库仑相互作用和位点间跳跃随分子轨道不同具有广泛可调性。Ag(100)衬底引起强轨道杂化、电荷转移和C2对称性破缺，产生部分填充的衬底介导色散态，使分子晶格金属化。H2Nc/Au(111)的ARPES结果在定性上证实了预测的色散和部分填充。

Conclusion: 金属衬底通过轨道杂化、电荷转移和对称性破缺将H2Nc从孤立分子转化为可调谐的二维晶格，分子超晶格可作为模拟各向异性晶格模型的多功能平台，为设计新型电子材料提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Compared to van der Waals moiré systems, molecular assembly has emerged as an exciting alternative platform for superlattice engineering via heterointegration. The electronic properties of the self-assembled square lattice monolayer molecular crystal of metal-free naphthalocyanine (H2Nc), in particular the electronic band dispersion and their tunability by metal substrates, remain less explored. Using density functional theory, supported by angle-resolved photoemission and scanning tunneling microscopy, we compare the electronic structure of a free-standing H2Nc monolayer with that of H2Nc lattice assembled on noble metal substrates. In the freestanding film, we identify both nearly flat, molecule-localized states and more dispersive bands, and we show that each can be compactly described by an anisotropic tight-binding Hamiltonian that yields band-resolved hopping anisotropies. We further reveal wide tunability in the Coulomb interaction and inter-site hopping based on different molecular orbitals. Adsorption on Ag(100) drives strong orbital hybridization, charge transfer, and C2 symmetry breaking, producing partially filled, substrate-mediated dispersive states that metallize the molecular lattice. Orbital analysis identifies C2-even and C2-odd components and maps the spatial pattern of charge redistribution tied to symmetry breaking. Complementary ARPES on H2Nc/Au(111) qualitatively corroborates the predicted dispersion and partial filling. These results clarify how metal substrates convert H2Nc from isolated molecules into a tunable 2D lattice and highlight molecular superlattices as a versatile platform to simulate anisotropic lattice models.

</details>


### [182] [Role of ionic quantum-anharmonic fluctuations on the bond length alternation and giant piezoelectricity of conjugated polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14472)
*Stefano Paolo Villani,Lorenzo Monacelli,Paolo Barone,Francesco Mauri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 量子离子涨落对功能化共轭聚合物压电性能的影响：尽管量子涨落显著改变结构性质并使二聚化相变边界偏移34%，但强压电响应依然存在，有效电荷的拓扑增强保持稳健甚至增强约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能化共轭聚合物因其预测的巨大压电性而成为有前景的机电材料，这种压电性源于异常大的动态有效电荷和在二聚化相变附近的增强响应。然而，量子离子涨落对压电性的影响尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 使用随机自洽谐波近似结合Rice-Mele双原子链模型，该模型参数化以复现原型碳炔的杂化泛函第一性原理计算。模型准确性通过有/无量子非谐效应的第一性原理计算进行验证。

Result: 离子涨落强烈影响结构性质，使二聚化相变边界偏移34%。尽管键长的量子涨落幅度与平均值相当，但强压电响应依然存在。有效电荷的拓扑增强保持稳健，甚至因量子诱导的电子带隙收缩而增强约20%。压电系数仍由内部弛豫主导并保持类形态特征，在重整化边界附近达到最大值，量子非谐性主要改变最佳增强窗口的位置。

Conclusion: 量子离子涨落虽然显著改变功能化共轭聚合物的结构性质，但不会破坏其强压电响应。有效电荷的拓扑增强机制保持稳健，压电性能在重整化相变边界附近仍能达到最大值，量子非谐性主要影响最佳性能窗口的位置而非破坏压电增强机制。

Abstract: Functionalized conjugated polymers are promising materials for electromechanical applications due to predicted giant piezoelectricity, arising from anomalously large dynamical effective charges and an enhanced response in the proximity of the dimerization phase transition. In this work, we assess the impact of quantum ionic fluctuations on piezoelectricity using the stochastic self-consistent harmonic approximation with a Rice-Mele diatomic chain model, parametrized to reproduce hybrid-functional first-principles calculations of prototypical carbyne. The model's accuracy is validated against first-principles calculations both with and without quantum-anharmonic effects. We find that ionic fluctuations strongly impact the structural properties, with the boundary of the dimerization phase transition shifted by $34\%$. Despite quantum fluctuations in the bond length reaching magnitudes comparable to the average, the strong piezoelectric response persists. The topological enhancement of the effective charges remains robust and is even enhanced by about $\sim20\%$ thanks to a quantum-induced shrinking of the electronic gap. The piezoelectric coefficient remains dominated by the internal relaxation and retains a morphotropic-like character, reaching maximum values near the renormalized boundary, with quantum anharmonicity mainly shifting the optimal enhancement window.

</details>


### [183] [Gas sensing potential of stacked graphene/h-BN structures: a DFT-based investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14530)
*Martin Siebel,Pavel Rubin,Raivo Jaaniso*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究使用周期性DFT分析NO2、NH3和O3在石墨烯/h-BN异质结上的吸附行为，发现h-BN覆盖层既作为活性吸附表面又保护石墨烯免于氧化。两种模型系统（扩展双层和岛状覆盖）表现出不同的电子结构和吸附特性，NO2和O3显著增强石墨烯导电性，NH3影响较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究石墨烯/h-BN异质结作为气体传感器材料的潜力，探索h-BN覆盖层在保护石墨烯免于不可逆氧化过程的同时，如何影响气体分子的吸附行为和传感性能。

Method: 使用周期性密度泛函理论（DFT）研究NO2、NH3和O3在石墨烯/h-BN异质结上的吸附。构建两种模型系统：扩展的石墨烯/h-BN双层（B36N36C72）和部分覆盖较小h-BN岛的石墨烯片（B11N11C72）。分析电子结构、电荷转移和吸附能。

Result: 两种系统的电子结构在狄拉克点附近差异显著：扩展双层中费米能级与原始石墨烯对齐，电荷转移可忽略；岛状系统中费米能级向低能移动，电子从石墨烯转移到h-BN。NO2在B11N11C72上结合更强，形成化学键；O3在该表面解离但在扩展双层上保持完整；NH3在岛状系统中异常地作为电子受体。NO2和O3显著增加石墨烯电导率，而NH3引起的变化较弱。

Conclusion: 石墨烯/h-BN异质结具有作为气体传感器材料的潜力，h-BN覆盖层既能保护石墨烯又能提供活性吸附位点。不同结构配置导致显著不同的吸附行为，特别是岛状覆盖系统表现出更强的气体-基底相互作用和更明显的传感响应。

Abstract: Using periodic DFT, we examined the adsorption of NO2, NH3, and O3 on the h-BN side of a graphene/h-BN heterostructure designed as a model gas sensor material. The h-BN overlayer serves both as an active adsorption surface and as protection that may reduce irreversible processes such as graphene oxidation. Two model systems were considered: an extended graphene/h-BN bilayer (B36N36C72) and a graphene sheet partially covered by a smaller h-BN island (B11N11C72). Their electronic structures differ strongly near the Dirac point. In the extended bilayer, the Fermi level remains aligned with that of pristine graphene, indicating negligible charge transfer. In the island-covered system, the Fermi level shifts to lower energies, reflecting electron transfer from graphene to h-BN. These differences lead to distinct adsorption behavior. NO2 binds much more strongly to B11N11C72, forming a chemical bond, while O3 dissociates on this surface but remains intact on the extended bilayer. NH3 unusually acts as an electron acceptor in the island system. Overall, NO2 and O3 substantially increase graphene conductivity, whereas NH3 induces much weaker changes. These results highlight the potential of graphene/h-BN heterostructures for gas sensing.

</details>


### [184] [Loss of altermagnetic order and smooth restoration of Kramers' spin degeneracy with increasing temperature in CrSb and MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15035)
*Christopher D. Woodgate,Nabil Menai,Arthur Ernst,Julie B. Staunton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究通过无序局域矩方法分析热诱导自旋涨落如何改变两种典型反铁磁体CrSb和MnTe的电子结构，发现即使在奈尔温度以上仍需自旋极化描述，并量化了反铁磁序参数的温度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究热诱导自旋涨落对反铁磁体电子结构的影响，特别是反铁磁序参数的温度依赖性及其对自旋输运性质的意义。

Method: 采用无序局域矩图像进行自洽的ab initio计算，分析CrSb和MnTe两种典型反铁磁体在顺磁态下的电子结构，研究Kramers自旋简并的恢复过程。

Result: 1. Cr和Mn原子在顺磁态下仍保持局域磁矩，即使在奈尔温度以上仍需自旋极化描述；2. 金属性CrSb在远低于TN温度下自旋简并恢复，反铁磁特征因磁无序导致费米能级附近强色散态严重展宽而消失；3. 半导体MnTe的带隙基本不受磁无序影响，自旋简并仅在接近和高于TN温度时恢复；4. 量化了两种材料反铁磁序参数的温度依赖性。

Conclusion: 热诱导自旋涨落显著改变反铁磁体的电子结构，金属性和半导体性反铁磁体表现出不同的温度响应行为，这对理解其自旋输运性质具有重要影响。

Abstract: We describe how thermally induced spin fluctuations modify the electronic structures of two prototypical altermagnets, CrSb and MnTe, via application of the disordered local moment picture. For both materials, our self-consistent, ab initio calculations demonstrate that local magnetic moments persist on Cr and Mn atoms in their paramagnetic states, necessitating a spin-polarised description of the electronic structure even above the Néel temperature, $T_\mathrm{N}$. Moreover, Kramers' spin degeneracy, which is broken for both materials in their altermagnetic ground states, is shown to be smoothly restored - on the average - as the local moments thermally disorder. In metallic CrSb, this occurs at temperatures well below $T_\mathrm{N}$ and the signature effects of its altermagnetism are lost as the magnetic disorder induces heavy smearing of strongly dispersive electronic states around the Fermi energy. By contrast, in semiconducting MnTe, with its band gap largely unaffected by magnetic disorder, the spin degeneracy only returns at temperatures close to and above $T_\mathrm{N}$. We quantify the temperature dependence of the altermagnetic order parameter and the underlying electronic structures of both materials, with significant implications for their spin transport properties.

</details>


### [185] [Radiation-induced segregation in dilute Fe-Cr: A rate-theory framework for the Cr enrichment-depletion transition at the grain boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14574)
*Russell Oplinger,Mukesh Bachhav,Karim Ahmed,Sourabh Bhagwan Kadambi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于物理的速率理论模型研究铁素体Fe-Cr合金中Cr在晶界的辐射诱导偏析，揭示了温度依赖的传输特性决定偏析方向，而剂量率、晶粒尺寸和位错密度仅影响偏析幅度和空间范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射诱导偏析（RIS）对铁素体Fe-Cr合金在辐照下的机械完整性和晶间腐蚀敏感性构成重大挑战，但Cr偏析机制尚未完全理解。需要建立物理模型来系统研究RIS现象。

Method: 采用基于物理的速率理论模型，参数化使用自洽平均场理论推导的Onsager传输系数，研究稀Fe-(0.1 at.%) Cr合金中晶界处的RIS。在空位和自间隙原子产生率相等且体相位错吸收率相等的条件下模拟，并考虑更现实的缺陷产生和吸收偏置条件。

Result: 在对称点缺陷通量条件下，模型成功模拟了实验观察到的低温Cr富集向高温贫化的转变。温度依赖的传输特性决定偏析方向，而剂量率、晶粒尺寸和位错密度仅影响偏析幅度和空间范围。在更现实的空位优先产生和/或自间隙原子优先吸收条件下，富集-贫化转变温度向低温移动。

Conclusion: 仅基于传输系数的RIS预测仅在对称点缺陷通量条件下有效，准确的预测必须考虑缺陷产生和吸收的偏置效应。该工作为理解铁素体合金中的RIS提供了机制框架，并为先进核系统合金设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Radiation-induced segregation (RIS) poses a significant challenge for ferritic Fe-Cr alloys under irradiation, as it can compromise mechanical integrity and increase susceptibility to intergranular corrosion. Yet, the mechanisms governing Cr segregation remain incompletely understood. In this study, We present a physics-based rate-theory model parameterized using self-consistent mean field theory-based Onsager transport coefficients to investigate RIS at the grain boundary (GB) in dilute Fe-(0.1 at.%) Cr. Under equal production rates of vacancies and self-interstitial atoms (SIA), and their equal absorption rates by bulk dislocations, the model simulates the experimentally observed transition from Cr enrichment at low temperatures to depletion at higher temperatures. Under these unbiased conditions, systematic investigation reveals that while temperature-dependent transport properties dictate the segregation direction, dose rate, grain size, and dislocation density only influence the magnitude and spatial extent of Cr segregation. However, under more realistic conditions of preferential vacancy production within damage cascade and/or preferential SIA absorption by bulk dislocations, the enrichment-to-depletion transition shifts to lower temperatures. Our findings demonstrate that RIS predictions based solely on transport coefficients are valid only under symmetric point defect flux conditions, and that biases in defect production and absorption must be considered for accurate predictions. This work provides a mechanistic framework for understanding RIS in ferritic alloys and informs alloy design for advanced nuclear systems.

</details>


### [186] [Sequential Quenching to Predict Semiconductor Defect Concentrations from Formation & Migration Energies: The Case of CdTe:As Doping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14618)
*Khandakar Aaditta Arnab,Intuon Chatratin,Anderson Janotti,Michael Scarpulla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"顺序淬火"方法作为第三种缺陷计算类型，用于模拟有限冷却速率下受扩散限制动力学的缺陷浓度，解决了传统全平衡或全淬火计算无法准确预测实际冷却过程的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缺陷计算通常假设瞬时淬火或完全平衡状态，这两种极端情况很少在实际中出现。半导体中的缺陷浓度受生长和冷却过程中热历史的强烈影响，需要一种更符合实际冷却过程的计算方法。

Method: 提出顺序淬火方法，利用缺陷形成能和迁移能，模拟有限冷却速率下受扩散限制动力学的缺陷浓度。每种缺陷的浓度在其特征温度下冻结，该温度由扩散速率、与源/汇的距离以及冷却速率决定。不同电荷态通过电荷中性相互作用，但在不同温度下冻结。

Result: 以As掺杂CdTe为例，SQ计算显示快速扩散的缺陷（如Cd间隙）在较低温度下仍保持移动性，并在较大特征距离下冻结，导致快速冷却或块体样品中出现强补偿和n型行为。较慢冷却和减小特征距离可抑制施主冻结并增强p型激活。

Conclusion: 顺序淬火提供了一个物理透明且计算高效的框架，能够将冷却条件、样品几何形状和缺陷动力学与CdTe及相关材料中的掺杂剂激活联系起来，解决了传统方法无法准确预测实际冷却过程的问题。

Abstract: Defect concentrations in semiconductors are strongly influenced by thermal history during growth and cooldown, yet most defect calculations assume either instantaneous quenching from high temperature or that full-equilibrium is maintained - two limiting cases rarely approached in reality. Here, we introduce sequential quenching (SQ) as a 3rd type of defect calculation utilizing defect formation and migration energies to model defect concentrations subject to diffusion-limited kinetics in samples cooled at finite rates. In SQ, the concentration of each defect is frozen at a characteristic temperature determined by its diffusion rate, distance to sources/sinks, and cooling rate. Because different charge-states interact through charge neutrality but freeze at different temperatures, the sequence of freeze-in events is non-commuting. Critically, not all room-temperature SQ solutions can be predicted from full equilibrium (EQ) or full-quenching (FQ) calculations - erroneous predictions are likely without SQ. We illustrate SQ using the example of As-doped CdTe, for which experimental data show differences in doping with cooling rate and between polycrystalline thin-films for photovoltaics and bulk crystals. SQ calculations reveal that fast-diffusing defects such as Cd-interstitials remain mobile to lower temperatures and freeze-in at larger characteristic distances, leading to strong compensation and n-type behavior in rapidly cooled or bulk samples. Slower cooling and reduced characteristic distances suppress donor freeze-in and enhance p-type activation. These results establish SQ as a physically transparent and computationally efficient framework for connecting cooling conditions, sample geometry, and defect kinetics to dopant activation in CdTe and related materials.

</details>


### [187] [Stabilization of the Orthorhombic Phase in Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 Nanoparticles by Oxygen Vacancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14642)
*Yuri O. Zagorodniy,Eugene A. Eliseev,Valentin V. Laguta,Petr Jiricek,Jana Houdkova,Lesya D. Demchenko,Oksana V. Leshchenko,Victor N. Pavlikov,Lesya P. Yurchenko,Anna O. Diachenko,Michail D. Volnyanskii,Myroslav V. Karpets,Mikhail P. Trubitsyn,Dean R. Evans,Anna N. Morozovska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过控制氧空位浓度，在Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒中稳定了正交相，理论计算证实氧空位诱导的化学应变可稳定极性/反极性长程有序结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒中稳定正交相（o-phase），特别是通过控制氧空位浓度来实现相稳定，这对于铁电材料应用具有重要意义

Method: 1) 在空气和CO+CO2气氛中退火制备Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒（平均尺寸7nm）；2) 通过电子顺磁共振谱和X射线光电子能谱测定氧空位浓度；3) 使用X射线衍射和核磁共振表征正交相分数；4) 基于Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire理论进行唯象计算

Result: 正交相分数依赖于由退火条件决定的氧空位浓度，理论计算证实氧空位诱导的化学应变可以稳定小尺寸铪锆氧化物纳米颗粒中的正交相，并形成极性和反极性长程有序

Conclusion: 通过控制退火条件调节氧空位浓度是稳定Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒中正交相的有效方法，氧空位诱导的化学应变在稳定极性结构方面起关键作用

Abstract: In this work we study the stabilization of the o-phase in small Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 nanoparticles (the average size 7 nm) annealed in air and in the CO+CO2 ambient. Concentration of the oxygen vacancies, which is determined by annealing conditions, was estimated from the electron paramagnetic resonance spectra and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. The fraction of the orthorhombic phase that was controlled by the X-ray diffraction and nuclear magnetic resonance, depends on the concentration of oxygen vacancies, which are defined by annealing conditions. Phenomenological calculations based on Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire theory confirm that the chemical strains induced by oxygen vacancies can stabilize the orthorhombic phase with polar and antipolar long-range ordering in small hafnia-zirconia nanoparticles.

</details>


### [188] [A phase field model with arbitrary misorientation dependence of grain boundary energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14660)
*Philip Staublin,Yuri Mishin,Peter W. Voorhees*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的取向场模型，通过使自由能泛函系数成为晶粒间非局部取向差的函数，从而能够嵌入任意取向差依赖的晶界自由能，克服了现有模型无法再现晶界自由能随取向差增大而降低的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有取向场模型在模拟多晶材料时存在显著局限性：通常无法再现晶界自由能随取向差增大而降低的现象，这限制了模型在真实多晶材料应用中的准确性。

Method: 对Kobayashi-Warren-Carter晶粒生长模型进行改进，使自由能泛函的系数成为晶粒间非局部取向差的函数。通过沿晶界法线方向在固定距离处插值取向场来计算非局部取向差，从而在模型中嵌入任意取向差依赖的晶界自由能。

Result: 改进后的模型能够嵌入任意取向差依赖的晶界自由能，包括在取向差依赖关系中引入尖锐的尖点。模型还扩展到三维情况，展示了其在模拟真实多晶材料中的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过引入非局部取向差依赖的自由能泛函系数，成功克服了现有取向场模型在再现晶界自由能随取向差变化方面的局限性，为更准确地模拟多晶材料中的晶粒生长提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Grain growth in polycrystals is often simulated using orientation-field models, which employ a field to represent the local orientation of the crystal lattice. These models can be challenging to represent a realistic misorientation dependence of grain boundary free energy. We prove that existing orientation-field models, in general, cannot reproduce a decrease in the grain boundary free energy with a increasing misorientation angle, demonstrating a significant limitation of previous models in applications to polycrystalline materials. To overcome this limitation, we propose a modification to the Kobayashi-Warren-Carter model for grain growth wherein the coefficients of the free-energy functional become functions of the misorientation between the grains, which is a non-local quantity. Due to this modification, an arbitrary dependence of the grain boundary free energy on the misorientation can be embedded in the model. We propose calculating the non-local misorientation by interpolating the orientation field at a fixed distance in both directions along the local grain boundary normal vector. The capabilities of the model are demonstrated by introduction of a sharp cusp to the misorientation dependent grain boundary free energy. Finally, we propose an extension of the model to three dimensions.

</details>


### [189] [Acrylamide Conformers: A Revision of Published Density Functional Theory Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14675)
*William Scott,Estela Blaisten-Barojas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高精度DFT计算验证了丙烯酰胺存在三种稳定构象体（一个平面trans构象和两个镜像skew构象），澄清了文献中关于构象体数量（2-4个）的争议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清文献中关于丙烯酰胺稳定构象体数量的不一致报道（有些文献报道2个，有些报道3个或4个），通过高精度计算验证实际存在的构象体数量

Method: 使用高精度密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，在wB97XD/Def2TZVPP水平上验证丙烯酰胺的稳定构象体和过渡态能垒，计算振动光谱、原子电荷、笛卡尔坐标和本征反应坐标路径

Result: 确认丙烯酰胺存在三种稳定构象体：1）最稳定的平面trans构象（文献中称为"sys"），2）两个能量简并的三维skew构象（互为镜像结构）。验证了这三个构象体之间的三个过渡态能垒

Conclusion: 丙烯酰胺实际上只有三种稳定构象体，而不是文献中报道的2-4个，通过高精度DFT计算澄清了这一长期存在的争议，为后续研究提供了准确的构象信息

Abstract: Acrylamide, with PubChem identifier CID=6579 is broadcasted to have four stable conformers contrasting with several journal publications characterizing only two or three. In this revision summary the discrepancy is clarified. Through very high precision density functional theory (DFT) calculations, three stable conformers and the three transition state barriers existing between them are verified to exist and validated with our own DFT calculations The most stable conformer is a planar molecular structure termed "sys" or "trans" in the literature. Meanwhile, a less stable structure termed "skew" pertains to two 3-dimensional structures that are energy-degenerate, but differ in their structure for being mirrored images of each other. Vibrational spectra, partial atomic charges, Cartesian coordinates, and Intrinsic Reaction Coordinate paths are summarized and recalculated with DFT at the wB97XD/Def2TZVPP level for the three stable acrylamide isomers: the sys/trans lowest in energy structure, and the two skew mirrored structures.

</details>


### [190] [Decoupling structural and bonding effects on ferroelectric switching in ScAlN via molecular dynamics under an applied electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14747)
*Ryotaro Sahashi,Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过机器学习力场分子动力学模拟，揭示了ScxAl1-xN铁电材料中结构效应和键合效应对剩余极化和矫顽场的独立贡献机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScxAl1-xN作为有前景的纤锌矿型铁电材料，其Sc含量增加会同时降低矫顽场和剩余极化。实验上结构参数u的增加和键强度的减弱是耦合的，两者对铁电翻转的独立贡献尚不清楚。

Method: 采用机器学习力场分子动力学模拟，在施加电场下系统解耦结构效应和键合效应：1)通过面内应变人工调控u参数；2)在固定u下改变Sc含量；3)使用静态微动弹性带计算验证静态翻转势垒

Result: 1)剩余极化完全由结构效应决定，与u呈普适线性关系，与成分无关；2)矫顽场偏离结构趋势，受额外成分影响；3)在相同u下，Pr保持恒定，而Ec随Sc含量增加因键减弱而系统降低；4)静态计算仅捕获u相关的势垒，无法显式体现键合效应对Ec的影响

Conclusion: 剩余极化严格由结构效应控制，而矫顽场由结构效应和键合效应叠加决定。研究强调了动态分子动力学模拟对于全面理解成分可调材料中铁电翻转机制的必要性。

Abstract: ScxAl1-xN has emerged as a promising wurtzite-type ferroelectric material, where increasing the Sc composition reduces both the coercive field (Ec) and remanent polarization (Pr). This composition-dependent behavior is physically attributed to two simultaneous changes: the increase in the internal structural parameter u (structural effect) and the weakening of bond strength (bonding effect). Because these factors are strongly coupled in experiments, their individual contributions to ferroelectric switching remain unclear. In this study, we systematically decoupled these effects using machine-learning force field-based molecular dynamics (MD) simulations under an applied electric field. By artificially tuning u via in-plane strain at a fixed composition, we demonstrated that Pr is determined exclusively by the structural effect, exhibiting a universal linear dependence regardless of the composition. In contrast, Ec deviated from this structural trend, implying an additional compositional contribution. To isolate this, we evaluated configurations with identical u but varying Sc compositions; Pr remained constant, whereas Ec systematically decreased due to bond weakening. Furthermore, static nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations revealed that the static switching barrier depends solely on u, failing to explicitly capture the bonding effect on Ec. These results establish that while Pr is governed strictly by the structural effect, Ec is determined by a superposition of structural and bonding effects. Our findings highlight the necessity of dynamic MD simulations for fully understanding ferroelectric switching in compositionally tunable materials.

</details>


### [191] [Synchrotron-radiation X-ray topography and reticulography of bulk $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ crystals grown from a crucible-free melt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14751)
*Yongzhao Yao,Koki Mizuno,Kazuki Ohnishi,Yukari Ishikawa,Masanori Kitahara,Taketoshi Tomida,Rikito Murakami,Vladimir Kochurikhin,Liudmila Gushchina,Kei Kamada,Koichi Kakimoto,Akira Yoshikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用同步辐射X射线形貌术和X射线网状术研究了OCCC法生长的β-Ga₂O₃单晶结构特性，发现籽晶下方区域晶体质量高，直径扩大时出现晶格扭转，位错分析揭示了<010>取向螺位错主导缺陷结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究OCCC法生长的β-Ga₂O₃单晶的结构特性，阐明缺陷形成机制，为优化生长条件提供依据。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线形貌术和X射线网状术分析晶体结构，通过摇摆曲线半高宽评估晶体质量，分析晶格取向和位错密度分布。

Result: 籽晶下方区域晶体质量高（摇摆曲线半高宽约26角秒），直径扩大时出现扭转型晶格取向差，<010>取向螺位错密度约10⁵cm⁻²，翼区密度更高（约10⁶cm⁻²）。

Conclusion: 阐明了OCCC法生长β-Ga₂O₃单晶的缺陷形成机制，特别是直径扩大过程中的晶格扭转和位错分布特征，为优化生长工艺提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The structural properties of a $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ single crystal grown by the oxide crystal growth from cold crucible (OCCC) method were investigated using synchrotron radiation X-ray topography and X-ray reticulography. The region grown beneath the seed exhibits high crystalline quality with a rocking curve full width at half maximum of about 26 arcsec. During diameter enlargement, a twist-type lattice misorientation develops between the central and laterally expanded regions, originating near the shoulder and propagating along boundaries parallel to the $\langle010\rangle$ growth direction. Dislocation analysis reveals that $\langle010\rangle$-oriented screw dislocations dominate the defect structure with densities of ~$10^{5}$cm$^{-2}$, while higher densities (~$10^{6}$cm$^{-2}$) appear in the wing region. These results clarify defect formation in OCCC-grown $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ and provide insights for optimizing growth conditions.

</details>


### [192] [Interfacial exchange and magnetostatic coupling in a CoFeB/Thulium Iron Garnet heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14766)
*Walid Al Misba,Jenae E. Shoup,Miela J. Gross,Dhritiman Bhattacharya,Kai Liu,Caroline A. Ross,Daniel B. Gopman,Jayasimha Atulasimha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究铁磁绝缘体TmIG与铁磁金属CoFeB/W/CoFeB/MgO/W多层膜之间的交换耦合，通过调节CoFeB厚度控制交换耦合与静磁耦合的相对强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索铁磁绝缘体与铁磁金属层之间的耦合机制，为实现基于铁磁绝缘体的快速、低能耗自旋电子器件提供基础，利用铁磁金属层实现对铁磁绝缘体磁状态的有效电学读取。

Method: 使用振动样品磁强计、磁光克尔显微镜和一阶反转曲线研究结合微磁模拟，分析TmIG/GGG衬底上沉积的CoFeB/W/CoFeB/MgO/W多层膜的耦合特性，通过改变CoFeB层厚度（x ≤1 nm至≥3 nm）调控耦合机制。

Result: 观察到强烈的层间交换耦合和静磁耦合，CoFeB厚度x≤1 nm时交换耦合占主导，厚度增至3 nm或以上时静磁耦合占主导，耦合强度可通过CoFeB厚度调控。

Conclusion: 铁磁绝缘体与铁磁金属层之间的耦合机制可通过结构参数精确调控，这为实现基于铁磁绝缘体的高效自旋电子器件提供了重要途径，特别是利用铁磁金属层实现对铁磁绝缘体磁状态的电学读取。

Abstract: We investigate the exchange coupling between a ferrimagnetic insulator (FI) thulium iron garnet (TmIG) deposited on a gadolinium gallium garnet (GGG) substrate, which shows perpendicular magnetic anisotropy and a ferromagnetic metal (FMM) stack with oxide capping that consists of CoFeB(x)/W(0.4 nm)/CoFeB(0.8 nm)/MgO(1 nm)/W(5 nm). Vibrating sample magnetometry, magneto-optical Kerr microscopy and first-order reversal curve studies coupled with micromagnetic simulations are used to analyze the coupling between these layers. Strong interlayer exchange coupling and magnetostatic coupling are observed in the samples where the relative strength between these interactions can be controlled by varying the thickness of the CoFeB layer. Films with CoFeB thickness x <=1 nm are strongly exchange coupled, whereas the magnetostatic coupling dominates when the thickness is increased to 3 nm or above. These findings have important implications towards realizing fast and energy efficient spintronic devices using a FI, as its coupling to the FMM layer can be used for effective electrical read out of the magnetic state of the FI.

</details>


### [193] [Predicting electron-phonon coupling and electronic transport at the moiré scale in twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14800)
*David J. Abramovitch,Marco Bernardi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一种可扩展的原子级电子势方法，用于研究莫尔系统中电子-声子相互作用和声子限制的电子输运，可处理数千原子的大体系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统第一性原理计算由于计算成本限制，只能处理约100个原子的体系，无法研究包含数千原子的莫尔系统。需要开发可扩展的方法来定量研究大尺度系统中的电子-声子相互作用和输运性质。
