Method: 开发了包含Holstein型和Peierls型项的原子级电子势，用于模拟电子-声子相互作用和声子限制的电子输运。该方法能够准确再现第一性原理计算的电子-声子耦合和电阻率，可处理包含数千原子的大单元胞体系。

Result: 方法在石墨烯和大角度扭曲双层石墨烯中准确再现了第一性原理结果。应用于不同扭转角（低至1.6°，5044原子单元胞）的TBG，发现电阻率随扭转角减小而显著增加（13.2°到1.6°增加两个数量级），主要由于电子能量尺度的逐渐减小。预测结果与2.0°和1.6° TBG实验趋势一致，包括电阻率及其对温度和带填充的依赖性。

Conclusion: 建立了一种可扩展的定量研究方法，用于研究莫尔材料和其他先前无法达到长度尺度系统中的电子-声子相互作用和输运性质，为大规模体系的计算提供了有效工具。

Abstract: First-principles calculations can accurately describe electron-phonon (e-ph) interactions and electronic transport in a wide range of materials, but are currently limited to unit cells with up to $\sim$100 atoms due to computational cost. Here, we develop an atomistic electronic potential with Holstein- and Peierls-like terms for modeling e-ph interactions and phonon-limited electronic transport that enables the study of moiré systems with thousands of atoms per unit cell. This method can accurately reproduce first-principles e-ph coupling and resistivity in graphene and large-angle twisted bilayer graphene (TBG). Using this approach, we study TBG over a range of twist angles down to 1.6$^\circ$ (5044-atom unit cell), and report the evolution of e-ph interactions and phonon-limited resistivity with twist angle. The predicted resistivity increases by two orders of magnitude between 13.2$^\circ$ and 1.6$^\circ$, driven by the progressive reduction of the electronic energy scale. Our calculations can predict key experimental trends in 2.0$^\circ$ and 1.6$^\circ$ TBG, including the resistivity and its dependence on temperature and band filling. Our work establishes a scalable approach for quantitative studies of e-ph interactions and transport in moiré materials and other systems with previously inaccessible length scales.

</details>


### [194] [Hybrid Tribo/piezoelectic Electrospun Nanofibers for Energy Harvesting Enhancement in Flexible Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14857)
*Hao Zhang,Yurong He,Yaofeng Jin,Hui Wang,Wanqi Ye,Lidong Chen,Kaiyang Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过静电纺丝技术制备PVDF基纳米纤维，并引入碳纳米管或石墨烯纳米片作为纳米填料，开发了一种兼具摩擦电和压电效应的混合纳米发电机，实现了高达1.133 W/m²的功率密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦电纳米发电机和压电纳米发电机作为收集机械能并转换为电能的有前景平台，但其材料选择、结构设计和能量输出机制仍面临挑战。需要开发高性能混合纳米发电机以满足柔性电子设备的供电需求。

Method: 采用静电纺丝技术制备PVDF基纳米纤维，利用其产生高β相含量的能力提升压电性能。分别将不同浓度的碳纳米管和石墨烯纳米片作为纳米填料掺入PVDF溶液中，以增强压电响应。纳米填料的引入不仅改变纤维直径，还修饰纤维表面粗糙度，从而增强摩擦电效应。

Result: 制备的纳米发电机输出性能主要由压电效应主导，电输出与PVDF基纳米纤维的β相含量呈强正相关。最高β相含量达到85.3%，并一致产生1.133 W/m²的最佳能量输出。原型器件的功率密度达到W/m²级别，相比传统TENGs或PENGs有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种高性能混合纳米发电机，通过静电纺丝制备的PVDF基纳米纤维结合纳米填料，实现了摩擦电和压电效应的协同增强，为柔性电子设备提供了扩展的供电机会。

Abstract: Triboelectric nanogenerators or TENGs and piezoelectric nanogenerators or PENGs have emerged as promising platforms for harvesting mechanical energy and converting it into electrical energy for powering flexible electronic devices. However, the material selection and structure design of such hybrid nanogenerator, and mechanisms of energy output still remain challenges. In this work, electrospinning is employed for the fabrication of nanofibers, particularly polyvinylidene fluoride or PVDF based nanofibers, due to its capability to generate high beta phase contents that effectively increase the piezoelectric performance of the PVDF friction layer, thereby enhancing the overall electrical performance for flexible electronics by merging tribo-piezoelectric power. Furthermore, various concentrations carbon nanotubes or CNT or graphene nanosheets or GNS are individually incorporated into the PVDF solution as nanofillers or NF to enhance the piezoelectric responses of the PVDF based nanofibers. The introduction of nanofillers is found to not only alter the fiber diameter but also modify the surface roughness of the electrospun nanofibers, and thus, enhancing the triboelectric effect. In addition, the output performance of the fabricated nanogenerator is predominantly governed by the piezoelectric effect rather than triboelectric effect, as the electrical output shows a strong positive correlation with the beta phase content of PVDF based nanofibers: the highest beta phase content reached to 85.3 percent and consistently resulted in the optimal energy output of 1.133 watt per meter square. Notably, the power density achieved by the prototype device reaches to the level of watt/m2, representing a substantial improvement compared with that of the conventional TENGs or PENGs reported to date, providing expanded opportunities for flexible electronic devices

</details>


### [195] [Scalar Spin Chiral Order via Bond Selectivity in Strained Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14971)
*Xin Liu,Li Ma,Mingyue Zhao,Shun Niu,Yu Liu,Yang Li,Jiayao Zhu,Yiwen Zhang,Fengxian Ma,Dewei Zhao,Guoke Li,Congmian Zhen,Denglu Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，在居里温度~740K的共线亚铁磁体Mn4N中，利用各向同性应变作为调控参数，实现了从共线到非共面磁结构的连续转变，从而诱导出长程标量自旋手性序，其大小从0增加到~2.32。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量自旋手性（SSC）在非共面磁体中驱动拓扑输运，但现有SSC有序的温度通常低于100K。当前实现近室温SSC有序的方法主要依赖外场或化学掺杂。在高温共线磁体中生成和控制SSC序仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 以居里温度~740K的共线亚铁磁体Mn4N为平台，采用第一性原理计算，研究各向同性应变作为清洁连续调控参数对磁结构的影响。通过定量轨道分辨键合分析，揭示应变如何选择性抑制Mn 3d轨道与N 2p轨道之间的键合。

Result: 随着应变增加，磁基态从共线连续演变为非共面构型，激活SSC序并将其大小从0增强至~2.32。应变选择性抑制Mn-N键合，降低共价自旋配对激活面内Mn3c磁矩，同时抑制N介导的铁磁超交换相互作用，使最近邻Mn3c位点间的平衡转向反铁磁交换相互作用。

Conclusion: 研究结果建立了在高温共线磁体中构建SSC序的强大应变介导途径，为高温拓扑磁电子学提供了新策略。

Abstract: Scalar spin chirality (SSC) drives a series of topological transports in noncoplanar magnets. However, the ordering temperature of magnet hosting intrinsic SSC order is typically below 100 K. Current approaches to achieve near room temperature SSC order largely rely on external fields or chemical doping in noncollinear magnets. A significant challenge persists in generating and controlling SSC order in high temperature collinear magnets. Here, using the collinear ferrimagnet Mn4N with Neel temperature ~740 K as a platform, we demonstrate that isotropic strain acts as a clean and continuous tuning parameter to induce long range SSC order by first principles calculations. As strain increases from to, the magnetic ground state evolves continuously from a collinear to a noncoplanar configuration, activating the SSC order and enhancing its magnitude from 0 to ~2.32. Our quantitative orbital-resolved bonding analysis reveals that strain selectively suppresses the bond between Mn 3d orbitals and N 2p orbitals, driving dual prerequisites for the SSC order. Specifically, the decreased covalent spin-pairing activates Mn3c moments within the plane, simultaneously the suppressed N-mediated ferromagnetic superexchange interaction shifts the balance of the nearest-neighbor Mn3c sites toward antiferromagnetic exchange interaction. Our findings establish a powerful strain mediated route to construct the SSC order in high temperature collinear magnets.

</details>


### [196] [Electric-Field-induced Two-Dimensional Fully Compensated Ferrimagnetism and Emergent Transport Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14996)
*Jin-Yang Li,Yong-Kun Wang,Ying Zhang,Si Li,Wen-Li Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 单层CoS和CoSe在电场调控下可转变为完全补偿亚铁磁态，展现出零净磁化但自旋分裂的电子能带结构，并产生完全自旋极化电流、反常霍尔效应和磁光效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索完全补偿亚铁磁系统，这类系统具有零净磁化和自旋分裂能带特性，但相比最近发现的交变磁体研究较少。研究电场调控单层CoS和CoSe磁态的可能性及其在自旋电子学中的应用潜力。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算结合理论分析，研究单层CoS和CoSe在外加垂直电场下的磁态转变。分析PT对称性破缺对电子能带结构和磁性质的影响。

Result: 单层CoS和CoSe具有面外奈尔矢量的共线反铁磁基态，电子能带因PT对称性而自旋简并。施加垂直电场破坏PT对称性，诱导出具有显著自旋分裂的完全补偿亚铁磁态。这些态产生完全自旋极化电流、反常霍尔效应以及磁光克尔和法拉第效应。

Conclusion: 单层CoS和CoSe是电场调控完全补偿亚铁磁态的有前景平台，为自旋电子学应用提供了新的可能性，展示了电场控制磁态和自旋相关输运现象的潜力。

Abstract: The recent discovery of altermagnetism has demonstrated that spin-split electronic band structures can emerge in magnetic systems with zero net magnetization. In contrast, fully compensated ferrimagnetic (fFIM) systems remain far less explored, despite exhibiting similar characteristics such as vanishing magnetization and spin-split bands. Here, based on first-principles calculations combined with theoretical analysis, we demonstrate that monolayer CoS and CoSe can be driven into fFIM states by an external electric field. These materials possess collinear antiferromagnetic ground states with out-of-plane Néel vectors, and their electronic bands are spin degenerate due to $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry. When an out-of-plane electric field is applied, $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry is broken, inducing fFIM states with pronounced spin splitting. Moreover, we show that the resulting fFIM states host fully spin-polarized currents, anomalous Hall effects, and magneto-optical Kerr and Faraday effects. Our results establish monolayer CoS and CoSe as promising platforms for electric-field-controlled fFIM states and spintronic applications.

</details>


### [197] [Domain Walls Stabilized by Intrinsic Phonon Modes and Engineered Defects Enable Robust Ferroelectricity in HfO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15041)
*Chenxi Yu,Jiajia Zhang,Xujin Song,Dijiang Sun,Shangze Li,Fei Liu,Xiaoyan Liu,Wei Xi,Jinfeng Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究开发了基于声子模式展开的统一框架，系统研究了声子模式和缺陷对HfO₂铁电体畴壁结构的影响，揭示了界面声子模式在畴壁稳定性中的关键作用，以及缺陷对铁电畴的钉扎和稳定作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO₂铁电体在AI时代具有重要应用前景，但对其畴壁在相结构稳定和极化翻转中的作用缺乏深入理解。需要从微观物理起源揭示掺杂和缺陷工程如何增强HfO₂的铁电性。

Method: 开发了基于声子模式展开的统一理论框架，结合第一性原理计算，系统研究声子模式和缺陷对畴壁结构的影响。通过EELS和STEM实验验证理论预测。

Result: 发现界面声子模式对畴壁稳定性起关键作用；缺陷能够钉扎和稳定铁电畴，从而稳定亚稳正交相并促进极化翻转。理论预测的畴结构和缺陷分布在La掺杂HfO₂铁电薄膜中得到实验证实。

Conclusion: 该研究从微观物理起源提供了对掺杂和缺陷工程增强HfO₂铁电性的新理解，建立了声子模式-缺陷-畴壁稳定性之间的内在联系，为优化HfO₂铁电性能提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ has attracted extensive research interest for its applications in AI era. The domain walls play a crucial role in phase structure stabilization and polarization switching of ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$, however, a thorough understanding is still lacking. Here, we developed a unified framework based on phonon mode expansion to systematically study the effects of phonon modes and defects on domain wall structures. Using this approach combined with first-principle calculations, we revealed that the interface phonon modes play a key role in stability of domain walls; defects pin and stabilize ferroelectric domains, which in turn stabilizes the metastable orthorhombic phase and facilitates polarization switching. This provides an insight from the microscopic physics origin into the enhanced ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ by doping and defect engineering. Furthermore, the theoretically predicted domain structures and defect distributions were observed in La-doped $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ ferroelectric films by EELS and STEM experiments, which confirms the validity of our findings.

</details>


### [198] [Persistent incommensurate amorphous/crystalline meta-interfaces enable engineering-grade superlubricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15089)
*Wan Wang,Zijun Ding,Panpan Li,Wanying Ying,Hongxuan Li,Xiaohong Liu,Huidi Zhou,Jianmin Chen,Wengen Ouyang,Li Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于非晶/晶态异质界面的材料无关策略，实现工程相关条件下的鲁棒超润滑，使用DLC/MoS2模型系统，通过激光图案化阵列和MXene增强，在毫米级接触尺寸、12.7 GPa接触压力和40%湿度下实现摩擦系数~0.008的超润滑性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦消耗全球大量能源，促使追求超润滑状态。传统方法依赖晶格失配抑制周期性能量势垒，但实际界面总存在缺陷、边缘和晶界，会恢复高摩擦状态。需要一种能在工程相关条件下实现鲁棒超润滑的新策略。

Method: 引入材料无关策略：利用非晶/晶态异质界面实现鲁棒超润滑。以类金刚石碳（DLC）和晶态MoS2为模型系统，通过实验和原子模拟验证界面在所有取向上保持非公度性。通过激光图案化DLC/MoS2元接触阵列，并用Ti3C2Tx MXene增强，构建分层界面结构。

Result: DLC/MoS2界面在所有取向上保持非公度性，摩擦时能量势垒消失。而扭曲MoS2双层易重新取向为公度高摩擦状态。激光图案化阵列在极端条件下（毫米级接触尺寸、12.7 GPa接触压力、RH 40%空气）实现摩擦系数~0.008，持续100000次循环。

Conclusion: 非晶/晶态异质界面策略通过四个协同因素实现鲁棒超润滑：界面固有非公度性、DLC支撑刚性、MXene机械增强和几何图案化负载分布。这建立了将结构超润滑从纳米级模型系统扩展到实际可持续工程技术的通用设计范式。

Abstract: Friction dissipates a substantial portion of global energy, motivating the pursuit of superlubricity, a state of near-zero friction, in real-world systems. Conventional approaches rely on crystalline lattice mismatch to suppress periodic energy barriers, but real interfaces invariably contain defects, edges and grain boundaries that restore high-friction states. Here we introduce a materials-agnostic strategy based on amorphous/crystalline heterointerfaces to achieve robust superlubricity under engineering-relevant conditions. Using diamond-like carbon (DLC) and crystalline MoS2 as a model system, we show through experiments and atomistic simulations that their interface remains incommensurate at all orientations and exhibits vanishing energy barriers during friction. In contrast, twisted MoS2 bilayers readily reorient into commensurate, high-friction states. We scale this effect by fabricating laser-patterned arrays of DLC/MoS2 meta-contacts reinforced with Ti3C2Tx MXene, forming hierarchical interfaces that sustain a friction coefficient of ~0.008 over 100000 cycles under combined extreme conditions: millimetre-scale contact size, 12.7 GPa contact pressure and RH 40% air. This unprecedented performance arises from four synergistic factors: intrinsic incommensurability at amorphous/crystalline interface, the rigidity of DLC support, MXene-based mechanical reinforcement and normalized load distribution by geometric patterning. These findings establish a general design paradigm that extends structural superlubricity from nanoscale model systems to practical technologies for sustainable engineering.

</details>


### [199] [Probing a two-dimensional soft ferromagnet Cr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$ by a tuning fork resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15123)
*Hengrui Gui,Zekai Shi,Jiawen Zhang,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan,Lin Jiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用石英音叉谐振器测量Cr₂Ge₂Te₆的磁各向异性，揭示其准二维易轴铁磁特性，为音叉基磁向测量提供理想参考系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁各向异性对理解磁性材料的自由能景观至关重要，但传统磁强计难以定量表征。需要开发新方法来直接测量磁向磁化率，并区分自旋起源各向异性和轨道磁性

Method: 采用石英音叉谐振器技术直接测量磁向磁化率，研究Cr₂Ge₂Te₆的温度、磁场和角度依赖性响应，通过准二维易轴铁磁模型进行分析

Result: Cr₂Ge₂Te₆表现出准二维易轴铁磁行为，磁向磁化率从传统的cos(2θ)形式演变为明显的凹陷结构，为音叉基磁向测量提供了理想参考系统

Conclusion: 音叉谐振器为各向异性低维磁体中的磁化旋转刚度提供了灵敏的热力学探针，为区分自旋起源各向异性和轨道磁性提供了有用框架

Abstract: Magnetic anisotropy encodes key information about the free-energy landscape of magnetic materials, but its quantitative characterization often requires probes beyond conventional magnetometry. A quartz tuning-fork resonator provides direct access to the magnetotropic susceptibility. Here we use this technique to investigate the magnetic anisotropy of the layered ferromagnet Cr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$. The temperature-, field-, and angle-dependent responses are consistently described by a quasi-two-dimensional (2D) easy-axis ferromagnetic model. In particular, the evolution of the magnetotropic susceptibility reveals how the angular profile changes from a conventional cos(2$θ$) form to a pronounced dip structure as the magnetization approaches directional saturation. These results establishCr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$ as an ideal reference system for tuning-fork-based magnetotropic measurements. More broadly, they provide a useful framework for distinguishing spin-origin anisotropy from orbital magnetism, as in the case of CsV3Sb5. Our work demonstrates that tuning-fork resonators offer a sensitive thermodynamic probe of the rotational stiffness of magnetization in anisotropic low-dimensional magnets.

</details>


### [200] [Monolithic integration of diverse crystalline thin films on diamond for near-junction thermal management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15142)
*Tiancheng Zhao,Tianqi Bai,Yang He,Wenhui Xu,Xinxin Yu,Ruochen Shi,Zhenyu Qu,Jiaxin Liu,Rui Shen,Haodong Jiang,Yeliang Wang,Jiaxin Ding,Dongchen Sui,Shibin Zhang,Lei Zhu,Ailun Yi,Kai Huang,Min Zhou,Huarui Sun,Zhonghui Li,Peng Gao,Tiangui You,Xin Ou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过多步转移印刷技术将多种单晶薄膜集成到金刚石衬底上，重点优化β-Ga₂O₃/金刚石界面，实现了149 MW m⁻² K⁻¹的超高界面热导，将MOSFET热阻降至1.58 K mm W⁻¹，为6G射频前端的热管理提供了解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G射频前端追求极致小型化和高功率，热耗散成为核心挑战。需要解决近结热管理问题，特别是高功率器件在金刚石衬底上的界面热传导问题。

Method: 采用多步转移印刷技术将β-Ga₂O₃、Si、GaN和LiTaO₃单晶薄膜单片集成到金刚石衬底上。通过超高真空退火处理β-Ga₂O₃/金刚石界面，形成原子级锐利界面和共价键合。结合振动电子能量损失谱分析和分子动力学模拟研究界面声子模式。

Result: 实现了149 MW m⁻² K⁻¹的优异界面热导，创造了金刚石基β-Ga₂O₃ MOSFET的1.58 K mm W⁻¹最低热阻记录。研究发现界面声子模式主导了超高界面热导。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个可扩展的金刚石基单片集成平台，通过界面工程解决了高功率射频前端的近结热管理挑战，为6G射频前端的热耗散问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The pursuit of extreme miniaturization and high power in 6G RF front-ends has cast thermal dissipation as the central challenge. Here, we have demonstrated the monolithic integration of functionally distinct single-crystal thin films, including \b{eta}-Ga2O3, Si, GaN, and LiTaO3, onto a single diamond substrate using a multi-step transfer printing technique. Focusing on the critical \b{eta}-Ga2O3/diamond interface, we achieve an exceptional interfacial thermal conductance (ITC) of 149 MW m-2 K-1 through ultra-high vacuum (UHV) annealing, creating an atomically sharp interface featuring covalent bonding. Vibrational electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS) analysis combining with molecular dynamics (MD) simulations reveal that distinctive interfacial phonon modes at the \b{eta}-Ga2O3/diamond heterointerface dominate ultrahigh ITC. We experimentally demonstrate that by improving the ITC, the thermal resistance (Rth) of a diamond-based \b{eta}-Ga2O3 MOSFET is driven to a record-low value of 1.58 K mm W-1, underscoring the critical role of interface engineering in near-junction thermal management for diamond-integrated devices. This work demonstrates a scalable, diamond-based monolithic integration platform designed to solve the near-junction thermal challenges in high-power RF front-ends.

</details>


### [201] [Decoding the Complexity of Ferroelectric Orthorhombic HfO2: A Unified Mode Expansion Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15162)
*Chenxi Yu,Jiajia Zhang,Xujin Song,Fei Liu,Jinfeng Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于声子模式展开的统一框架，用于研究HfO₂铁电性，将正交晶系相结构、畴壁和开关路径统一到模式分析基础上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO₂薄膜的铁电性通常归因于极性正交OIII相的形成，但该相的低对称性复杂性阻碍了对HfO₂铁电性质的研究。

Method: 开发基于声子模式展开的统一框架，将OIII相和其他正交相表示为高对称立方相加上立方声子模式的激发，将正交相的复杂性编码到模式中。

Result: 阐明了正交相稳定性的起源（来自模式能量泛函）；列举了不等价畴壁并计算其稳定判据；总结了所有可能的开关机制。

Conclusion: 该统一框架可简化畴壁结构和转变路径的研究，为从声子模式分析角度研究HfO₂铁电性提供新视角。

Abstract: The ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ thin films is widely attributed to the formation of a polar orthorhombic phase named OIII phase. However, the complexity of OIII phase originated from its low symmetry becomes an obstacle for studying ferroelectric properties of $\mathrm{HfO}_2$. Here, we developed a unified framework based on phonon mode expansion for studying ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$. In this framework, phase structures, domain walls and switching paths of orthogonal crystal system can be studied from the same basis of mode analysis. The OIII phase and other orthogonal phases can be represented by the high-symmetry cubic phase with the excitation of cubic phonon modes, into which the complexity of orthogonal phases is faithfully coded. To present the capability of this mode expansion approach, we clarified the origin of orthorhombic stability from the energy functional of modes; enumerated inequivalent domain walls and calculated their stable criteria; and summarized all possible switching mechanisms. This unified framework can be used to simplify the study of domain wall structures and transition paths. Furthermore, it can provide a new perspective for ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ from phonon mode analysis.

</details>


### [202] [A biomimetic feedback loop for sustaining self-lubrication and wear resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15181)
*Fuyan Kang,Shilin Deng,Panpan Li,Rui Zhao,Xiaohong Liu,Hongxuan Li,Huidi Zhou,Jianmin Chen,Wengen Ouyang,Li Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Cu(Au)/C纳米复合薄膜作为仿生自调节润滑剂，利用摩擦热触发软金属纳米颗粒熔化和迁移，在摩擦界面催化形成有序碳纳米结构，实现自限性反馈循环，获得超低摩擦系数和超长磨损寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够自我感知和自我调节的智能材料是可持续技术的前沿领域。传统润滑材料缺乏自适应能力，无法根据摩擦条件动态调整性能。本研究旨在创建一种仿生自调节润滑系统，利用摩擦热作为内在激活信号，实现材料的智能响应。

Method: 制备Cu(Au)/C纳米复合薄膜作为自调节润滑剂。系统利用摩擦热触发软金属纳米颗粒（如Cu或Au）的熔化和沿纳米孔的迁移。迁移到摩擦界面的金属催化原位形成有序碳纳米结构。通过实时监测摩擦系数、电阻和金属释放来验证自主调节机制。

Result: 该系统实现了自限性反馈循环：高摩擦系数产生热量，熔化金属纳米颗粒；迁移的金属通过形成低摩擦纳米结构降低摩擦系数，从而减少热量并停止进一步迁移，直到摩擦再次升高。这种机制实现了稳定的超低摩擦系数（约0.04）和优异的磨损寿命（>40 km），即使在真空中也能保持性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用摩擦热作为内在激活信号，该系统建立了一个通用的智能自调节材料范式。这种仿生自调节润滑剂不仅具有优异的摩擦学性能，而且其原理可扩展到摩擦学以外的应用领域，为智能材料设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Intelligent materials that self-sense and self-regulate are an emerging frontier in sustainable technology. Here we introduce Cu(Au)/C nanocomposite films that act as bioinspired self-adjusting lubricants. In these films, frictional heating triggers melting and migration of soft metal nanoparticles (NPs) such as Cu or Au along nano-pores to the friction interface, where the metal catalyzes the in-situ formation of ordered carbon nano-structures. Real-time monitoring of friction coefficient, electrical resistance(R), and metal release confirms an autonomous cycle: high friction coefficient generates heat, melting the metal NPs; the migrating metal then lowers friction coefficent by creating low-friction nanostructures, which reduces heat and arrests further migration until friction rises again. This self-limiting feedback enables stable ultra-low friction (~0.04) and an exceptional wear life (>40 km) even in high vacuum. By utilizing friction-derived heat as an intrinsic activation signal, our system establishes a general paradigm for intelligent, self-regulating materials with applications extending beyond tribology.

</details>


### [203] [UniMatSim: A High-Throughput Materials Simulation Automation Framework Based on Universal Machine Learning Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15294)
*Yanjin Xiang,Yihan Nie,Yunzhi Gao,Haidi Wang,Wei Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: UniMatSim是一个模块化Python框架，用于统一集成多种通用机器学习原子间势（UMLIPs），自动化从结构优化到稳定性验证的高通量材料模拟工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前UMLIPs生态系统碎片化，缺乏统一的接口标准和集成框架，阻碍了其在自动化高通量工作流中的部署应用。

Method: 开发UniMatSim框架，通过抽象接口实现多种UMLIPs（如CHGNet、M3GNet、MACE）的无缝切换，集成任务编排功能，提供弹性、声子、分子动力学等关键性质的标准化模块，并支持低维材料的自动化处理。

Result: 以2D Lieb晶格系统为测试案例，构建了多阶段高通量筛选工作流：从1,176个候选组成开始，通过四模型共识管道获得393个稳定结构，再经磁态筛选和DFT能带计算，最终得到59个具有交错磁带特性的Lieb晶格候选材料。

Conclusion: UniMatSim显著提高了计算效率和可重复性，为数据驱动的材料发现和设计提供了可靠的基础设施。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (UMLIPs) offer accuracy close to first-principles calculations at a fraction of the cost, showing significant potential for large-scale material simulations. However, the fragmented UMLIPs ecosystem lacks unified interface standards and integration frameworks, hindering their automated deployment in high-throughput workflows. To address this, we present UniMatSim, a modular Python framework. It systematically integrates various UMLIPs (e.g., CHGNet, M3GNet, MACE) and automates workflows from structural optimization to stability verification. The framework enables seamless model switching via abstracted interfaces, incorporates task orchestration, and provides standardized modules for key properties (elasticity, phonons, molecular dynamics), including automated handling for low-dimensional materials. As a test case, using the 2D Lieb lattice system, we constructed a multi-stage high-throughput screening workflow covering structural optimization, elastic stability, and phonon spectrum calculations. Starting from 1,176 candidate compositions, a four-model consensus pipeline yields 393 stable structures. These are refined by magnetic-state screening and DFT band-structure calculations to 59 Lieb-lattice candidates with staggered-magnetic-band characteristics. Results show UniMatSim significantly improves computational efficiency and reproducibility, providing a reliable infrastructure for data-driven materials discovery and design.

</details>


### [204] [Will it form a glass? Tackling glass formation using binary classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15312)
*Diogo P. L. Carvalho,Ana C. B. Loponi,Daniel R. Cassar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用随机森林分类器预测无机非金属液体在典型实验室条件下形成玻璃的概率，基于超过50,000个样本的数据集，模型在未见数据上达到ROC-AUC约0.89和PR-AUC接近0.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃形成是玻璃科学中最重要和基础性的开放问题之一。预测液体是否能容易地冻结成玻璃看似简单实则困难，本文旨在解决无机非金属液体的玻璃形成预测问题。

Method: 采用二元分类方法，使用超过50,000个样本的数据集训练随机森林分类器。实施了严格的模型选择流程，包括交叉验证的超参数调优，评估了四种不同的数据处理路径。使用SHAP值从训练好的模型中提取有价值的见解。

Result: 模型在保持数据集（未见数据）上达到ROC-AUC值约0.89和PR-AUC接近0.95。通过SHAP分析发现，组成化学元素的带隙能量与玻璃形成呈正相关。添加玻璃稳定性参数和Jezica后，性能没有改善但模型复杂度显著降低。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了预测无机非金属液体玻璃形成概率的有效分类模型。SHAP分析提供了与现有知识一致并扩展了的新见解，特别是带隙能量的正相关性。模型复杂度降低的结果对成分筛选（尤其是逆向设计问题）特别相关。

Abstract: Glass formation is one of the most important and fundamental open problems in glass science. Predicting whether a liquid can be easily frozen into a glass appears simple but is far from it. In this communication, we address glass formation in inorganic nonmetallic liquids using binary classification to predict the probability that a given liquid will form a glass under typical laboratory conditions. Using a dataset of more than 50,000 examples, we trained random forest classifiers that achieved ROC-AUC values around 0.89 and PR-AUC close to 0.95 on the holdout dataset (i.e., unseen data). A rigorous model selection routine was employed, including hyperparameter tuning with cross-validation, and four different data treatment routes were evaluated. Using SHAP values, we extracted valuable insights from the trained models that both agree with established knowledge and extend it. For example, we identified that the bandgap energy of the constituent chemical elements is positively correlated with glass formation. When glass stability parameters and Jezica were added to the dataset, no performance improvement was observed, but model complexity decreased significantly. This result is particularly relevant for composition screening, especially in inverse design problems.

</details>


### [205] [Coupled Ferroelectricity and Phonon Chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15325)
*Xiang-Bin Han,Cong Yang,Rui Sun,Xiaotong Zhang,Thuc Mai,Zhengze Xu,Aryan Jouneghaninaseri,Xiaoning Jiang,Rahul Rao,Yi Xia,Dali Sun,Jun Liu,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文首次在分子铁电体TGS中实验证明了铁电性与手性声子的耦合，实现了通过电场可逆切换晶体手性和声子角动量的电控手性声子技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手性材料（如石英）的手性和声子手性是固定的，无法切换。铁电材料具有可电切换的自发极化，若将手性与铁电极化耦合，则可实现电控晶体手性和声子角动量，这对固态自旋电子学架构中手性相关量子态的控制具有重要意义。

Method: 使用分子铁电体硫酸三甘氨酸（TGS），通过电场切换晶体手性，采用原位时间分辨磁光克尔效应测量揭示声子手性的可逆控制，结合密度泛函理论计算和圆偏振拉曼光谱验证相反的圆声子运动。

Result: 实验成功实现了声子手性的电控可逆切换：克尔旋转随电场切换而反转；声子手性在顺电相消失，在外消旋铁电态可调；DFT计算和圆偏振拉曼光谱证实了相反的圆声子运动。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了铁电性、结构手性、手性声子和自旋之间的电可寻址耦合路径，为基于铁电材料的手性声子自旋和声子控制技术开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The ability to control chirality and chiral phonons offers a route to manipulate the direction of spin and angular-momentum transport. In materials with rigid structural chirality, such as quartz, phonon chirality is fixed by the handedness and cannot be switched. By contrast, ferroelectric materials host a spontaneous polarization that can be reversibly switched by an external electric field. When chirality is coupled to this ferroelectric polarization, it enables electrical switching of crystal chirality and the associated phonon angular momentum, which is compatible with solid-state spintronic architectures, enabling control over chirality-dependent quantum states.1 Here, we report the experimental demonstration of the coupling between ferroelectricity and phonon chirality in the molecular ferroelectric triglycine sulfate. By electrically switching the crystal chirality, we achieve reversible and device-compatible control of phonon chirality, as revealed by in situ time-resolved magneto-optical Kerr effect measurements. The Kerr rotation reverses with electric-field switching, while phonon chirality vanishes in the paraelectric phase and is tunable in the racemic ferroelectric state. Furthermore, density functional theory calculations and circularly polarized Raman spectroscopy further corroborate the opposite circular phonon motions. These results establish an electrically addressable coupling pathway linking ferroelectricity, structural chirality, chiral phonons, and spin, opening a route toward chiral-phonon-enabled spin and phonon control technologies based on ferroelectric materials.

</details>


### [206] [Polar Charge-Ordered States in BiFeO$_3$/CaFeO$_3$ Superlattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15362)
*Rajan Gowsalya,Monirul Shaikh,Sathiyamoorthy Buvaneswaran,Saurabh Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: BiFeO₃/CaFeO₃超晶格通过界面电荷转移和晶格畸变实现极性电荷有序和铁电半导体行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究氧化物超晶格作为工程化电子相的平台，通过界面电荷转移和晶格畸变来调控极化、电荷有序和电子输运性质

Method: 结合第一性原理计算和对称模式分析，研究BiFeO₃/CaFeO₃超晶格的结构和电子性质

Result: 发现非中心对称Pc基态，具有Fe离子的极性电荷有序，结合C型反铁磁性和铁电半导体行为，间接带隙约0.6 eV

Conclusion: 铁酸盐超晶格是多功能材料设计的通用平台，可通过界面和应变工程可控调控极化、电荷有序和电子输运

Abstract: Oxide superlattices provide a promising route for engineering electronic phases through interfacial charge transfer and lattice distortions. Here, we investigate the structural and electronic properties of the BiFeO$_3$/CaFeO$_3$ superlattice using a combination of first-principles computations and symmetry-mode analysis. The incorporation of polar bismuth ferrite together with charge-transfer calcium ferrite generates strong lattice instabilities involving octahedral rotations and FeO$_6$ breathing distortions. Their cooperative coupling stabilizes a non-centrosymmetric $Pc$ ground state characterized by polar charge ordering of Fe ions. The resulting phase combines C-type antiferromagnetism with ferroelectric semiconductor behavior, featuring an indirect band gap of about 0.6 eV. Our findings establish ferrite superlattices as a versatile platform for designing multifunctional materials where polarization, charge ordering, and electronic transport can be controllably manipulated through interface and strain engineering.

</details>


### [207] [Epitaxial growth of topological insulator $β$-Ag2Te thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15406)
*Ayuki Takegawa,Kouya Imoto,Minoru Kawamura,Moeta Tsukamoto,Ryutaro Yoshimi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文报道了通过分子束外延技术生长β-Ag2Te薄膜，证实其为拓扑绝缘体，表面具有二维金属导电性而体态绝缘


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ag2Te最近被确认为拓扑绝缘体，需要建立可靠的外延生长方法以获得高质量薄膜，为研究表面狄拉克态和设计异质结器件提供平台

Method: 采用分子束外延技术，先在室温下在InP衬底上沉积Ag，然后在高温下供应Te，通过X射线衍射和透射电子显微镜分析晶体结构和外延排列

Result: 成功生长出(002)晶向的β-Ag2Te外延薄膜，电输运测量显示薄膜具有二维金属导电特性，而体态保持绝缘

Conclusion: 获得的外延β-Ag2Te薄膜为研究表面狄拉克态引起的涌现现象和设计异质结器件结构提供了可行平台

Abstract: We report epitaxial growth of $β$-Ag2Te thin films by molecular beam epitaxy. $β$-Ag2Te, recently identified as a topological insulator, was grown by depositing Ag on InP substrate at room temperature followed by Te supply at elevated temperature. X-ray diffraction measurements and transmission electron microscopy analyses confirmed the (002) crystal orientation and the epitaxial atomic arrangement of $β$-Ag2Te thin films. Electrical transport measurements revealed that the $β$-Ag2Te thin film exhibits two-dimensional metallic conduction while the bulk remains insulating. The epitaxial $β$-Ag2Te thin films obtained here provide a viable platform for investigating emergent phenomena arising from surface Dirac states and for designing heterojunction-based device structures.

</details>


### [208] [Microstructural characteristics, atomic-scale features, and growth mechanisms of deuterides (hydrides) in hafnium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15420)
*Di Wang,Catriona M McGilvery,James O Douglas,Siyang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用多种显微技术表征了Hf合金中氘化物-基体界面的结构与化学性质，为核反应堆用氢化物-金属复合材料的设计提供关键界面信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铪氢化物是下一代核反应堆（特别是快裂变控制棒和聚变系统屏蔽）的有前景材料，但其本征脆性促使其以氢化物-金属复合材料形式使用。这些复合材料的功能和机械性能受氢化物-基体界面多尺度结构的强烈影响，因此需要深入理解界面特性。

Method: 采用多种显微技术组合：扫描电子显微镜（带电子背散射衍射）、透射电子显微镜（带电子能量损失谱）和原子探针层析技术，对氘化处理的Hf合金中氘化物-基体界面进行系统表征。

Result: 获得了氘化物-基体界面的关键信息：晶体学取向关系、微观结构特征、失配诱导位错分布、电子能量损失特性，以及氘化物生长过程中的氧偏聚现象。

Conclusion: 这些发现有助于阐明界面演化机制，增进对氢化物-金属系统的理解，对核应用中这类材料的加工、性能和设计具有潜在指导意义。

Abstract: Hafnium hydride is a promising material for next-generation nuclear reactors, particularly as control rods for fast fission and shielding in fusion systems. The material's intrinsic brittleness encourages its use in the form of hydride-metal composites, where the functional and mechanical performance is strongly influenced by the multiscale structure of hydride-matrix interfaces. In this study, we employ a suite of microscopy techniques, including scanning electron microscopy with electron backscatter diffraction, transmission electron microscopy with electron energy-loss spectroscopy, and atom probe tomography, to investigate the deuteride-matrix interfaces in a deuterium-charged Hf alloy. We characterise their structure and chemistry, extracting key information including the deuteride-matrix crystallographic orientation relationship, microstructural features, misfit-induced dislocation distributions, electron energy-loss characteristics, and the segregation of oxygen during deuteride growth. These findings help clarify the mechanisms of interface evolution and may contribute to improved understanding of hydride-metal systems, with potential relevance for their processing, performance, and design in nuclear applications.

</details>


### [209] [Understanding early stages of low-temperature hydrogen-driven direct co-reduction of Fe-Ni mixed oxide thin films at the near atomic scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15456)
*Emmanuel Uwayezu,Shaolou Wei,Yujiao Li,Johannes D. Bartl,Dierk Raabe,Alfred Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过氢直接还原Fe50Ni50Ox薄膜，揭示了多相氧化物低温还原的早期动力学机制，发现还原过程受晶界成核控制，涉及相分离为富Ni金属相和磁铁矿，最终形成近等原子FeNi合金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多相氧化物氢共还原的动力学机制对于提高合金生产的可持续性和向氢基经济转型具有重要意义。薄膜平台为在近原子尺度研究这些过程提供了理想模型，可以精确控制前驱体结构和还原条件。

Method: 使用Fe50Ni50Ox薄膜（包含NiFe2O4和NiO相）作为模型体系，在280°C纯H2中进行不同时间的还原。采用原子探针层析成像（APT）等多种表征技术分析结构、形貌和纳米尺度变化。

Result: 低温还原是成核限制过程，具有超过5分钟的孕育期，晶界成核先于体相还原。APT显示早期还原涉及相分离为富Ni的FexNiy金属相和转化的剩余氧化物（磁铁矿Fe3O4）。进一步还原导致磁铁矿还原并合金化为近等原子FeNi合金。

Conclusion: 薄膜纳米结构和Ni的自催化效应（通过合金化和氢溢流）促进了低温还原和合金化。该研究为直接从氧化物低温制备成分可调的Fe-Ni合金薄膜开辟了新途径，并将多相氧化物氢直接还原的研究扩展到薄膜平台。

Abstract: Kinetic understanding of hydrogen co-reduction of multinary and multi-phase oxides is of interest for enhancing sustainability of alloy production and transition to a hydrogen-based economy. Benefits include decrease in energy consumption, enhanced kinetics, and conversion of oxides to alloys. Thin films provide a platform to study these processes as reactive co-deposition from multiple elemental, alloy or compound targets and precise oxygen flow control allow atomic mixing into various oxide phases which are well-defined nanoscale precursor structures for the subsequent reduction study at the near atomic scale. The early stages of hydrogen direct reduction of oxide thin films are investigated using a Fe50Ni50Ox thin film consisting of NiFe2O4 and NiO phases. After reduction at 280 C in pure H2 for different times, structural, morphological, and nanoscale changes were examined by different characterisation methods including atom probe tomography (APT). The low-temperature reduction is nucleation-limited marked by grain-boundary nucleation preceded by an incubation time of more than 5 min. APT revealed that the early-stages of the reduction involves phase separation into a Ni-rich FexNiy metallic phase and a transformed remaining oxide (magnetite, Fe3O4). Further reduction induces magnetite reduction and alloying into a nearly equiatomic FeNi alloy. The low-temperature reduction and alloying are facilitated by synergetic effects from the nanostructure of the film, and Ni autocatalytic effects through alloying and hydrogen spillover. The results pave the way for low-temperature formation of Fe-Ni alloy thin films with tunable compositions directly from oxides, and broaden the scope of hydrogen direct reduction of multinary oxides to thin-film platforms.

</details>


### [210] [High-Throughput Computational Exploration of MOFs for Short-Chain PFAS Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15503)
*Mengru Zhang,Satyanarayana Bonakala,Taku Watanabe,Karim Hamzaoui,Guillaume Maurin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发混合高通量计算筛选策略，结合经典力场和机器学习势函数，从MOF数据库中筛选出对短链PFAS（以PFBA为代表）具有高选择性和吸附性能的材料


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短链PFAS因持久性、迁移性强且对传统吸附剂亲和力弱而难以从水中去除，需要开发高效吸附材料

Method: 采用混合高通量计算筛选策略：从MOF数据库开始，先使用UFF力场进行蒙特卡洛模拟初步筛选，再用u-MLIP机器学习势函数重新评估，考虑框架柔性并排除不利的水-框架相互作用

Result: 筛选出四种高性能MOF，在PFBA吸附强度、PFBA/水选择性、可持续性、水稳定性和合成可行性方面达到最佳平衡

Conclusion: 结合经典力场和u-MLIP可实现可扩展且定量准确的MOF吸附筛选，为短链PFAS吸附剂的理性设计建立可转移原则

Abstract: Short-chain per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFASs) are increasingly replacing regulated long-chain PFASs, yet they remain challenging to remove from water due to their high persistence, mobility, and weak affinity toward conventional adsorbents. In this work, we developed a hybrid high-throughput computational screening (HTCS) strategy to identify high-performance MOFs for the selective adsorption of perfluorobutanoic acid (PFBA), a representative short-chain PFAS, from water. The workflow begins with a curated MOF dataset and employs Monte Carlo (MC) simulations based on synergistic use of a classical universal force field (UFF) and a universal machine-learned interatomic potential (u-MLIP), enabling scalable and quantitatively accurate prediction of adsorption across large MOF databases. A set of promising MOFs initially identified using UFF-based HTCS, that combine strong PFBA affinity and high PFBA/H2O selectivity were re-evaluated with u-MLIP to refine adsorption predictions and to assess guest-induced framework flexibility, enabling the exclusion of materials with unfavourable high water-framework interactions. Ultimately, four high-performance MOFs were identified that optimally balance strong PFBA interactions, high PFBA selectivity over water, and practical considerations including sustainability, water stability, and synthetic feasibility. This study demonstrates that combining classical force fields with u-MLIPs enables scalable, quantitatively accurate MOF adsorption screening and establishes transferable principles for the rational design of adsorbents targeting short-chain PFAS.

</details>


### [211] [Engineering van der Waals heterostructures for dispersion-selective meV-scale quantum sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15581)
*Elizabeth A. Peterson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用狄拉克材料范德华异质结构的选择性粒子探测技术，通过调控轨道杂化实现基于色散关系的粒子筛选，为下一代meV级量子传感器提供新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子传感技术面临的关键挑战是难以区分目标粒子信号与声子、磁振子等本征激发信号。需要开发能够基于粒子色散关系进行选择性探测的新方法，以推动量子信息应用和基础物理研究。

Method: 利用ZrTe5和HfTe5等层状狄拉克材料的范德华异质结构，通过应变和层数调控界面轨道杂化，使用第一性原理密度泛函理论(DFT)计算分析电子结构变化，设计基于色散关系的粒子过滤器。

Result: DFT计算表明，通过调控应变和层数可以成功调节狄拉克材料异质结构中的轨道杂化，为构建基于色散关系的粒子过滤器提供了原理验证，展示了狄拉克材料在meV级量子传感器中的可行性。

Conclusion: 狄拉克材料范德华异质结构能够通过轨道杂化调控实现基于色散关系的粒子选择性探测，为开发下一代meV级量子传感器提供了有前景的技术路径，有望解决当前量子传感中的信号区分难题。

Abstract: Quantum sensing of meV-scale scattering and absorption of impinging particles with electrons in solid state detectors is a challenging technological advancement with the potential to enable breakthroughs in quantum information applications and studies of fundamental physics. However, a key obstacle for current sensing schemes is the difficulty in distinguishing the signals from particles of interest and from intrinsic excitations, like phonons or magnons. Here we propose a technique to selectively detect impinging particles based not only on their imparted energy, but specifically by their dispersion relations. By harnessing interfacial orbital hybridization in van der Waals heterostructures of Dirac materials, interlayer charge transfer may be promoted only for pre-selected impinging particles of interest. Using first-principles density functional theory (DFT) calculations of heterostructures of the layered Dirac materials ZrTe5 and HfTe5, we examine the effects of strain and layer number for successfully tuning orbital hybridization in their electronic structure. We demonstrate a proof-or-principle feasibility study for using Dirac materials to construct dispersion filters to be leveraged for next-generation meV-scale quantum sensors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Hierarchical structure of primary and hybridization-induced superconducting correlations in bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13604)
*Hiroshi Watanabe,Hirofumi Sakakibara,Kazuhiko Kuroki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过变分蒙特卡洛方法揭示了双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇中s±超导配对的微观机制，发现配对主要源于Ni 3d_z²轨道的键合-反键合分裂，而轨道杂化将超导关联重新分配到d_x²-y²通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压下双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇的超导转变温度接近80K，引发了关于其微观起源的激烈争论。虽然s±能隙对称性被广泛提出，但负责配对的电子自由度仍未确定。需要澄清配对相互作用的来源以及不同轨道通道在超导中的作用。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛方法研究双层两轨道Hubbard模型，分析Ni 3d_z²和d_x²-y²轨道的相互作用。通过计算键合-反键合分裂效应和轨道杂化对超导关联的重新分布，揭示配对的分层结构。

Result: 发现配对相互作用主要源于Ni 3d_z²轨道的键合-反键合分裂，而轨道杂化将超导关联重新分配到d_x²-y²通道，尽管其本征配对相互作用较弱。这解释了为什么两个轨道通道表现出可比的长程关联。得到的s±态对费米面拓扑变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果调和了看似竞争的理论方案，提供了对多层关联超导体中轨道杂化独特作用的全面理解。配对起源与超导关联的分离解释了双层镍酸盐中s±超导态的微观机制。

Abstract: High-pressure superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, with a transition temperature approaching 80 K, has stimulated intense debate regarding its microscopic origin. Although an $s_{\pm}$ gap symmetry has been widely proposed, the electronic degrees of freedom responsible for pairing remain unsettled. Here we investigate a bilayer two-orbital Hubbard model using the variational Monte Carlo method and reveal a hierarchical pairing structure in bilayer nickelates. The primary pairing interaction originates from the bonding--antibonding splitting of the Ni $3d_{z^2}$ orbitals, while orbital hybridization redistributes superconducting correlations to the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ channel despite its weak intrinsic pairing interaction. This distinction between the origin of pairing and resulting superconducting correlations explains why the two orbital channels exhibit comparable long-range correlations. The resulting $s_{\pm}$ state is robust against changes in Fermi-surface topology. These results reconcile apparently competing theoretical scenarios and provide a comprehensive understanding, highlighting the distinctive role of orbital hybridization in multilayer correlated superconductors.

</details>


### [213] [Superhydrides on the way to ambient pressure: weak localization and persistent X-ray photoconductivity in BaSiH$_{8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14051)
*Dmitrii V. Semenok,Di Zhou,Sven Luther,Toni Helm,Hirokazu Kadobayashi,Yuki Nakamoto,Katsuya Shimizu,Kirill S. Pervakov,Andrei V. Sadakov,Oleg A. Sobolevskiy,Vladimir M. Pudalov,Simone Di Cataldo,Roman Lucrezi,Lilia Boeri,Michele Galasso,Frederico G. Alabarse,Ivan A. Troyan,Viktor V. Struzhkin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Ba-Si-H体系中合成了立方BaSiH₈，该材料在18-31 GPa压力下合成后可在常压下保持稳定，具有从超导到半导体/弱金属的压致相变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低超氢化物的稳定压力是氢饱和化合物化学中最重要挑战之一，本研究旨在探索Ba-Si-H体系在高压下的行为，寻找可在常压下稳定的超氢化物。

Method: 在0-142 GPa压力范围和4-317 K温度范围内，采用输运测量、¹H核磁共振、单晶和粉末X射线衍射等方法研究Ba-Si-H体系，合成立方BaSiH₈。

Result: 成功在18-31 GPa压力下合成立方BaSiH₈，该材料可在减压至常压后保持稳定并从金刚石压砧中回收。在142 GPa下表现出金属性和超导性（T_c=9 K，B_c2(0)=13-16 T）；在50 GPa以下表现为简并半导体（带隙<0.4 meV）或弱金属，具有弱电子局域化、负磁阻、光伏效应和X射线/可见光范围内的持续光电导性。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了可在常压下稳定的Ba-Si多氢化物的高压合成，克服了超氢化物化学中的关键瓶颈，为氢储存的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reducing the stabilization pressure of superhydrides represents one of the most important challenges in hydrogen-saturated compound chemistry. Moving in this direction, we studied the Ba-Si-H system at 0-142 GPa using transport measurements, 1H nuclear magnetic resonance, single-crystal and powder X-ray diffraction in the temperature range of 4-317 K. We synthesized the previously predicted cubic BaSiH$_{8}$ at pressures of 18-31 GPa. Remarkably, we demonstrate that BaSiH$_8$ remains stable upon decompression to ambient conditions and can be recovered from the diamond anvil cell. Obtained Ba-Si polyhydrides exhibit metallic and superconducting properties ($\textit{T$_c$}$ = 9 K, $\textit{B$_{c2}$}$(0)=13-16 T) at 142 GPa. However, at pressures below 50 GPa, these hydrides behave as degenerate semiconductors (bandgap < 0.4 meV) or poor metals with weak electron localization, negative magnetoresistance, photovoltaic effect, and persistent photoconductivity in the X-ray and visible range. Our work demonstrates the high-pressure synthesis of Ba-Si polyhydrides that remain stable upon decompression to ambient conditions, overcoming a critical bottleneck in superhydride chemistry and establishing a foundation for practical applications in hydrogen storage.

</details>


### [214] [The stripe state at 1/8 Ba doping hosts optimal superconductivity in La-214 cuprates under low in-plane stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14108)
*V. Sazgari,S. S. Islam,M. Lamotte,J. N. Graham,O. Gerguri,P. Kràl,I. Maetsu,T. Shiroka,G. Simutis,R. Khasanov,R. Sarkar,A. Steppke,N. A. Shepelin,M. Müller,M. Bartkowiak,M. Janoschek,J. Chang,H. H. Klauss,T. Adachi,G. D. Gu,J. M. Tranquada,H. Luetkens,Z. Guguchia*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: LBCO-0.125在面内单轴应力作用下，超导转变温度从5K大幅提升至37K，同时LTT相被完全抑制，静态自旋条纹有序体积分数减少，表明条纹相关相互作用增强配对强度，而静态条纹有序主要与超导相位相干竞争而非配对本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LBCO体系在1/8掺杂异常附近的应力敏感性，特别是x=0.125的共格相在对称选择性晶格调控下的响应，以理解条纹有序与超导性的竞争机制。

Method: 结合μ子自旋旋转(μSR)、交流磁化率和电阻率测量，研究LBCO-0.125在45°方向施加面内单轴应力下的超导性、自旋条纹有序和结构响应；同时对x=0.115和0.135进行电阻率测量以追踪异常两侧的演化。

Result: LBCO-0.125在0.5 GPa应力下，体超导转变温度从5K大幅提升至37K；自旋条纹有序起始温度仅轻微下降，但磁体积分数减少约一半；所有掺杂样品的LTT相相关电阻率峰被完全抑制；x=0.125在应力下达到最高Tc，实现37K的零电阻态和46K的超导转变起始温度。

Conclusion: LTT相的抑制和静态自旋条纹有序体积分数的减少是发展最佳三维超导性的关键；条纹相关相互作用增强配对强度，而静态条纹有序主要与超导相位相干竞争而非配对本身，这解释了x=0.125在应力下表现出最高Tc的现象。

Abstract: The cuprate system La$_{2-x}$Ba$_{x}$CuO$_{4}$ (LBCO) exhibits a pronounced sensitivity to in-plane uniaxial stress, particularly near the 1/8 doping anomaly, where stripe order strongly suppresses bulk superconductivity. While previous studies have focused on compositions close to 0.125, the commensurate $x$=0.125 phase remains largely unexplored under symmetry-selective lattice tuning. Here, we combine muon-spin rotation ($μ$SR), AC susceptibility, and electrical resistivity to investigate superconductivity, spin-stripe order, and structural response in LBCO-0.125 under in-plane uniaxial stress applied 45$^\circ$ to the Cu-O bond direction. Complementary resistivity measurements on $x$=0.115 and 0.135 track the evolution across both sides of the anomaly. We observe a giant enhancement of the bulk superconducting transition temperature in LBCO-0.125, increasing from 5 K to 37 K under 0.5 GPa. While the onset temperature of spin-stripe order decreases only modestly, the magnetic volume fraction is reduced by about a factor of two, with local order preserved. Simultaneously, the resistivity peak associated with the LTT phase is fully suppressed across all dopings. These results demonstrate that suppression of the LTT phase and reduction of the static spin-stripe-ordered volume fraction are crucial for the development of optimal three-dimensional superconductivity. Strikingly, the composition $x$=0.125, with the most robust stripe stability and the lowest ambient-pressure $T_{\rm c}$ develops the highest $T_{\rm c}$ under stress, reaching a zero-resistance state at 37 K and an onset of the superconducting transition as high as 46 K. This behavior indicates that stripe-related interactions enhance pairing strength, while static stripe order competes with superconductivity primarily at the level of phase coherence rather than pairing itself.

</details>


### [215] [Quantum Interference Breaks Bias Symmetry at Extended Superconducting Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14329)
*Vishal Tripathi,Goutam Sheet*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 粒子-空穴对称性在有限空间界面上不保证偏压对称输运，量子干涉导致本征偏压不对称电导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量的粒子-空穴对称性会强制超导界面处的偏压对称输运，但作者发现这一预期在有限空间界面上普遍失效

Method: 采用保持精确粒子-空穴对称性的紧束缚散射形式，分析电子和空穴在扩展界面传播中积累的不等相位，将界面建模为有效安德烈夫干涉仪

Result: 扩展界面导致电子和空穴积累不等相位，产生本征偏压不对称电导；界面作为安德烈夫干涉仪表现出特征阻尼振荡；超导能隙作为尖锐交叉尺度主导偏压依赖性

Conclusion: 偏压不对称性可作为干涉测量和光谱学探针，用于探测非局域界面物理和超导能标，特别适用于扩展界面不可避免的混合和拓扑系统

Abstract: Particle-hole symmetry of the Bogoliubov-de~Gennes Hamiltonian is widely assumed to enforce bias-symmetric transport at superconducting interfaces. We show that this expectation fails generically for interfaces with finite spatial extent due to quantum interference. Using a tight-binding scattering formalism that preserves exact particle-hole symmetry, we demonstrate that propagation through an extended interface causes electrons and holes to accumulate unequal phases, leading to intrinsic bias-asymmetric conductance. The interface thereby acts as an effective Andreev interferometer with characteristic damped oscillations arising from coherent multiple reflections within the barrier. While the asymmetry originates from normal-state interference, its bias dependence is governed by the superconducting gap, which emerges as a sharp crossover scale that can be clearly resolved even when conventional coherence peaks are weak or absent. Thus we present bias asymmetry as an interferometric, spectroscopic probe of nonlocal interface physics and superconducting energy scales in hybrid and topological systems where extended interfaces are unavoidable.

</details>


### [216] [A Unified Understanding of the Experimental Controlling of the T$_\text{c}$ of Bilayer Nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14519)
*Zeyu Chen,Jia-Heng Ji,Yu-Bo Liu,Ming Zhang,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究基于双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇的超导转变温度(T_c)调控实验，提出统一的强耦合t-J∥-J⊥模型解释，揭示J⊥变化是影响T_c的主要机制，并预测电子掺杂和增强压应变可提升T_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，通过稀土Sm/Nd替代、压力调控、薄膜压应变以及空穴掺杂等多种实验手段调控双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇超导转变温度的实验引起了广泛关注。这些实验现象需要统一的理论框架来解释，特别是理解不同调控手段背后的共同物理机制。

Method: 采用先前提出的最小单d_{x²-y²}轨道双层t-J∥-J⊥模型，结合密度泛函理论计算获取不同实验条件下的模型参数。采用slave-boson平均场和密度矩阵重整化群相结合的方法求解模型，并与实验对比。同时进行弱耦合随机相位近似(RPA)的对比研究。

Result: 1. 压力下体材料T_c随Sm/Nd替代比例增加而增强；2. 体材料T_c-压力关系呈圆顶形曲线；3. 薄膜T_c随压应变增强而增加；4. 空穴掺杂导致T_c随掺杂水平δ增加而降低，源于d_{x²-y²}轨道态密度减少。这些结果与实验定性一致，且发现T_c变化主要源于J⊥随实验条件的变化。

Conclusion: 强耦合t-J∥-J⊥模型为双层镍酸盐的超导调控实验提供了自然的统一理解框架。研究预测通过高价元素替代La实现电子掺杂，或进一步增强薄膜压应变，可有效提升T_c。强耦合模型比弱耦合RPA方法更能自然地解释实验现象。

Abstract: Recently, a series of experiments which control the T$_\text{c}$ of the bilayer nickelates La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ through varying environmental conditions, including the rare-earth Sm/Nd substitution, the pressure on the bulk material, the compressive strain on the film and the hole doping through over-oxidation or alkaline earth element substitution have caught great interests. Here, we provide a unified understanding toward all these experiments based on the minimal single $d_{x^2-y^2}$-orbital bilayer $t-J_\parallel-J_\perp$ model proposed previously. With model parameters input from density-functional-theoretical calculations under varying experimental conditions, we adopt combined slave-boson-mean-field and density-matrix-renormalization-group approaches to solve the model and compare with experiments. Our results yield that, the bulk T$_\text{c}$ under pressure enhances with the Sm/Nd substitution fraction, the bulk T$_\text{c}$-pressure relation takes a dome shaped curve, the T$_\text{c}$ of the thin film enhances with compressive strain. The obtained parameters dependence of T$_\text{c}$ in these three experiments mainly originates from the variation of $J_\perp$ with experimental conditions. As for the hole doping, our results provide that T$_\text{c}$ decreases with the hole doping level $δ$, due to reduced density of state for the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-orbital. All these results are qualitatively consistent with experiments. We further conduct a comparative weak-coupling random-phase-approximation (RPA) based study on these experiments and find that our strong-coupling $t-J_\parallel-J_\perp$ model provides a more natural understanding of the experiments. We propose that electron doping implemented through substitution of La by element with higher valence, or further enhancement of the compressive strain in the film, can enhance T$_\text{c}$.

</details>


### [217] [Probing the Meissner effect in single crystals of $\mathbf{Bi_2Sr_2Ca_2Cu_3O_{10+δ}}$ via wide-field quantum microscopy under high pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15102)
*Masahiro Ohkuma,Ryo Matsumoto,Shintaro Adachi,Shinobu Onoda,Takao Watanabe,Kenji Ohta,Yoshihiko Takano,Keigo Arai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究使用不同压力传递介质（cBN和KBr）研究了Bi-2223超导体的压力依赖性，发现超导转变对压力环境高度敏感，在KBr中可维持至23GPa，而在cBN中11GPa以上即消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于Bi-2223高压研究存在矛盾结果：流体介质中Tc可能回升，而固体介质中则出现绝缘性转变。缺乏不同压力传递介质的直接比较研究。

Method: 采用基于氮空位中心的宽场量子显微镜技术，在高压下探测磁响应，分别使用cBN（立方氮化硼）和KBr（溴化钾）作为压力传递介质。

Result: 在KBr介质中，70K附近的抗磁响应（指示超导转变）可维持至23GPa；而在cBN介质中，该响应在11GPa和70K以上消失。表明Bi-2223对压力环境高度敏感。

Conclusion: 研究证实了压力环境对Bi-2223超导性质的关键影响，强调了静水压力在铜氧化物超导体研究中的重要性，解释了先前研究结果的矛盾性。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure dependence of the superconducting transition temperature ($T_{\rm c}$) in optimally doped Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{10+δ}$ (Bi-2223) single crystals using different pressure-transmitting media. Previous high-pressure studies have reported conflicting behaviors, ranging from a resurgence of $T_{\rm c}$ of optimally doped Bi-2223 in fluid media to an insulating-like transition in solid media. However, a direct comparison of the effects of different pressure-transmitting media is lacking. Here, we employed wide-field quantum microscopy based on nitrogen-vacancy centers to probe the magnetic response under high pressure, utilizing cBN and KBr as media. We observed that a diamagnetic response near 70 K, indicative of the superconducting transition, persisted up to 23 GPa in KBr, whereas it disappeared above 11 GPa and 70 K in cBN. These results demonstrate the high sensitivity of Bi-2223 to the pressure environment and highlight the critical role of hydrostatic pressure in cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [218] [Hidden Zeeman Field in Odd-Parity Magnets: An Ideal Platform for Topological Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15147)
*Xun-Jiang Luo,Zi-Ting Sun,Xilin Feng,Mingliang Tian,K. T. Law*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文揭示了奇宇称磁体（OPMs）中隐藏的塞曼场效应，纠正了以往研究仅关注非相对论性自旋分裂（NSS）而忽略时间反演对称性破缺的误解，建立了OPMs作为实现无外场拓扑超导体的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇宇称磁体作为一类非常规磁性材料，其基本理解仍不完整。以往研究仅关注非相对论性自旋分裂，而忽略了磁序本身固有的时间反演对称性破缺。本文旨在揭示这种对称性破缺带来的隐藏塞曼场效应，并探索其在拓扑超导体中的应用潜力。

Method: 通过解析的f波磁体模型，证明非相对论性自旋分裂微观上源于一个涌现的规范场，表现为实空间的自旋环流序。利用OPMs中大的NSS（eV尺度）使常规超导性与隐藏塞曼场共存，构建了支持大拓扑区域的拓扑超导体平台。

Result: 发现OPMs普遍存在源于时间反演对称性破缺的隐藏塞曼场，该场从根本上重塑了能带结构。大的NSS（eV尺度）使常规超导性能与数百meV的塞曼分裂稳健共存。基于OPMs设计了一系列拓扑超导体，支持包括单向Majorana边缘态在内的不同Majorana边界模式。

Conclusion: 本文纠正了关于奇宇称磁体的一个基本误解，揭示了其隐藏的塞曼场效应，建立了OPMs作为实现无外场且稳健的拓扑超导体的多功能平台。这一发现为拓扑超导体的实际应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Odd-parity magnets (OPMs) have emerged as a fundamental class of unconventional magnetisms, characterized by time-reversal-preserving non-relativistic spin splitting (NSS). Despite growing interest, the fundamental understanding of OPMs remains critically incomplete, as previous studies have focused exclusively on NSS while overlooking the intrinsically broken time-reversal symmetry ($\mathcal{T}$) inherent to magnetic order. In this work, we reveal that OPMs universally host a hidden Zeeman field rooted in this $\mathcal{T}$-breaking, which fundamentally reshapes their band structure. Through an analytical $f$-wave magnet model, we show that NSS microscopically originates from an emergent gauge field, manifesting as a real-space spin loop current order. Crucially, the large NSS (eV scale) enables conventional superconductivity to coexist robustly with the hidden Zeeman field, with Zeeman splitting reaches hundreds of meV. This unique band structure establishes OPMs as an ideal platform for topological superconductors (TSCs), supporting large topological regions. Based on OPMs, we engineer a series of TSCs hosting distinct Majorana boundary modes, including unidirectional Majorana edge states. Our work corrects a fundamental misconception about OPMs and establishes them as a versatile platform for field-free and robust TSCs.

</details>


### [219] [Quench Protection in Insulated REBCO Conductors: Design Optimization and Fast Detection via REBCO SQD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15244)
*Hajar Zgour,Walid Abdel Maksoud,Bertrand Baudouy,Antoine Guinet*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 比较两种保护绝缘REBCO导体在失超时的方案：铜稳定剂优化和共绕REBCO超导失超探测器，通过THEA模拟验证两者都能有效降低热点温度并实现早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法国PEPR SupraFusion项目框架下，开发基于高温超导体的聚变能演示装置面临绝缘REBCO导体失超保护的挑战：正常区传播慢、验证延迟导致热点温度达到破坏性水平。

Method: 比较两种保护方法：(1) 优化铜稳定剂截面设计；(2) 采用共绕的REBCO超导失超探测器（SQD），该探测器电气隔离但热耦合，通过故意脱氧降低Tc和Ic以实现更早的失超检测。使用一维THEA模型进行模拟分析。

Result: THEA模拟显示：合适的稳定剂截面选择能在失超事件中保持热点温度在安全限值内；REBCO SQD的使用能在更低的温度下实现失超检测。

Conclusion: 两种方法都能有效解决绝缘REBCO导体失超保护问题：铜稳定剂优化通过热管理控制温度，而REBCO SQD通过早期检测机制提供保护，为聚变能应用中的高温超导系统提供了可行的保护策略。

Abstract: This work was conducted within the framework of the exploratory French project PEPR SupraFusion, which aims to advance the field of fusion energy by developing High-Temperature Superconductor (HTS)-based demonstrators capable of storing significant energy while operating under high magnetic fields and currents. Ensuring a reliable protection during a quench in Insulated REBCO conductors is challenging\,: slow normal-zone propagation and validation delays allow the hotspot's temperature to reach damaging levels. We compare (i) conductor protection via copper-stabilizer optimization and (ii) a co-wound, REBCO superconducting quench detector (SQD) that is electrically isolated yet thermally coupled and intentionally deoxygenated to lower Tc and Ic for an earlier transition. Onedimensional THEA modeling shows that a good choice of stabilizer cross-section makes the protection possible during quench events by keeping the temperature of the hotspot within a safe limit. The simulations also demonstrate that the use of a REBCO SQD enables the quench detection at lower temperatures.

</details>


### [220] [First-principles prediction of high-temperature superconductivity in stretched carbon nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15305)
*Hua-Zhen Li,Xun-Wang Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 碳纳米管在单轴拉伸应变下可实现高温超导，临界温度在4.5%应变时达到峰值162K


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准一维系统的超导性是一个重要但被低估的研究领域，特别是碳纳米管在应变调控下的超导特性

Method: 基于电子-声子耦合机制，系统研究准一维碳纳米管在单轴拉伸应变下的超导性

Result: 计算得到的超导临界温度在4.5%单轴拉伸应变时达到峰值162K，远高于未应变碳纳米管；声子整体软化、强电子-声子耦合以及费米能级处电子态密度增加是关键因素

Conclusion: 拉伸是调控一维材料超导性的有效方法，更重要的是表明碳纳米管中可能出现高温超导现象

Abstract: Superconductivity in quasi-one-dimensional systems is an significant but undervalued research field. In this work, based on the electron-phonon coupling mechanism, we systematically investigate the superconductivity in quasi-one-dimensional carbon nanotube under uniaxial tensile strain. The calculated superconducting critical temperature attains its peak value of 162 K at a uniaxial tensile strain of 4.5\%, being drastically higher than the counterpart in the unstrained carbon nanotube. An overall softening of phonons, strong electron-phonon coupling, and an increase of electronic density of states at the Fermi level, play key roles in achieving high-temperature superconductivity in this system. Our research demonstrates that stretching is an effective approach to modulating the superconductivity one-dimensional materials, and more importantly, indicates that high-temperature superconductivity may occur in carbon nanotubes.

</details>


### [221] [Effect of pulse duration on current-induced selective oxygen migration in high-Tc superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15509)
*Fridrich Egyenes,Daniel Stoffels,Stefan Marinkovic,Bernd Aichner,Huidong Li,Anna Palau,Milan Tapajna,Wolfgang Lang,Alejandro V. Silhanek*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究探索了脉冲宽度对YBCO薄膜中电迁移起始电流的影响，发现当脉冲宽度小于10μs时，电迁移起始电流迅速增加，表明电迁移过程变得更加非热主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要局限于毫秒或更长时间尺度的脉冲激励，其中热效应无疑会影响电迁移过程。本研究旨在探索脉冲宽度对电迁移起始电流的影响，特别是在更短时间尺度下电迁移过程的非热特性。

Method: 通过实验测量YBCO桥结构中电迁移起始电流与脉冲宽度的关系，覆盖200ns到1ms的范围。同时使用分析和数值方法估算局部温度变化，以区分热效应和非热效应的影响。

Result: 当脉冲宽度δt减小到约10μs以下时，电迁移起始电流I_EM迅速增加。温度分析表明，随着脉冲缩短，局部温度降低，电迁移过程变得更加非热主导。

Conclusion: 短脉冲下的电迁移过程主要由非热机制主导，这对忆阻器操作和薄膜中热磁不稳定性的描述具有重要意义。研究结果为理解不同时间尺度下电迁移机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: High current densities can induce the directional diffusion of atoms in metallic films. In YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-δ}$ (YBCO), this electromigration process selectively acts on oxygen atoms lying in the Cu-O chains, permitting to vary the oxygen concentration in a targeted spot of high current density. This approach has proven successful in mapping the phase diagram of the material as a function of carrier concentration or as a way to manufacture memristive devices owing to its reversibility under small bipolar excitations. Thus far, most of the investigations have been limited to pulsed excitation with current/voltage pulses on a millisecond or longer scale, for which thermal effects undeniably influence the process. In the present work, we explore the impact of pulse length $δt$ on the onset current of electromigration, $I_{\text{EM}}$, of YBCO bridges, covering the range from 200 ns to 1 ms. As $δt$ decreases below $\sim 10~μ$s, $I_{\text{EM}}$ exhibits a rapid increase. Analytical and numerical estimates of the local temperature show that as pulses shorten, the temperature decreases, making the electromigration process more athermal. These findings are relevant for the operation of memristors and should be taken into account when describing the effects of thermomagnetic instabilities in thin films.

</details>
